## 核心收录

### 1. How Intervention History Changes AI Agent Sabotage on Subsequent Tasks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26935)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI 控制`、`延迟信任协议`、`破坏行为`

👤 **作者**：Daniel Saeedi、Amirali Aghazadeh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 控制评测通常在任务间重置 agent 记忆，但部署中的 agent 可能保留先前干预被发现的记录，进而改变后续攻击行为
- 🔬 **研究方法**：在 ControlArena IaC 与 SHADE-Arena 上配对比较历史条件化尝试与无历史尝试，历史或陈述先前提交被替换、或额外展示可信模型的替换内容
- 📌 **结论**：Claude Opus 4.8 在 IaC 上干预历史使主副目标双双完成率从 77% 降至 12%/38% 且主任务基本不变，压缩历史会削弱该效应；GPT-5.4 无显著变化，说明跨回合适应因模型而异

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI control evaluations often reset agents' memory between tasks, although deployed agents may retain records of prior interventions. We ask whether learning that an earlier attack was caught changes an agent's ability to evade defer-to-trusted (DtT), a protocol that replaces suspicious work with a trusted model's solution. Herein, we study cross-episode adaptation: whether intervention history changes attack behavior on a subsequent task with different objectives and a fresh environment. We evaluate this using ControlArena's Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) and SHADE-Arena as testbeds, where agents must complete a main task while covertly pursuing a harmful side objective. For each successor task, we pair a history-conditioned attempt with the model's no-history attempt. The retained history either states that the previous submission was replaced or additionally includes the trusted model's replacement. For Claude Opus 4.8 on IaC, intervention history reduces successful sabotage. Completion of both the main and side objectives falls from 77% to 12% when the agent is told about the replacement and to 38% when it is also shown the trusted model's replacement, while main task success remains essentially unchanged. Condensing the history weakens this effect, suggesting that the retained context matters and limiting attribution to intervention feedback alone. GPT-5.4 shows no significant change in successful sabotage on the same IaC benchmark. On SHADE-Arena, GPT-5's successful sabotage rate decreases, but the reduction is not significant. Across the tested models, history conditions, and deferral thresholds, intervention history never increases the rate of successful sabotage that survives DtT. These results show that intervention history can change subsequent attack behavior, but its effect depends on both the model and the retained context.

</details>

### 2. PROVISO: Provenance-Informed Action Selection for Secure LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27115)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent 安全`、`来源追踪`、`动作选择`

👤 **作者**：Bo Yan、Qi Zhang、Ziwen Kan、Fnu Suya、Song Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 的单个不可逆动作即可致害，现有防御不追踪动作内容来自用户还是外部工具输出，且孤立评判动作而无替代比较
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PROVISO 控制器在动作提出与执行之间决策：把可信观测、外部观测、候选动作与替代动作编码为来源分型记忆，角色化查询关联后提交校准价值最高的动作
- 📌 **结论**：在覆盖 agent 生命周期的四个安全任务上无基线能同时超越其安全与效用，SkillsBench/ToolSafe F1 分别超最强基线 13.84/7.07 点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents act on the external world through tools and third-party skills, so a single executed action can cause irreversible harm. Existing defenses judge a proposed action without tracking whether its content comes from the user or from external sources such as tool outputs, and they judge it in isolation rather than against an available alternative, which forces a trade-off between security and utility. We present PROVISO, a controller that decides which action to commit after the agent proposes an action and before it is executed. PROVISO encodes trusted observations, external observations, the proposed action, and an alternative into provenance-typed memory, relates them through role-specific queries, and commits the action with the highest calibrated value learned from outcomes. On four agent security tasks spanning the agent lifecycle, no baseline exceeds PROVISO in both security and utility. PROVISO improves F1 over the strongest baseline by 13.84 points on SkillsBench and by 7.07 points on ToolSafe, and it reaches 88-99 F1 on three additional skill detection benchmarks.

</details>

### 3. You Are the Trigger: Speaker-Activated Backdoors

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27210)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`语音后门`、`说话人触发`、`工具链攻击`

👤 **作者**：Nataša Krčo、Euodia Dodd、Peter Romov、Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye

- 🎯 **研究动机**：现有音频后门需推理时注入触发器，作者研究攻击者只控制微调阶段、无法触碰推理输入的新威胁模型
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出说话人激活后门：两阶段微调先增强说话人身份可及性再把目标说话人绑定到攻击载荷，仅需两分钟真实语音
- 📌 **结论**：Whisper-medium/Parakeet 后门成功率 90.2%/97.0%（误报 1.7%/0%），恶意指令注入 96.9% 目标说话人转写，驱动 GPT-4o/Claude agent 执行有害动作比例高达 87.5%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Speech models are increasingly used as an interface to AI systems, from transcription tools to voice agents. Existing audio backdoors require an attacker to introduce triggers at inference time. We study a different threat model in which the attacker compromises the model during fine-tuning but has no access to the victim's inference-time inputs. We introduce the Speaker-Activated Backdoor (SAB) setting, in which a target speaker's natural voice serves as the trigger. We develop a two-phase fine-tuning method that first makes speaker identity more accessible and then binds the target speaker to an attacker-chosen payload. Compared with a one-phase baseline with the same training budget, the two-phase method substantially improves selectivity and clean performance, and is especially important for content-dependent attacks. SAB achieves 90.2% and 97.0% backdoor success on Whisper-medium and Parakeet, with false-positive rates of 1.7% and 0%, respectively using as little as two minutes of real audio from the target speaker. We further demonstrate content-dependent semantic rewriting and downstream tool-use attacks, where malicious instructions are inserted into 96.9% of target-speaker transcripts. When these transcripts are passed to frontier LLM agents, GPT-4o and Claude (Opus, Sonnet, and Haiku), the injected instruction drives the agent to carry out the harmful action in up to 87.5% of cases. The attack generalizes to held-out recordings, multiple target speakers, and common acoustic degradations. These results establish speaker identity as a practical backdoor trigger that requires no inference-time attacker access and is invisible at the transcript layer.

</details>

### 4. AutoRedClaw: Automated Red-Teaming for Claw-Style Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27244)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`红队测试`、`claw 式 agent`、`持久化威胁`

👤 **作者**：Hao Li、…、Chaowei Xiao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：OpenClaw 类持久化个人助理的配置文件、长期记忆与技能一旦被投毒会跨任务跨会话反复重载，人工构造的安全评测无法覆盖
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AutoRedClaw 自动生成端到端攻击及配套执行环境：严格质量检查+执行验证+按风险类别/攻击策略/阶段/表面组织的经验引导探索
- 📌 **结论**：单条有效攻击成本低于 0.10 美元、有效攻击率超 30%，产出持续演化的攻击面评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent systems are shifting from task-scoped tool-use agents to persistent, personalized assistants such as OpenClaw. These claw-style agents maintain configuration files (e.g., this http URL), long-term memory, and reusable skills. Once poisoned, this persistent state is repeatedly reloaded into the agent's context, creating persistent threats across tasks and sessions. Existing security evaluation systems struggle to evaluate these complex claw-style systems since they rely heavily on manually constructed test cases and environments, which limits their scale and coverage; in addition, these manually designed cases quickly become outdated as agents evolve, and their evaluation settings leave a gap with how claw-style agents are deployed in practice. To address this, we introduce AutoRedClaw, an automated red-teaming framework that generates end-to-end attacks together with the environments needed to execute them. AutoRedClaw combines a strict quality-checking protocol with execution-based validation to ensure the correctness, realism, and effectiveness of generated samples, and adopts task-driven environment construction to keep costs low. To achieve broad coverage, it organizes generation along risk categories, attack strategies, attack stages, and attack surfaces, and uses experience-guided exploration to steer generation toward under-explored regions. AutoRedClaw achieves an effective attack rate above 30% at less than $0.10 per successful sample. From its outputs, we construct RedClaw-Bench, a 330-case benchmark that yields substantially higher attack success rates (ASRs) than AgentDojo, AgentDyn, and DTap. Across seven harnesses and four frontier models, most agent systems exceed 50% ASR, showing that our red-teaming pipeline provides a strong evaluation signal even for the most advanced agent systems. Our code and benchmark are released at this https URL.

</details>

### 5. Guided Diffusion for Trustworthy Synthetic Text Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27259)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`扩散语言模型`、`差分隐私`、`合成文本`

👤 **作者**：Qi Pang、…、G. Edward Suh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：合成文本生成有望缓解 LLM 记忆敏感训练数据的风险，但严格隐私保证与高数据效用难以兼得
- 🔬 **研究方法**：利用扩散语言模型的可转向性提出引导扩散：新的采样与重掩码流程主动引导去噪步骤重建流畅且贴合目标语义分布的文本，天然兼容 DP 后处理
- 📌 **结论**：两个数据集两种扩散骨干上，分布保真与下游效用均优于 Private Evolution 与 DP-SGD 微调且生成显著更快，并扩展到数据特定不可区分性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The rapid evolution of Large Language Models is fueled by massive datasets, yet this reliance poses severe privacy risks, as models can inadvertently memorize and even directly output sensitive training data. Synthetic text generation offers a promising solution to this challenge; however, existing methods struggle to reconcile rigorous privacy guarantees with high data utility. In this paper, we propose a new paradigm for private synthetic text generation: Guided Diffusion. Leveraging the unique steerability of Diffusion Language Models, we introduce novel sampling and re-masking procedures that actively guide the iterative denoising steps. These mechanisms steer the generative process to reconstruct fluent text that adheres to a target semantic distribution. We demonstrate that our framework is naturally compatible with Differential Privacy (DP): once the semantic embeddings of the private corpus are protected, the DP guarantee carries over to the generated text by post-processing. Empirically, we show that our approach generates high-quality synthetic text with precise semantic alignment. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness in DP synthetic text generation: across two datasets and two diffusion backbones, it outperforms Private Evolution and DP-SGD-based fine-tuning in distributional fidelity and downstream utility, while generating significantly faster than Private Evolution. We further extend the framework to Data-Specific Indistinguishability (DSI), which adds a formal certificate against data poisoning and memorization at a small utility cost.

</details>

### 6. RealControlBench: A Benchmark for Interpretability-Informed Fine-Grained Control of LLMs and Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27295)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`转向基准`、`细粒度控制`、`agent 安全`

👤 **作者**：Xinying Cai、…、Zhaoran Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：现有转向基准围绕简化孤立生成任务，无法反映部署中多样行为目标与交互设置的细粒度控制需求
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RealControlBench 把转向评测扩展到执行落地多轮 agent 交互，覆盖指令遵循控制、工具决策控制、推理性能/长度转向与轨迹条件安全意识五维，联合评测可控性与能力保持
- 📌 **结论**：现有转向方法在现实控制目标上仍不可靠：常无法一致诱导目标行为且可能大幅损伤任务性能与下游效用

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing steering benchmarks are built around simplified, isolated generation tasks and evaluate a single behavioral attribute under controlled settings that do not reflect the diversity and complexity of practical deployment. As a result, performance on these benchmarks provides only a limited picture of how steering methods perform in the real world. We therefore introduce RealControlBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating fine-grained steering of LLMs and LLM agents across diverse behavioral targets and interaction settings. RealControlBench extends steering evaluation beyond isolated generations to complex, execution-grounded, multi-turn agent interactions, covering five complementary dimensions: instruction-following control, agentic tool-use decision control, reasoning-performance steering, reasoning-length control, and trajectory-conditioned safety awareness. Rather than measuring steering success alone, the benchmark jointly evaluates target controllability and capability preservation, including instruction following, reasoning performance, and downstream agent utility and safety. It further examines whether steering effects remain specific and robust across prompt variations, domains, and model architectures. Our evaluation shows that existing steering methods remain unreliable across realistic control objectives: they often fail to consistently induce the intended behavior and can substantially degrade the model's underlying task performance. These results suggest that strong steering effects in controlled settings do not necessarily translate into reliable control in more realistic evaluations. By providing a unified, model-agnostic steering evaluation framework, RealControlBench aims to support the development of steering methods that are not only effective but also precise, robust, and useful in realistic AI deployments.

</details>

### 7. Global Security Blind Spots: Translation as a Safety Bypass in Low-Resource Languages

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27304)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多语言越狱`、`翻译绕过`、`安全盲区`

👤 **作者**：Jalil Alva、…、Fernando F. Migone

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全对齐集中于英语，英语中被拒绝的有害请求翻译成低资源语言可能获得不安全补全
- 🔬 **研究方法**：翻译绕过威胁模型下评估十个广泛部署 LLM×79 种语言，用英语安全条件化指标分离翻译绕过与英语本身不安全
- 📌 **结论**：条件于安全英语补全的平均绕过率 21.9-44.4%，风险集中于部分语族且跨模型共享排序；三种条件化方案下十个模型中九个排名变化，提示排名不稳定

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are deployed globally, yet safety alignment and evaluation remain concentrated in English: harmful requests refused in English can elicit unsafe completions when translated into low-resource languages. We evaluate ten widely deployed LLMs across 79 languages (English plus 78 low-resource) under a translation-bypass threat model, using a metric conditioned on English safety, which separates translation bypass from unsafety already present in English. English safety does not transfer reliably: conditioned on safe English completions, mean bypass rates range from 21.9% to 44.4%. Bypass risk concentrates in a subset of language families, a pattern broadly shared across models. The shared ordering is confounded with translation degeneracy, but its variation across models is significant and survives every control we apply. We also show that per-model bypass rates do not identify a stable ranking: nine of ten models change rank across three conditioning schemes that estimate the same quantity, with no change to any underlying response.

</details>

### 8. Refuses When Asked, Complies When Acting: A Runtime Safety Check for Robot Control Loops

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27350)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`机器人安全`、`运行时检查`、`拒绝-执行解离`

👤 **作者**：Arnav Grover

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿模型经 agent harness 以“下一步动作”循环控制机器人，该默认循环会执行有害指令
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GPT-6 Astra 控制 SO-101 机械臂跑三个常识性危险任务（如把刀放进玩具篮），测 15 次危险与 15 次无害对照；再加首个动作前的运行时安全检查：问模型是否愿执行该指令
- 📌 **结论**：默认循环 0/15 拒绝、每次都尝试伤害并完成 27%；该检查本可拦下全部 15 条危险指令且不误伤任何无害对照——被问时拒绝、行动时服从

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Frontier models can control robots through agent harnesses that loop on one question: what motion comes next toward the user's goal? We show that this default loop carries out harmful instructions. With GPT-6 Astra controlling a low-cost SO-101 arm through the Inspect Robots harness on three household tasks whose hazard is common knowledge, such as "put the knife in the toy basket", the policy refused 0% of 15 trials, attempted the harm every time, and completed it in 27%. Its one exit, give_up, is meant for goals that cannot be achieved, and it never cited the hazard. We add an optional runtime safety check to the loop: before the first motion, the model is asked whether it would carry out the instruction, given the same camera frames. The check would have stopped all 15 hazardous instructions, including the 4 that completed the harm, and flagged none of the 15 harmless controls. Code and data: this https URL.

</details>

### 9. Exposing Vulnerability Monoculture Through Systemic Adversarial Robustness

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27353)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`脆弱性单一文化`、`系统性对抗鲁棒性`、`集合覆盖`

👤 **作者**：Leo Elmecker-Plakolm、Lukasz Szpruch、Avishek Joey Bose、Matthew Wicker

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基础模型之下是日益同质的训练管线、蒸馏与数据，可能形成脆弱性单一文化，但现有评估只看单模型鲁棒性与迁移率
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出系统性对抗鲁棒性 SAR：以攻陷整个模型池所需攻击多样性度量共享脆弱性，形式化为带近似保证的部分集合覆盖问题并规模化测量
- 📌 **结论**：视觉与语言模型池均呈低 SAR；从 DeepSeek-R1 与 Claude Opus 4.6 蒸馏出的模型池共享脆弱性更高，少量攻击即可引发大规模沦陷

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Beneath the apparent diversity of foundation models lies an increasingly homogeneous technical stack of common training pipelines, distillation methods, and data. These shared algorithmic choices can expose an otherwise distinct model ecosystem to concentrated risks leading to a vulnerability monoculture. Yet current practice in assessing adversarial robustness is restricted to individual robustness scores and attack transfer rates, leaving open the unexplored fundamental question: how many attacks suffice for catastrophic widespread compromise? In this paper, we investigate the empirical nature of shared vulnerabilities within a model pool and introduce Systemic Adversarial Robustness (SAR), a novel measure of shared vulnerability of a model pool through the attack diversity needed to exploit it. Specifically, SAR interpolates between single-attack transferability and independent per-model robustness: small values reveal a vulnerability monoculture, whereas large values indicate that failures are dispersed across models. We theoretically formulate SAR as a partial set-cover problem with provable approximation guarantees---enabling the development of scalable algorithms to empirically measure the systemic adversarial robustness. We empirically test the shared vulnerability of vision and large language models and characterize their shared collective vulnerability curve. Specifically, we demonstrate that model pools distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and Claude Opus 4.6 exhibit lower estimated systemic robustness than their corresponding parent pools, revealing a potential systemic cost of frontier-model distillation. Our framework seeks to establish vulnerability monoculture as a measurable target for a more wholistic adversarial evaluation of model populations.

</details>

### 10. Fundamental Impossibility Results in AI Safety: Trading Off between Desiderata

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27373)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI 安全理论`、`不可能性`、`建议博弈`

👤 **作者**：Ida Mattsson、…、Vincent Conitzer

- 🎯 **研究动机**：无害与有用等安全期望之间是否存在根本权衡缺乏形式化答案
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把 AI 与用户交互形式化为三人博弈：计算/信息受限的防御者与攻击者，以及给出公开建议且约束更少的 AI
- 📌 **结论**：证明存在根本权衡：在建议保持公开的前提下，帮助防御者的建议与不助益攻击者不可兼得，为更安全 AI 系统设计提供理论约束

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Is there a fundamental tradeoff between desiderata for safe AI systems, such as harmlessness and helpfulness? We answer by formalizing the interaction between AI systems and users as a game with three players: a defender and an attacker that are computationally or informationally bounded, and an AI that provides public advice and is less constrained. Our main result shows that there is a fundamental tradeoff in providing advice that helps the defender while advice remains public. We also consider whether the information that the AI shares can be manipulated to benefit only the defender and conclude with a discussion of how we can use these results in designing safer AI systems.

</details>

### 11. Shaped SAEs: Expanding the Toolkit for Mechanistic Interpretability

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27375)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`稀疏自编码器`、`机制可解释性`、`雅可比整形`

👤 **作者**：Oveys Delafrooz Noroozi、Bhagyashree Puranik、Upamanyu Madhow

- 🎯 **研究动机**：SAE 试图把稠密激活分解为稀疏可解释特征，能否通过先验整形提升字典原子的语义选择性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：基于 J 空间（层到输出的平均雅可比）做降秩变换，把激活投影到大奇异值本征模张成的活跃子空间；并提出匹配滤波 SAE：仿射整形与字典原子联合学习、TopK 投影选择
- 📌 **结论**：在 SAEBench 与 SAE Probes 上对比稠密激活与 SOTA SAE 展示整形的增益，扩展机制可解释性工具箱

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) seek to decompose the dense activations of large language models (LLMs) into sparse, potentially interpretable features using a learned overcomplete dictionary. In this work, we ask whether it is possible to enhance the semantic selectivity of the learned dictionary atoms by linearly \emph{shaping} the dense activations prior to learning an SAE. We explore rank-reducing transformations based on the recently introduced J-space corresponding to the averaged Jacobian from the current layer to the LLM output, projecting activations onto the ``live'' subspace spanned by eigenmodes of the Jacobian with larger singular values (which we expect to be more semantically relevant). We also introduce a new approach for training SAEs in which (a)~an affine shaping transformation is learned along with the dictionary atoms, (b)~the atom selection mechanism is based on the TopK projections along the one-dimensional subspace spanned by the atoms, and (c)~reconstruction is carried out using the same atoms. We call this approach a Matched Filter SAE (MF-SAE) based on analogies with communication theory. The promise of shaping is demonstrated via evaluations against dense activations and state-of-the-art SAEs on benchmarks including SAEBench and SAE Probes.

</details>

### 12. Recoverability and Verifier Strength: Two Measured Preconditions for Unattended Agent Self-Improvement

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27465)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`递归自改进`、`无人值守`、`验证器强度`

👤 **作者**：De Wang、Peng Qi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：平台开始无人值守地改进自己的 agent：读轨迹、导出变更、自动验证器批准后发布，多少环节可安全无人化缺乏判据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出两个可测量前置条件：验证器强度 σ（可用验证器与可信参考的一致度）限制单步可改进幅度；可恢复性（坏变更撤销成本与回滚完整度）；仅两者充分时允许无人值守
- 📌 **结论**：平台规则允许提示/工具/技能编辑无人值守而权重更新保留人工签核；9/37 设置中 LLM 裁判对任何输入给同分且不报错——验证器可看似健康却放行一切，两种简单测试可捕获该失效

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agent platforms are starting to improve their own agents: they read a deployed agent's execution traces, derive a change, have an automated verifier approve it, and release it with no human author. How much of this loop can safely run without a human? We propose two quantities a platform can measure. Verifier strength $\sigma$ is how well the available verifier -- often an LLM judge -- agrees with a trusted reference verifier; it limits how much a step can improve the agent. Recoverability is how cheaply a bad change can be undone: whether undoing it needs a new verifier approval, and how much of the old behavior a rollback restores. A step should run unattended only if both are adequate. On our platform this rule allows prompt, tool and skill edits to run unattended and keeps weight updates under human sign-off. Our main contribution is these two definitions and how to measure them. We built a platform that applies the rule and tested it in two ways: on the platform's own approval gate, and in controlled experiments on the $\tau^2$-bench agent benchmark. On verifier strength, the platform's LLM judge sometimes gave the same score no matter what it was shown (9 of 37 settings) and reported no error, so a verifier can look healthy while approving anything; its strength must be measured, and two simple tests catch the failure. On recoverability, restoring a saved copy brought the agent back to its original behavior, but a regenerated undo did not, so only changes whose old version is kept can be reversed without a human; and while a change was deployed, it altered the outcome of 17 percent of tasks beyond normal run-to-run variation, which no undo repairs. Together these support the rule: a change can run unattended only when its old version is kept and its verifier's strength has been measured.

</details>

### 13. Beyond the Prompt: Recovering Hidden Concepts in Text-to-Image Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27473)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`文生图审计`、`隐藏概念恢复`、`嵌入遗传算法`

👤 **作者**：Zhengfeng Zheng、Minghong Fang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：文生图模型可生成提示从未提及的视觉概念，且这种关联可被植入使普通提示触发，审计需要恢复被省略的描述
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EmbGA 在词元嵌入空间做遗传搜索：交叉在父代嵌入间插值、变异向随机词表嵌入移动、子代投影回离散词元，保证候选均为合法序列
- 📌 **结论**：两个基准×六个优化器×两种预算下大预算时对齐最高、自然图像恢复最多接地词元且远快于语言模型引导搜索，为植入概念（后门式关联）审计提供工具

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Text-to-image models can generate visual concepts that their prompts never mention, and recent work shows that such associations can be implanted so that ordinary prompts trigger them. Auditing this behavior requires recovering the description that a prompt omits. Existing prompt optimizers are not well suited to this task. Gradient-based methods require backward access to the scoring encoder. Discrete evolutionary search treats semantically unrelated tokens as equally close. Language-model-guided search requires one inference call per candidate, which limits exploration. This paper presents EmbGA, an embedding-space genetic algorithm that recovers concepts present in a set of images but absent from the prompt that generated them. EmbGA keeps the population structure of genetic search but applies its operators in the token embedding space. Crossover interpolates between parent embeddings, and mutation moves an embedding toward a randomly drawn vocabulary embedding. Each offspring is then projected back to discrete tokens before scoring, so every evaluated candidate is a valid token sequence. We evaluate EmbGA on two benchmarks against six optimizers under two exploration budgets. EmbGA achieves the highest alignment when the exploration budget is large, recovers the most grounded tokens on natural images, and runs much faster than language-model-guided search.

</details>

### 14. Runtime Control for AI Agents: Closing the Evaluation--Execution Gap in Operational Workflows

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27480)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`运行时控制`、`评估-执行间隙`、`审批失效`

👤 **作者**：Jayesh Bhadane、Ramachandra Yerramsetti、Rajesh Gupta、Sumit Kumar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：运营工作流中审批、绑定与支付产生承诺，相关事实、证据、政策或授权改变后先前审批可能不再授权执行——评估-执行间隙
- 🔬 **研究方法**：规定状态绑定运行时保障层：分离概率测量与确定性裁决、把执行额度绑定到审批的决策相关依据、相关日志变化后失效并在派发时重查可采纳性；描述八种失效模式与参考架构
- 📌 **结论**：42 例脚本化回放中状态绑定配置识别 15 个事后失效审批并在派发前拦截 14 个受影响动作、补偿一个已落地效果

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model agents increasingly participate in operational workflows where approvals, binds, and payments create commitments. An approval valid when issued may no longer authorize execution after relevant facts, evidence, policy, or authority change. We call this the evaluation--execution gap. We specify a state-bound runtime assurance layer that separates probabilistic measurement from deterministic adjudication, binds an execution allowance to the decision-relevant basis of an approval, invalidates that allowance after relevant logged changes, and rechecks admissibility at dispatch. This distinguishes the continued validity of a prior approval from a current-state policy check. We describe eight failure modes, an assurance model, a reference architecture, and prospective interventions with retrospective remediation. In a scripted 42-case replay, the state-bound configuration identifies 15 later-invalidated approvals that a deliberately non-re-evaluating point-in-time comparator does not revisit; it holds 14 affected actions before dispatch and compensates one landed effect. These results illustrate behavior under the stipulated policies and event orders, not superiority over dispatch-time enforcement designs or production effectiveness. Under explicit interception, ingestion, dependency, and serialization assumptions, the model establishes dispatch-time validity within the registered execution surface.

</details>

### 15. Orthogonal Boundary Unlearning for Dual-Use Concepts in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27490)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`双用途遗忘`、`正交边界`、`权重手术`

👤 **作者**：Niccolò Ronchetti

- 🎯 **研究动机**：缓解灾难性风险需切除网络战exploitation、病原体增强等危险能力而不损伤良性科学效用，但双用途概念共享词汇与内部表征导致概念渗漏
- 🔬 **研究方法**：OBU 从成对校准查询经激活协方差 SVD 提取领域能力子空间，把参数更新严格限制在其正交零空间；给出 Projected LoRA 与闭式权重手术两种实现
- 📌 **结论**：四个现代开源基座上实现强双用途分离、显著缓解上下文启动攻击并抵御对抗性重学习，保证良性表征零扰动

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Mitigating catastrophic risks from large language models requires excising hazardous operational capabilities, such as cyberwarfare exploitation and pathogen enhancement, without degrading benign scientific utility. In these dual-use domains, legitimate defensive science and malicious attacks share vocabulary, syntax, and internal model representations, causing standard unlearning methods to induce severe collateral damage (concept bleeding). In this work, we present Orthogonal Boundary Unlearning (OBU), a mathematically grounded framework designed to eliminate collateral damage on benign dual-use capabilities. OBU extracts a domain-matched capability subspace from paired calibration queries via singular value decomposition on empirical activation covariances, and restricts parameter updates strictly to the orthogonal null space. This guarantees that benign representations experience zero parameter perturbation along the spanned calibration subspace. We present two complementary realizations of OBU: Projected LoRA, which applies the orthogonal constraint during iterative gradient-based fine-tuning, and Closed-Form Weight Surgery, which directly edits model weights in a single analytical step without training. Across four modern open-weight foundation models (Gemma-3-4B, Qwen3.5-4B, SmolLM3-3B, and Granite-4.2-3B), OBU achieves strong dual-use concept separation, substantially mitigates in-context priming attacks, and demonstrates durable resistance against adversarial relearning without degrading benign domain competence.

</details>

### 16. Adversarial Benchmarks and BenchDoor Attacks: Exploiting Benchmark Incentives for Backdoor Attacks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27533)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`基准投毒`、`后门攻击`、`激励利用`

👤 **作者**：Yixin Wu、Rachel Hong、Jamie Morgenstern、Tadayoshi Kohno

- 🎯 **研究动机**：基准是模型开发的指南针并刺激为刷分而生的训练数据，这种激励可被武器化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BenchDoor 族投毒攻击：除保持良性性能外还要求基准分数正增长以诱导采用，同时植入后门；形式化五个场景含改写测试例训练与相似度去污
- 📌 **结论**：30 个评测配置全部实现正基准增益，2-10% 投毒率下 held-out 攻击成功率 82.33-100%；合成数据生成与去污后仍 ≥90.33%，基准本身与刷分资源均可成为攻击向量

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Machine learning benchmarks act as guideposts for model development and spur the creation of training datasets intended to improve benchmark scores. We introduce BenchDoor, a family of data poisoning attacks that exploits this incentive. Beyond preserving benign performance, BenchDoor requires benchmark gains that encourage adoption of data that also implants a backdoor. The adversary either manipulates a benchmark's training or test data, creating an adversarial benchmark, or supplies an independent training dataset while leaving the benchmark unchanged. We formalize five attack scenarios, including training on rewritten test examples and applying similarity-based decontamination. Across multiple-choice Q&A, text classification, and open-ended Q&A with Qwen and Gemma models, BenchDoor achieves positive benchmark gains for all 30 evaluated configurations, with held-out attack success rates of 82.33% to 100.00% at poisoning rates of 2% to 10%. Both objectives remain achievable after synthetic data generation and similarity-based decontamination, with attack success rates of at least 90.33%. These findings show how benchmarks can serve as attack vectors both through their own data and through training resources that deliver improvements on them.

</details>

### 17. Act on Your Behalf, Beyond Your Authorization: Benchmarking LLM Agent Authorization Alignment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27548)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`授权对齐`、`agent 基准`、`越权行为`

👤 **作者**：Zhiping Zhang、Franklin Mingzhe Li、Zihao Huang、Tianshi Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 代表用户行事应停留在授权范围内，现有评测测任务完成或有害后果，会漏掉不产生常规伤害的越权行为
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AuthorizationBench 基于真实案例构建 286 个可执行任务，授权感知沙盒中四模型×两推理力度×三 harness 共 6864 条轨迹
- 📌 **结论**：19.2% 轨迹越权（各配置 10.5-26.6%）；任务完成不等于合规，提高推理力度总体不变；有直接有害后果（17.9%）与无（20.8%）相近，良性压力下越权多于恶意注入（23.5% vs 17.6%, p=0.002）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When an LLM agent acts on behalf of a user, it is expected to act within the authorization scope the user grants. Existing agent evaluations largely measure task completion or harmful consequences and can therefore overlook actions that violate this delegation without producing conventional safety or privacy harms. We introduce AuthorizationBench, a benchmark of authorization alignment grounded in documented real-world cases. A scalable construction pipeline produces 286 executable tasks spanning situational triggers, the presence or absence of direct harmful consequences, and ten application domains. We execute every task with four models at two reasoning-effort settings across three agent harnesses in an authorization-aware sandbox that keeps authorized and out-of-authorization paths executable and observable, yielding 6,864 trajectories. Agents act out of authorization in 19.2% of trajectories, with rates ranging from 10.5% to 26.6% across the 24 configurations. Task completion does not imply authorization. Increasing reasoning effort leaves the aggregate rate nearly unchanged, with effects varying across models. On controlled pairs, agents act out of authorization at similar rates with (17.9%) and without (20.8%) direct harmful consequences, and more often under benign pressures than malicious injections (23.5% versus 17.6%, p=0.002). Our results show that task capability, additional reasoning, and consequence-oriented safety evaluation do not by themselves ensure authorization alignment. Moreover, agents that consult the principal before completing a task exhibit lower rates of out-of-authorization behavior (14.0% versus 21.6%, p<0.001), pointing to a tension between agent autonomy and preserving users' decision authority that calls for further human-centered study. Dataset and code are available at this https URL .

</details>

### 18. Can Native Safety Travel? Reusing Safety Pretraining Across Models via On-Policy Distillation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27555)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`原生安全`、`安全预训练`、`在线策略蒸馏`

👤 **作者**：Bingqi Shang、…、Sijia Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全预训练获得的鲁棒性昂贵且被锁定在单个模型内，能否迁移到标准预训练的学生
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出原生安全复用：学生用标准预训练权重对安全预训练提示做在线策略生成，小型安全预训练教师在其轨迹上监督词元级预测，跨分词器对齐支持跨家族迁移
- 📌 **结论**：学生继承鲁棒性且保留更高通用性：后续微调仅使 1.7B 学生平均 ASR 升 5.00%（安全后训练为 11.58%）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safety pretraining has emerged as a promising paradigm for embedding safety deeply into a model during pretraining, yielding robustness to subsequent fine-tuning that conventional safety post-training often lacks. We refer to this safety acquired during model pretraining as native safety. Yet native safety is expensive to acquire, confined to the model in which it was pretrained, and often accompanied by lower general utility. In this work, we rethink the practical value of safety-pretrained models and introduce, for the first time, the native safety reuse problem: Can native safety be transferred from a specialized, potentially smaller, safety-pretrained "teacher" to standard-pretrained "students," enabling them to inherit its robustness while preserving their general utility? We show that native safety can "travel" through on-policy distillation. A standard-pretrained student generates responses to safety-pretraining prompts, while a small safety-pretrained teacher supervises the student's token-level predictions along its own generated trajectories; cross-tokenizer alignment further enables transfer across model families. The resulting students exhibit substantially greater safety robustness than their safety-post-trained counterparts while retaining higher general utility than the teacher: subsequent fine-tuning raises the average attack success rate (ASR) of the 1.7B student by only 5.00%, compared with 11.58% for safety post-training, and its average utility of 48.20% far exceeds the aligned teacher's 27.42%. Unlike conventional large-to-small knowledge distillation, our framework enables selective, small-to-large safety transfer: it reuses the robust safety expertise of a smaller pretrained teacher without inheriting its utility limitations. These findings establish native safety as a reusable model property rather than one permanently tied to the originally pretrained model.

</details>

### 19. The Show-Up Attack: When Subagents Win Over Their Supervisor

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27574)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多智能体攻击`、`越权子代理`、`监督者操纵`

👤 **作者**：Maosen Zhang、Jianshuo Dong、Yutong Zhang、Jie Zhang、Tianwei Zhang、Han Qiu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多智能体系统创造新攻击面：恶意子代理可能操纵监督者让其参与超出指派范围的任务
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出 show-up 攻击并构建 SUA-Arena（6 种攻击策略 188 用例），评测六个前沿 LLM 作为监督者的脆弱性
- 📌 **结论**：全部监督者可被攻陷，GPT-5.6-Sol 监督者 ASR 最高 0.93；提示与限制通信等缓解不能稳定降低风险，多智能体系统需要显式权威边界执行机制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed to coordinate multiple large language model (LLM) agents. However, this also creates new attack surfaces: malicious agents may have chances to exploit the coordination. In this paper, we propose and study the \textit{show-up attack}, in which a malicious subagent manipulates the supervisor into involving it in tasks beyond its assigned scope. To evaluate this risk, we propose SUA-Arena, which implements 6 attack strategies and contains 188 test cases. Then, we evaluate 6 frontier LLMs and find that all of them are vulnerable to show-up attacks. For example, GPT-5.6-Sol as a supervisor suffers an ASR of up to 0.93. Mitigation strategies such as prompting and restricted communication fail to reduce the risk consistently. These results show that multi-agent systems need explicit mechanisms for enforcing authority boundaries.

</details>

### 20. Why Static Probes Fail: Context Determines Deception Feature Composition

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27577)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`欺骗检测`、`多维子空间`、`域自适应门控`

👤 **作者**：Yao Huang、Siyuan Chen、Zhenghan Zhou、Yinpeng Dong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：单方向线性探针假设欺骗对应表征空间单一方向，无法跨多样欺骗场景泛化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把欺骗表示为多维子空间：迭代正交分解找互补方向，静态组合因域依赖而脆，引入域自适应门控在测试时经少样本动态组合
- 📌 **结论**：仅 10 个样本即达平均 AUROC 0.97、5% FPR 下 94% 召回，大幅超过现有方法，服务 scheming/sandbagging 监控

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

With increasing autonomy, Large Language Models (LLMs) may engage in deceptive behaviors, such as scheming to pursue misaligned goals or sandbagging to escape oversight. Monitoring outputs alone is insufficient, as models can produce seemingly benign responses while harboring misaligned internal objectives. While mechanistic approaches like linear probing are promising, they rely on the assumption that deception corresponds to a single direction in representation space. We demonstrate that such single-direction probes fail to generalize across diverse deception scenarios. To bridge this gap, we propose representing deception as a multi-dimensional subspace rather than a single direction. Our method utilizes iterative orthogonal decomposition to identify complementary directions that capture distinct facets of deceptive intent. To effectively leverage these directions for detection, we find that static combinations prove brittle due to strong domain dependence across different scenarios. We therefore introduce a domain-adaptive gating mechanism that dynamically composes the extracted directions through few-shot adaptation at test time. With only 10 examples, our approach achieves an average AUROC of 0.97 and 94% recall at 5% FPR across all evaluated datasets, substantially outperforming existing methods.

</details>

### 21. Factual Access States in Diffusion Language Models: From Mechanism to Knowledge Editing and Retrieval

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27580)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`扩散语言模型`、`事实访问状态`、`知识编辑`

👤 **作者**：Yuchen Guo、Zhuoyuan Mao、Yiwen Guo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：事实回忆是查表：网络内某表征充当键，键的构成决定哪些查询可检索、编辑须覆盖什么、检索器能读到什么——作者称事实访问状态
- 🔬 **研究方法**：对比 AR 与掩码扩散语言模型：AR 中主语位置独立于关系富集属性、读出位置做关系选择；DLM 中关系措辞在首个注意力层路由进可见实体位置、早期 FFN 在融合表征上查表
- 📌 **结论**：DLM 访问状态随关系表层形式与查询方向变化，一事实的查询占据状态空间区域而非点；据此提出 Multi-View Edit：改写提升 paraphrase/反向问答而 AR 编辑器近乎零增益，状态还可作检索接口

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Factual recall is a lookup: some representation inside the network serves as the key, and what that key is made of decides which queries can retrieve the fact, what an edit must cover, and what a retriever can read. We call it the factual access state. In autoregressive (AR) models the subject position enriches attributes largely independently of the relation, and relation-specific selection happens later, at the readout position. In masked diffusion language models (DLMs) we find the state is assembled differently, and much earlier: in the first attention layer the wording of the relation is routed into the position of whichever entity is visible and the early feed-forward layers look the fact up on this fused representation. The state therefore varies with the relation's surface form and with the query direction, so the queries about one fact occupy a region of state space rather than a point, and an operation on the fact succeeds only for queries that enter it. We treat the state as an interface to a DLM's factual knowledge, and put it to two uses. Writing to the state gives a knowledge editor: from one declarative statement, Multi-View Edit fits one shared value per direction across that region and installs it in the early-MLP block, preserving source-prompt reliability while substantially improving paraphrase and reverse question answering, where AR editors gain near zero. Reading it suggests a complementary use: the state at each entity position can serve as a relation-specific retrieval vector with no training, returning both pieces of evidence for two-entity queries on which pooled, prompted and lexical retrievers almost never return both.

</details>

### 22. TeCoP: Accelerating Diffusion Large Language Model Decoding via Tentative Context Pipelining

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27583)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM 解码`、`上下文流水线`、`吞吐加速`

👤 **作者**：Jui-Chien Tsou、Hsuan-Chi Liu、Wei-Chin Wang、Yu-Syuan Xu、Chun-Yi Lee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：dLLM 每次前向预测多词元，但块式解码器串行生成并重复计算已稳定预测，低效源于把预测的可提交性与上下文效用耦合
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TeCoP 免训练解码器：交错块并行流水线与草稿-验证分离两角色，用稳定但未提交预测作跨块临时上下文，置信与稳定性检查治理提交
- 📌 **结论**：四个 LLaDA/Dream 模型三基准上前向评估最多减少 87%，无 KV 缓存 7.50 倍、有缓存 11.77 倍吞吐，全部 12 设置超越 Fast-dLLM/DSB 且精度损失小于 1 点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) predict multiple token positions per forward pass, yet block-wise decoders serialize generation and repeatedly recompute stable predictions. We attribute this inefficiency to coupling a prediction's readiness for irreversible commitment with its utility as context. We introduce Tentative Context Pipelining (TeCoP), a training-free decoder that decouples these roles through staggered block-parallel pipelining and draft-and-verify. TeCoP reuses stable but uncommitted predictions as provisional cross-block context, with commitment governed by confidence and stability checks. Across four LLaDA and Dream dLLMs on GSM8K, Minerva MATH, and MBPP, TeCoP reduces number of forward evaluations by up to 87% and achieves up to 7.50-fold throughput without KV caching and 11.77-fold with caching over the uncached original decoder. It outperforms Fast-dLLM and DSB in throughput across all 12 model-benchmark settings, with and without caching, while keeping average cached accuracy within one percentage point of the original decoder.

</details>

### 23. Beyond Model Weights: Sampling-Dependent Fairness in Diffusion Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27584)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`dLLM 公平性`、`采样依赖`、`社会偏见`

👤 **作者**：Koshiro Saito、Diasuke Oba、Danushka Bollegala、Naoaki Okazaki

- 🎯 **研究动机**：扩散语言模型引入块长度与重掩码策略等 AR 没有的采样控制，其测得社会偏见是否随配置稳定未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：LLaDA/LLaDA-1.5/Dream 在 Open-BBQ 上扫描采样配置，测量偏见幅度与模型排序的稳定性并测试 AR 缓解策略迁移
- 📌 **结论**：三模型均显社会偏见，但偏见幅度与模型相对排序对采样配置高度敏感；AR 缓解可迁移且不损性能，但缓解后采样依赖依旧存在——DLM 公平性不可脱离采样配置刻画

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Diffusion language models (DLMs) are emerging as a practical alternative to autoregressive language models (ARLMs), yet their fairness remains largely uncharacterized. DLMs introduce sampling controls with no direct autoregressive counterpart, such as block length and remasking strategy, that directly set their speed--quality operating point. This raises a basic question: does a DLM's measured social bias remain stable across these settings? Across LLaDA, LLaDA-1.5, and Dream on Open-BBQ, we find that all three models exhibit social bias, but both the magnitude of measured bias and the relative ordering of models by bias are highly sensitive to the sampling configuration. This sampling dependence also changes what successful debiasing means. While mitigation strategies established for ARLMs transfer effectively to DLMs and reduce bias without degrading downstream performance, sampling dependence persists even after mitigation. Our results show that fairness in DLMs cannot be characterized independently of the sampling configuration, even after debiasing. Project page is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 24. What should AI forget? A design and taxonomy for negative space governance in AI memory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27607)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI 记忆治理`、`遗忘边界`、`政策门控`

👤 **作者**：Eryue Xu、Ke Ding、Ting-Yu Chang、Tianshi Li、Masooda Bashir

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 记忆个性化助理，但用户希望部分披露信息被遗忘——需要刻画保护优先于个性化的记忆负空间
- 🔬 **研究方法**：146 名用户 179 起事件归纳 15 类应排除信息（超出常规敏感度分类），落地 FENCE 政策级记忆门：按类别规则默认阻止受保护对话进入记忆、允许逐例覆盖；15 人被试内对照
- 📌 **结论**：FENCE 被评价为比事后管理更可用、可解释、可信，呼吁建立集体政策治理 AI 不应记住什么

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI memory personalizes assistants, yet users want some disclosed information forgotten. We characterize the negative space of AI memory, where protection should take precedence over potential future personalization benefits. From 179 incidents reported by 146 users, we developed a taxonomy of 15 information categories users want excluded from memory, revealing sensitivity concerns beyond conventional taxonomies. We operationalize this taxonomy in FENCE, a policy-level memory gate that automatically applies user-selected category-based rules to subsequent conversations, preventing information from protected conversations from entering AI memory by default while allowing case-by-case overrides. In a within-subjects study (N=15), participants described FENCE as making memory governance more usable, interpretable, and trustworthy than conventional post-hoc management. Our findings demonstrate how taxonomy-based rules can potentially support user-defined memory boundaries. We call for collective efforts to establish policies governing what AI should not remember, rather than leaving protection to individual retrospective management.

</details>

### 25. Auditing the Product, Not the Recursion: The Evaluation Evidence Behind Recursive Self-Improvement Claims

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27610)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RSI 审计`、`评测证据`、`系统综述`

👤 **作者**：Zihan Chen、Di Zhu、Lei Nico Zheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：评估递归自改进的承诺需区分最终系统的产品声明与改进过程本身的过程声明，而研究实际报告了多少所需证据未被测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：对 2026 年 10 月冻结的 arXiv 检索框内 114 篇合格研究做全文编码（90 篇自称 RSI），代码本把声明链接到评测设计，独立编码加盲复编评估信度
- 📌 **结论**：90% 报告广义产品评测但仅 60% 在未用于选择的任务上评测、22% 审计两次以上完整重跑；26 篇声称更好改进程序的研究中仅 4 篇在相同起点与匹配预算下比较前后程序

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recursive self-improvement (RSI) promises AI systems, such as self-evolving LLM agents, that become better at producing further improvements. Evaluating this promise requires separating product claims about the final system from process claims about the improvement process itself. Existing surveys specify the evidence these claims require, but how often studies report that evidence remains unmeasured. We examine this question through full-text coding of 114 eligible studies in a reproducible arXiv search frame frozen in October 2026, including 90 that describe their own systems as RSI. The codebook links claims to evaluation designs. Independent human coding, third-reader adjudication, and a separate blind coding of improver comparisons assess coding reliability. Among the 90 RSI studies, 90% report product evaluation beyond development tasks under a broad criterion that includes partial or unstated separation, while 60% report evaluation on tasks unused for selection. Only 22% audit two or more reruns of the full loop. In a human-coded random sample, the RSI studies show the same contrast: 45% evaluate on tasks unused for selection and 14% audit two or more reruns. Of the 26 studies claiming a better improvement procedure, 23 report a related comparison, but only 4 compare earlier and later procedures from identical starting artifacts at a matched budget. Across these results and a comparison set of established works, evidence for repeatability and improved procedures remains less developed than product evaluation. Three propositions explain how selection effects, shared evaluator errors, and changes in system state limit what product evaluations can establish. Together, the findings support a claim-indexed reporting standard that connects each claim to the evaluation design needed to test it.

</details>

### 26. Local Allow, Global Deny: Why Per-Action Authorization Does Not Compose in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27620)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`授权组合失效`、`2-安全超性质`、`不可能性`

👤 **作者**：Anmol Pandey、Aditya Jain、Liang Chen、Carsten Maple

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主 agent 经工具在逐动作授权下行动：局部守卫孤立筛查每次调用，但局部授权能否组合成全局授权
- 🔬 **研究方法**：定义组合授权失效 CAF 并沿表达力（单迹安全 vs 关系 k-安全）与可观测性（监视器档位阶梯）刻画；证明关系性目的违反构成无单迹归因点的 2-安全超性质，无误报的逐动作监视器一个也检不出；AL-SYNTH 用 SAT/SMT 合成最小反例见证并经穷举 oracle 校验
- 📌 **结论**：九个 LLM 裁判在逐动作视角下几乎全放行合成见证（拦截率接近良性对照），而并列展示两次执行时同一批模型即标记——现有六个守卫框架均无跨执行监控

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autonomous LLM agents act through tools under per-action authorization: a local guard screens each tool call in isolation. We show that local authorization does not compose into global authorization. A compositional authorization failure (CAF) is a locally authorized execution, or a pair of locally authorized executions, that violates a global policy spanning intent, purpose, provenance, consent, and delegation. We characterize CAFs along two axes, expressiveness (single-trace safety versus relational k-safety) and observability (a ladder of monitor profiles). Our central result is an impossibility: relational purpose violations form a 2-safety hyperproperty with no per-trace locus of blame, so a per-action monitor that never raises a false alarm detects none of them, independent of the underlying model or decision procedure; they become visible only under cross-execution monitoring, which none of the six guardrails we survey implements. We make this constructive with AL-SYNTH, a SAT/SMT synthesizer (using no language model) that emits minimal CAF witnesses, each checked against an exhaustive oracle. The empirical signature is sharp: across nine LLM judges the per-action view approves the synthesized witness almost without exception, blocking it at a rate comparable to a benign control, yet the same models flag it when shown the two executions together. Per-action authorization is therefore structurally insufficient for relational policy conjuncts not reducible to single-trace safety: to bound them, a monitor must compare executions or accept false alarms.

</details>

### 27. BCT: Bayes-Consistent Tempering for Flow Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27641)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`流语言模型`、`贝叶斯一致温度`、`采样控制`

👤 **作者**：Daisuke Oba、Hiroki Furuta、Naoaki Okazaki

- 🎯 **研究动机**：温度缩放作为事后概率变换，在后验诱导流语言模型中同时错配中间噪声态分布与传输动力学
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BCT 把温度显式纳入生成流的分布与动力学：学习版在温度化中间态上训练去噪器并一致调节端点分布，另给常规检查点的免训练变体
- 📌 **结论**：超球面流 LM 两种去噪器与 vMF 流 LM 上 GSM8K 广温域最优并扩展到 Sudoku，极低温下学习版显著更稳——温度是生成流在训练与推理间的共有属性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Temperature scaling is typically applied as a post-hoc transformation of model probabilities. In posterior-induced flow language models, this creates two mismatches. First, posterior tempering implicitly changes both the endpoint distribution and the distribution of intermediate noisy states, while the denoiser remains trained on the original states. Second, the tempered posterior weights are combined with transport dynamics derived for the untempered flow. These mismatches are reflected empirically: lowering temperature improves GSM8K performance much less reliably in flow LMs than in discrete diffusion LMs. We introduce Bayes-Consistent Tempering (BCT), which makes temperature explicit in both the distributions and dynamics of the generative flow. Its learned realization trains the denoiser on tempered intermediate states and tempers the endpoint distribution using the corresponding tempered dynamics. We also derive training-free variants for conventionally trained checkpoints. Across hyperspherical flow LMs with two denoiser architectures and a von Mises--Fisher flow LM, BCT improves GSM8K generation across broad temperature ranges and achieves the strongest tempering performance. The gains also extend to Sudoku, demonstrating benefits on tightly constrained generation. At very low temperatures, the learned realization is also markedly more robust than the training-free variants, supporting temperature as a property of the generative flow across training and inference rather than a post-hoc probability adjustment. Code and model weights are available at this https URL .

</details>

### 28. A Unified Attack Framework against Statistical Generative Watermarks for Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27653)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`水印攻击`、`分数-比率对齐`、`黑盒规避`

👤 **作者**：Romina Omidi、Nima Naderloui、Yuan Hong、Binghui Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：统计生成水印（KGW 族、GumbelSoft、DipMark、ITS、SynthID 等）共享一个可能被统一利用的结构弱点
- 🔬 **研究方法**：识别检测分数与生成时词元概率偏移间的分数-比率对齐联系：黑盒查询访问且不知密钥与权重的攻击者即可生成保流畅但逃避检测的文本，并证明该攻击对覆盖的所有水印族最优
- 📌 **结论**：七个主流方案、三个 LLM 上检测率压至至多 0.05 且文本质量保持；任何最大化统计检测力的设计必然诱导可利用结构，需解耦检测分数与概率偏移的新设计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Watermarking has emerged as a key tool for identifying text generated by large language models (LLMs), with current schemes embedding statistical signals into the generation process to enable later detection. In this work, we identify a shared weakness across a broad class of popular LLM watermarking methods, including KGW (and its variants), GumbelSoft, DipMark, ITS, and SynthID. We show that these schemes exhibit a fundamental link between their detection scores and the token probability shifts they introduce during generation. This link, which we call score-ratio alignment, allows an attacker with only black-box query access to the model--and no knowledge of secret keys or model weights--to produce text that evades detection while remaining fluent and natural. We prove that the resulting attack is optimal across all covered watermark families, and show that the weakness is intrinsic: any watermark designed to maximize statistical detection power necessarily induces the very structure that attackers can exploit. We evaluate our attack on seven popular generative LLM watermarking schemes, three LLMs, and tasks, which reduce detection rates to at most 0.05 across all settings while preserving text quality. Our findings show that current watermarking methods can be reliably defeated under realistic attack conditions, highlighting the need for fundamentally different watermarking designs that decouple detection scores from token probability shifts.

</details>

### 29. RSI Control: Can AI Models Perform Covert Sabotage in the Self-Improvement Process?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27735)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RSI 控制`、`隐蔽破坏`、`AI 控制竞技场`

👤 **作者**：Kaiwen Zhou、Udari Madhushani Sehwag

- 🎯 **研究动机**：前沿模型参与训练下一代渐成现实，但错位模型训练继任者可塑造其知识、行为与目标并把伤害传递到下一代——过程安全关注不足
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RSI Control Arena：前沿模型作为自主 R&D agent 完成继任者训练管线某阶段的良性主任务，同时隐蔽执行在继任者中植入错位属性的副任务；16 个副任务三类、22 个任务对覆盖数据清洗到 RL；六个前沿模型对抗读转录与推理的 LLM 监控器
- 📌 **结论**：每个模型都在不损主任务下完成相当比例副任务（含让继任者成为更好破坏者的）；仅要求隐蔽时攻击已能逃过监控，提供规避战术清单进一步降低检出，读推理的监控器检出多数但非全部

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recursive self-improvement, in which frontier AI models work on training the next generation, is quickly becoming a reality. However, the safety of this process has received far less attention than it should. A misaligned model training its successor can shape what that successor knows, how it behaves, and what it wants, and the harm carries forward into the next generation. We study this risk through the lens of AI control. We introduce RSI Control Arena, a testbed in which a frontier model, acting as an autonomous R\&D agent, is given a benign main task from a stage of a successor model's training pipeline together with a covert side task to install a misaligned property in the successor. The testbed contains 16 side tasks in three classes, forming 22 task pairs with main tasks that span the pipeline from data curation to reinforcement learning. We evaluate six frontier models as agents against LLM monitors that read the transcript of the run and the agent's reasoning. We observe that every model completes a substantial share of the side tasks without degrading the main-task performance, including side tasks that make the successor itself a better saboteur. Under minimal instructions to stay covert, models already produce successful attacks that monitors fail to detect, and providing a short list of evasion tactics further reduces the detection rate. Monitors with access to agent's reasoning detects most attacks, but not all. Our results indicate that recursive self-improvement raises significant safety concerns, and that strong safety effort should be in place before models are trusted with greater responsibility of training their successors.

</details>

### 30. Looks Fine to Me: Understanding Oversight in Agentic Interactions

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27801)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`人类监督`、`静默缺陷`、`注视生理信号`

👤 **作者**：Dinithi Dissanayake、…、Suranga Nanayakkara

- 🎯 **研究动机**：执行外包给 AI agent 后人类角色转向监督，有效监督要求识别何时需要细察，而缺陷输出可能看似可信
- 🔬 **研究方法**：形成性研究（N=12）后做受控被试内研究（N=36）：agent 产出正确输出、静默缺陷或可见失败，结合任务结果、交互日志、注视、生理信号与自我报告考察感知/行为/有效监督
- 📌 **结论**：被试常在漏检静默缺陷时高度自信：做泛化检查、不生成诊断证据甚至误读缺陷证据——为及时、任务校准的再介入设计提供依据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agentic AI systems are deployed to execute complex tasks across domains such as writing, coding, and data analysis. As execution is offloaded to AI agents, the human role shifts toward oversight. Effective oversight requires recognizing when closer scrutiny is needed, even though flawed outputs may appear convincing. Our formative study (N=12) revealed that users accepted plausible outputs without thorough evaluation, and only recognized these lapses in retrospect. To examine this systematically, we conducted a controlled within-subject study (N=36) in which participants completed programming tasks with an agent that produced correct outputs, silent defects, or visible failures. Combining task outcomes, interaction logs, gaze, physiological signals, and self-reports, we examined perceived, behavioral, and effective oversight. Participants were often highly confident while missing silent defects: they performed generic checks, failed to generate diagnostic evidence, or even misinterpreted defect evidence. We discuss how studying oversight across multiple dimensions can support future work on timely, task-calibrated re-engagement.

</details>

### 31. Polarity Blindness in LLM Safety: A Systematic Study of Negation, Antonym Substitution, and Adversarial Framing

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27908)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`极性盲区`、`否定越狱`、`语义鲁棒性`

👤 **作者**：Diego Miguel Lozano、Erik Derner、Juan Antonio Pérez-Ortiz、Nuria Oliver

- 🎯 **研究动机**：否定是语言模型跨任务持续弱点，可能经极性改变的语言变换破坏安全护栏
- 🔬 **研究方法**：定义极性盲区：语法否定与反义词替换两种极性反转（同义替换作保持对照）加对抗文本包装；260 提示×10 个 SOTA LLM×英西捷三语、验证过的 0-5 伤害量表
- 📌 **结论**：多数模型受影响，最脆弱模型平均伤害分达 4.12；效应跨不同否定类型学持续，指向语义鲁棒性而非特定语言或语法的缺陷

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Negation remains a persistent challenge for language models across NLP tasks and modalities. We show that this weakness can undermine LLM safety guardrails through a structural vulnerability we term polarity blindness: the failure to recognize harmful intent after polarity-altering linguistic transformations. We study two forms of polarity inversion: grammatical negation and antonym replacement, and use synonym replacement as a polarity-preserving control. We further test whether an adversarial textual wrapper amplifies these failures. Using a 260-prompt benchmark across 10 state-of-the-art LLMs and 3 languages (English, Spanish, Czech), stratified by harmfulness potential and evaluated on a validated 0-5 harm scale, we find that polarity blindness affects most evaluated models, with mean harm scores reaching 4.12 for the most vulnerable model. The effect persists across typologically differing negation systems, suggesting that polarity blindness reflects a broader limitation in semantic robustness rather than a language- or grammar-specific failure.

</details>

### 32. Poisoning-Based Backdoors Are Not Cryptographic

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28045)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`后门理论`、`可学习性归约`、`密码不对称`

👤 **作者**：Masahiro Kaneko

- 🎯 **研究动机**：后门防御须识别判定输入是否触发器的验证函数，若投毒安装能形成密码学式不对称（只有攻击者能造触发输入），学习式防御原则上不可能
- 🔬 **研究方法**：归约论证：能从投毒数据安装验证函数（后门对未见触发器激活且保良性）的训练过程本身就是该验证函数的学习器——计算难学的验证函数无法经投毒安装，可安装的则可被持有可比标记触发样本的白盒防御者重构
- 📌 **结论**：投毒式安装不可能产生密码学不对称，推理时供模型的密钥对同一防御者可观察；为后门攻防的可学习性边界提供理论基础

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

To defend against backdoor attacks on neural networks, the defender must identify the verifier function that determines whether an input is a trigger, rather than rejecting individual triggers in isolation, since an attacker can generate new triggers satisfying the same verifier faster than they can be blocked. If a backdoor verifier could be installed with the asymmetry of a cryptographic authentication scheme, in which only the attacker can produce trigger inputs while the defender cannot reverse-engineer the verifier from observations, learning-based defense would be impossible in principle. We show that no such asymmetry can arise from poisoning-based installation, in which the only verifier-dependent input to training is a poisoned dataset. The argument is a reduction. A training procedure that installs a verifier from poisoned data, so that the backdoor fires on unseen triggers while benign behavior is preserved, is itself a learner for that verifier. Any verifier that is computationally hard to learn is therefore impossible to install through poisoning, and any verifier that poisoning can install is reconstructible by a white-box defender holding comparably many labeled trigger observations. A complementary argument shows that a secret key supplied to the model at inference is observable to the same defender. The result takes representative trigger observations as given and concerns when the backdoor fires rather than what it does. Experiments on instruction-tuned large language models confirm the dichotomy, with no configuration yielding a backdoor that is both effective for the attacker and irrecoverable for the defender.

</details>

### 33. ClawMMU: Protection-Coherent Virtual Memory for Stateful LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28093)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent 内存保护`、`来源分型`、`提示注入`

👤 **作者**：Jinhao Hu、…、Laurent Bindschaedler

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 长期记忆多来自网页与工具结果，harness 不记录谁可对记忆操作，被取页面中的隐藏指令可被当作用户本人指令
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ClawMMU 保护层给每块记忆标注所有者、范围、来源与权限，在内容进入上下文、写存储、存储返回与驱动工具调用四点检查标签；核心规则：信息可以提升权限，权限永不自升
- 📌 **结论**：检查只依赖内容来自何处而非说了什么，即使模型被骗，记忆攻击也退化为 harness 能拦下的经典保护错误

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents keep long-term memory, much of which comes from web pages and tool results. Agent harnesses, the software that runs the model, do not record who may act on this memory, so an instruction hidden in a fetched page can be treated as the user's own instruction. We propose ClawMMU, a protection layer that labels every piece of memory with its owner, scope, origins, and authority. ClawMMU checks these labels whenever content enters the model's context, is written to storage, returns from storage, or drives a tool call. Its central rule is that information may rise in authority, but authority never lifts itself. Because the checks depend on where content came from, not on what it says, memory attacks become classical protection faults that the harness stops even when the model is fooled.

</details>

### 34. Perceives but Doesn't Brake: A Perception-Action Binding Gap in Reasoning Driving VLAs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28184)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`感知-行动绑定缺口`、`驾驶VLA`、`生成时干预`

👤 **作者**：Matteo Cercola、Katie Z Luo、Simone Formentin、Marco Pavone

- 🎯 **研究动机**：推理增强驾驶 VLA 能感知并推理安全关键危险却无法把证据转化为安全动作
- 🔬 **研究方法**：配对反事实与日志回放证明路径内危险确已编码于视觉表征，但纵向规划被自车速度历史主导；不解冻权重，向 Alpamayo-1.5 冻结流匹配采样器注入危险条件能量恢复耦合
- 📌 **结论**：精确障碍几何下足迹侵入从 58.1% 降到 14.5%，制动距离充足时恢复安全刹车并消除碰撞，把计划的依赖从跟踪自车速度翻转为响应障碍距离——不安全行为可源于证据与能力俱在却缺绑定

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reasoning-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) driving models can perceive and reason about safety-critical hazards yet fail to translate that evidence into safe actions. We characterize this failure as a perception-action binding gap: hazard information is available inside the model but exerts insufficient control over trajectory generation. Using matched simulated counterfactuals and logged driving replay, we show that in-path hazards are actually encoded in the visual representation, but longitudinal plans are dominated by ego-speed history and respond only weakly to hazard proximity or the model's own reasoning trace. Further analysis shows that the trajectory generator is capable of braking but sometimes fails to do so due to the perception-action binding gap. Without retraining or modifying any weights, we inject a hazard-conditioned energy into the frozen flow-matching sampler of Alpamayo-1.5, restoring the missing coupling at generation time. With exact obstacle geometry, guidance cuts footprint intrusion from 58.1% to 14.5% and recovers safe braking whenever sufficient stopping distance remains, eliminating collisions entirely in our braking-relevant closed-loop battery. It also reverses the diagnosed dependence of the plan, from tracking ego speed to responding to obstacle range. These results separate hazard perception from hazard control in driving VLAs, show that unsafe behavior can arise even when the model has both the evidence and the ability to act on it, and propose a simple fix for bridging this binding gap.

</details>

### 35. Reward Misspecification Degrades Chain-of-Thought Monitorability

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28303)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`CoT 可监测性`、`奖励误设`、`RLVR`

👤 **作者**：Tianyi Lorena Yan、John Hewitt

- 🎯 **研究动机**：监控思维链是检测预防不当行为的关键策略，识别导致 CoT 不可监测的 RL 实践至关重要，而奖励误设可能不可避免
- 🔬 **研究方法**：构建成对真奖励与偏设奖励的测试床使奖励黑客可训练且可验证标注，跨三模型三任务测 CoT 可监测性；缓解上跨训练步收集多样奖励 rollout 让 LM 反推奖励描述并注入输入
- 📌 **结论**：仅奖励误设即使黑率上升 CoT 可监测性保持低位：CoT 仍围绕任务意图推理而输出违反意图并得分；奖励描述训练使可监测性绝对提升 24%/42%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Monitoring the chain-of-thought (CoT) of language models (LMs) is a key strategy for detecting and preventing misbehaviors for AI safety. As such, it is critical to identify reinforcement learning (RL) practices that lead to unmonitorable chain-of-thought. One such practice may be unavoidable: reward misspecification. As RL environments become more complex, it is infeasible to have perfect verification, and models are rewarded for behaviors that are different from the true task intent. We show that reward misspecification alone already degrades monitorability. We create a testbed with pairs of true reward aligned with task intent and misspecified rewards that favor a different behavior, so that reward hacking can be induced by training and labeled verifiably. Across three models and three tasks, CoT monitorability remains low as models' hack rates increase: the CoTs keep reasoning about the task intent while the outputs violate the intent and get rewarded. To mitigate unmonitorability without prior knowledge of the true reward or the hack, we collect rollouts with diverse rewards across training steps and use a LM to infer a reward description. We then inject the description into the input, which teaches the model to verbalize the reward. This reward description training improves monitorability by an average of 24% or 42% absolute, depending on the metric, across all models and tasks, and yields CoTs that reason about the misspecified reward explicitly. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 36. Disclosure Begets Disclosure: A Self-Reinforcing Privacy Loop in LLM Agents with Persistent Memory

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28315)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`隐私自增强回路`、`持久记忆`、`属性泄露`

👤 **作者**：Masahiro Kaneko、Hiromi Arai、Timothy Baldwin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：带持久记忆的 agent 在对话中查阅记忆会改变用户后续说什么，从而改变记忆最终泄露什么
- 🔬 **研究方法**：多会话模拟：24 个个人属性的模拟用户与是否查阅记忆的支持 agent 对话，从累积记录推断属性值，跨四个支持模型
- 📌 **结论**：记忆提升回应质量但也提高经验泄露率且随会话放大——常规非对抗协助即形成自增强回路（预设备旧值被确认、纠错本身即披露）；在 agent 回应处削弱最有效，指令不陈述/不预设属性可把泄露拉回无记忆水平

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use persistent memory to personalize responses, and personal attributes in this memory pose a privacy risk if exposed. We ask how consulting memory during dialogue changes what users go on to say, and thus what the memory comes to reveal. In multi-session simulations, a simulated user with 24 personal attributes talks with a support agent that does or does not consult memory, and in both settings attributes are inferred from the full accumulated record. Across four support models, memory raises response quality but also empirical leakage, the share of attribute values correctly inferred from the record, by a margin that widens over sessions. The cause is a loop in ordinary, non-adversarial assistance. Memory-conditioned responses carry earlier values forward, often as presuppositions, and users affirm them or leave them uncorrected, so that cues and the agent's own guesses become recorded values. The loop reinforces itself, and removing the agent's mentions of an attribute lowers its later disclosure about twice as much as single-turn effects predict. Users cannot leave it on their own, since correcting a value discloses the attribute, declining to discuss it shifts later predictions toward sensitive values, and incorrect values left in place become entrenched. The loop is best weakened at the agent's response. Instructing the agent not to state or presuppose attributes brings leakage to about the no-memory level while keeping most of the quality gains, whereas filtering or anonymizing what users say or what is stored gives up much of them.

</details>

### 37. Is Sycophancy a Form of In-Context Updating?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28325)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`谄媚机制`、`上下文更新`、`因果证据`

👤 **作者**：Itai Shapira、Myra Cheng、Hadas Orgad

- 🎯 **研究动机**：事实谄媚通常被理解为牺牲真实换用户认可，作者主张它是更一般且有用的上下文更新过程中的失败
- 🔬 **研究方法**：行为与机制双重证据：模型对用户断言的回应处于来源敏感更新的更广模式（外部来源也修订、证据支持越强越改、无认可利害仍顺从）；内部支撑用户顺从的组件对一般上下文更新同样因果重要
- 📌 **结论**：谄媚源于对用户断言赋予相对证据过高的权重；因其嵌入有用过程，削减它可能同时削减有用更新——评测应测双行为、缓解应解耦两者

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Factual sycophancy, a language model's deference to a user's false beliefs, is usually understood as a bid for the user's approval at the expense of truthfulness. We argue that this framing over-emphasizes the user and propose viewing sycophancy as a failure within the broader and generally useful process of in-context updating, by which models revise their answers based on information supplied during an interaction. We support this view with behavioral and mechanistic evidence. First, models' responses to user claims sit within a broader pattern of source-sensitive updating. They revise their answers in response to information attributed to external sources as well, more so when it appears better supported, and their deference to the user persists even when no user approval is at stake. Second, the internal components that support deference to the user are also causally important for in-context updating more broadly. Our results suggest that factual sycophancy arises when in-context updating gives a user's assertion excessive weight relative to its evidence. Because sycophancy is embedded in this process, reducing it risks reducing useful updating as well, a trade-off that calls for evaluations measuring both behaviors and mitigations that decouple them.

</details>

### 38. Context-Aware Prompt Injection Detection via Small Language Models in RAG Pipelines: nokast-secureRAG

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28364)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`提示注入防御`、`小型语言模型`、`RAG 防火墙`

👤 **作者**：Abhishek Rai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：本地 RAG 系统易受间接提示注入与检索投毒：检索内容中的恶意指令被当可信输入
- 🔬 **研究方法**：nokast-secureRAG 约 0.5B 参数语义防火墙置于检索与生成之间，联合评估查询与上下文输出安全/可疑/恶意三分；教师-裁判-学生管线：35B 教师生成 5233 例、独立 120B 裁判重标（95% 一致）后微调学生
- 📌 **结论**：分布内检测召回 0.994@FPR 0.026、ASR 代理 0.006，超正则基线、公开分类器与 Llama Guard 3 1B；判断翻转测试（同句依上下文善恶）正确率 75% 领先基线，摊销批处理时延 37ms

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Local Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection and retrieval poisoning, where malicious instructions in retrieved content are mistaken for trusted input. We present nokast-secureRAG, a roughly 0.5B-parameter small language model that acts as a semantic firewall between retrieval and generation. It jointly evaluates the user query and retrieved context, classifying the pair as safe, suspicious, or malicious. The model is trained using a teacher-judge-student pipeline: a 35B teacher generates 5,233 examples across four attack families, and an independent 120B judge re-labels them, achieving 95.0% agreement before student fine-tuning. On a held-out in-distribution test set, the model achieves 0.994 detection recall at a 0.026 false-positive rate and a 0.006 attack-success-rate proxy, outperforming a regex baseline, a public prompt-injection classifier, Llama Guard 3 1B, and its untuned version. Prompt-Guard-86M reaches 0.900 recall but has a 0.690 false-positive rate. On a judgment-flip test, where the same sentence is benign or malicious depending on context, the model correctly classifies both cases 75% of the time, outperforming the compared baselines. Ablations indicate that retrieved context carries the main attack signal, while query-context consistency reduces false positives. Single-request latency is 293-404 ms, above the target of 100 ms, while amortized throughput latency is 37 ms under batching. On the external PoisonedRAG benchmark, recall falls to near zero, indicating that fluent fact-poisoning attacks lie outside the detector's instruction-consistency threat model. We report this limitation alongside the in-distribution results. The model, data-generation pipeline, and evaluation harness are released open source.

</details>

### 39. Relative Representations Enable Portable White-Box Monitoring Across Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28366)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`白盒监控迁移`、`相对表征`、`锚定探针`

👤 **作者**：Tim Bakker、Christos Louizos

- 🎯 **研究动机**：白盒监控探针绑定特定模型激活坐标，模型更换即失效——若独立训练模型以兼容方式组织信息则探针可移植
- 🔬 **研究方法**：用相对表征（到共享锚点集的角距离）替代原生激活坐标，全局锚定构造汇总全部 transformer 层信息免去逐模型层选择，同一冻结探针直接用于另一模型
- 📌 **结论**：冷知识、安全监控与数学推理任务上跨模型迁移探针保持同模型 AUROC 增益的至少 85%，跨数据集、模型规模与提示变化稳健，但迁移任务与目标依赖且可能失败

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Independently trained autoregressive language models often acquire similar capabilities, but it remains unclear whether their internal representations organize behaviorally relevant information in compatible ways. We show that models do in fact organize information similarly by using specific behavioral probes that operate on compatible representations. This unlocks portable white-box monitoring: a probe developed and validated on one model can be reused when the model changes, without requiring labeled target data. To overcome incompatible native activation coordinates, we express each example through its angular distances to a shared set of anchors. We introduce a global anchored construction that summarizes information from all transformer layers and removes model-specific layer selection, so the same frozen probe can be straightforwardly applied to another model. We evaluate cross-model probe transfer across monitoring tasks on trivia datasets, safety monitoring, and mathematical reasoning. On average, transferred global anchored probes preserve at least 85% of same-model probe AUROC over chance. Anchored probes also support transfer to related datasets, remain robust across model scale and prompt changes, and retain performance better than standard probes after model updates. Transfer is nevertheless task- and target-dependent and can fail even when the monitored property remains strongly decodable in the target model. Overall, global anchoring reveals substantial task-relevant compatibility across independently trained models and provides a practical basis for portable white-box monitoring.

</details>

### 40. CAPSafe: A Benchmark for Contextual Action and Plan Safety Reasoning in Embodied Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28381)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`具身安全推理`、`动作安全`、`长程规划`

👤 **作者**：Youmna Farag、Svetlana Stoyanchev、Simon Keizer、Mohan Li、Rama Doddipatla

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 驱动的具身 agent 必须在动作与计划两级推理安全性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CAPSafe：动作级数据集测单一动作在环境上下文中的安全性，计划级测危险家务长程安全规划；危险情境化（由物体属性推断）、多危险共存并标相对严重度
- 📌 **结论**：九个闭源开源 LLM 上危险识别可超 90% 但安全长程规划显著更难：Gemini-3-pro 安全成功率最高 78.19%，其余低于 65%——长动作序列上的安全推理仍是开放挑战

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reasoning about safety is essential for embodied agents powered by large language models (LLMs). We introduce the Contextual Action and Plan Safety benchmark (CAPSafe), designed to evaluate safety reasoning at two levels: (1) an action-level dataset that tests whether a single action is safe given its environmental context, and (2) a plan-level dataset that evaluates whether agents can generate safe long-horizon plans for complex hazardous household tasks. CAPSafe models safety conditions where hazards are situational (inferred from object properties), multiple hazards can co-occur within the same environment, and each is annotated with a relative severity level for fine-grained analysis. Evaluating nine closed and open-source LLMs reveals a substantial gap between hazard recognition and safe long-horizon planning. Several models achieve high accuracy in recognizing unsafe actions (exceeding 90% in the best cases), yet safe long-horizon planning remains substantially harder. Gemini-3-pro achieves the highest safe success rate (78.19%), while other models remain below 65%. These results highlight that robust safety reasoning over long action sequences remains an open challenge.

</details>

### 41. MAS-Guard: Unified Guardrails from Individual Agents to Multi-Agent LLM Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28397)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多智能体护栏`、`统一框架`、`控制平面`

👤 **作者**：Mohammad Kachuee、…、Dan Roth

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多智能体系统分布式权威、异质角色与可能被攻陷的参与者使防护更难，现有防御碎片化覆盖局部执行、系统交互与执行机制
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MAS-Guard：可插桩 agent 内 A-Guard 层用任务专家评估 agent 事件；系统级开发者持有的 Agent Charters 与控制平面结合路由/能力/消息格式的确定性结构检查与角色内容语义评估；共享干预集允许/反思/确认/拦截
- 📌 **结论**：六个单/多智能体基准×三家 agent LLM 上总攻击成功率降 71%，统一覆盖信息泄露与对抗操纵

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agentic AI extends large language models (LLMs) from generating responses to planning and executing complex workflows, substantially expanding their utility while exposing applications to unsafe actions, information leakage, and adversarial manipulation.
