Multi-agent systems further expand these capabilities through specialization and collaboration, but distributed authority, heterogeneous roles, and opaque or potentially compromised participants make safeguarding even more challenging. Existing defenses address important individual threats but provide fragmented coverage across local execution, system-level interactions, and enforcement mechanisms. We introduce MAS-Guard, a unified framework that closes this gap for safeguarding single- and multi-agent AI applications containing both instrumentable and opaque agents. Within instrumentable agents, the proposed local Agent Guardrail (A-Guard) layer applies task-specific experts to assess agentic events. At the system level, developer-owned Agent Charters and a Control Plane combine deterministic structural checks of routes, agent capabilities, and message formats with semantic assessments of roles and message content. These complementary layers enable enforcement through a shared set of interventions: allow, reflection, confirmation, and block. Evaluated across six benchmarks covering diverse single- and multi-agent scenarios with three agent LLMs from different providers, MAS-Guard reduces the overall attack success rate by 71.5-86.3% relative to unguarded agents, while meeting the utility reference criterion on benign tasks.

</details>

### 42. After the Attacker Leaves: Benchmarking Carryover Attacks on LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28425)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`驻留攻击`、`跨会话`、`提示注入`

👤 **作者**：Zhiyue Xu、Xukai Wang、Zhiyuan Huang、Mingjie Zhan、Jian Zhao、Xu-Yao Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 经两通道跨会话持久状态：静默前置到系统提示的长期记忆文件与无用户输入定时唤醒的调度任务——一次性间接注入可在攻击者离开后长期复燃
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CarryoverBench：12 个人助理场景 101 攻击任务跨三触发通道（同轮 IPI、心跳调度、记忆复现），规则验证器把植入与触发记为独立阶段；五前沿模型五回合测衰减
- 📌 **结论**：植入成功后载荷五回合无 measurable 衰减、触发阶段几乎无额外防护；迁移到第二运行时 Hermes 衰减但不消除——驻留防御需运行时层控制而非仅提示层干预

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Modern LLM agents persist state across sessions via two channels: long-lived memory files silently prepended to the system prompt, and scheduled tasks waking the agent without user input. A one-time indirect prompt injection committed to either channel can re-fire across sessions long after the attacker leaves, threats we term carryover attacks. Existing prompt-injection benchmarks evaluate attacks only in the session where malicious content is first observed, leaving the gate governing when and how often a planted payload later re-fires unmeasured. We present CarryoverBench: 12 personal-assistant scenarios and 101 attack tasks across three trigger channels: Instant, same-turn IPI; Autonomous, heartbeat-scheduled; and Latent, memory-resurfaced. A rule-based verifier scores planting and triggering as independent stages. Across five frontier models, planted payloads re-fire without measurable decay over five rounds; conditional on successful planting, the trigger stage often provides little additional protection. Porting the Latent channel to a second runtime, Hermes, attenuates but does not eliminate the effect, suggesting that carryover defense requires runtime-layer controls in addition to prompt-level interventions.

</details>

### 43. Toolflation: Seller Agents Distort Tool Markets Through Spontaneous Generative Engine Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28454)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`工具市场失真`、`生成式引擎优化`、`agent经济`

👤 **作者**：Zhenwei Tang、Di Bai、Jintao Liu、Xidong Wu、Qianfeng Wen、Xinwu Cheng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 开始向 agent 销售：卖方 agent 撰写工具服务listing，买方 agent 读listing选择——卖方仅被要求更多被选会自发做 GEO 并扭曲市场
- 🔬 **研究方法**：用 Amazon/Instagram 真实数据工具建市场并每个工具跑每个问题以知真实能力，五个生产模型下观察卖方重写描述的行为与买方选择迁移
- 📌 **结论**：卖方声称越来越多的字段（含不到一半目标能返回的），能力最强工具在 10 组组合中 7 个丢份额；第五个模型下卖方互相抄袭致描述无法区分，买方按列表位置选择、几乎无用的工具赢 1/4-2/5 选择、任务成功率从 89% 跌到约 30%——无任何市场机制限制 toolflation

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Agents are starting to sell to other agents. Seller agents write the listings of tools, services and products, and buyer agents read these listings when they choose what to use. We show that seller agents told only to be selected more often practice generative engine optimization on their own, and that this distorts the market for sellers and for buyers. We build tool markets from real data tools on Amazon and Instagram and run every tool on every question, so we know which tools can answer each question. Under all five production models we test, sellers rewrite their descriptions to claim more and more fields, including fields that their tools return for fewer than half of the targets. We call this process toolflation. Nothing in the market limits it, and how far it goes depends on the model, as the number of claims that contradict the sellers' own evidence differs by an order of magnitude between models. Toolflation shifts the buyers' choices between sellers, and the most capable tool loses share in seven of the ten combinations of seller model and platform. Under four models, the choices stay with capable tools and buyer agents keep their task success. Under the fifth, sellers copy one another until the descriptions no longer tell the tools apart. Buyer agents then pick tools by their position in the list, tools that answer almost nothing win a quarter to two fifths of the choices, and task success falls from 89% to about 30% on Amazon, close to random choice, and from 77% to 40% on Instagram. The sellers did only what their vendors asked, and the cost fell on buyers they never dealt with. We therefore argue that honesty toward third parties under competition is part of safety alignment, and that models should be tested as sellers before they are deployed as sellers.

</details>

### 44. Proof-Carrying Contracts: Commitments AI Agents Cannot Break

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28484)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`证明携带合约`、`agent 经济`、`不可违背承诺`

👤 **作者**：Carlo Ciliberto、Alessandro Rudi、Enrico Tassi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 替人购物、交换信息、委托工作，但文本合约不能使其合规：会犯错、被所读内容改向或另有所图——不互信的 agent 经济需要可依赖的协议
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PC2 框架：请求操作运行前 agent 须提交保持约定约束的机器可检证明，Airlock 执行系统验 proof 并控制操作访问；各方指定要保的条件、agent 供给推理并保留策略自由，附可复用能力模块
- 📌 **结论**：证明对抗性 agent 也在执行全程每刻成立，并刻画何时蕴含世界安全，为保护委托双方的 agent 经济奠定基础

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI agents increasingly act on behalf of people or other agents, purchasing services, exchanging information and delegating work. What an agent must respect is written in instructions, policies or agreements, but text alone cannot make it comply. It may make mistakes, be redirected by what it reads, or pursue different interests. For an economy of such agents to function, parties need agreements they can rely on without inspecting or trusting one another's agents. We introduce Proof-Carrying Contracts (PC2), a framework for making agreed constraints enforceable throughout an agent's interactions with a system. Before a requested operation can run, the agent must supply a machine-checkable proof that it preserves the agreed constraints in every case allowed by its model. An execution system called the Airlock checks the proof and controls access to the operation. The parties specify the conditions to preserve, while the agent supplies the reasoning needed to justify its decisions and remains free to adapt its strategy. This avoids having to design a separate permission policy and prove that every decision it permits is safe. We prove that the constraints hold at every instant during execution, even for an adversarial agent, and identify when this implies safety in the world. Reusable capability modules provide tools and guarantees from which contracts can be constructed. PC2 provides a foundation for an agent economy whose agreements protect both those delegating work and those carrying it out.

</details>

### 45. Binary Discrete Diffusion: Candidate-Set Diffusion for Logic and Planning Tasks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28543)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`离散扩散`、`候选集扩散`、`逻辑规划`

👤 **作者**：Rodrigo Casado Noguerales、Dimitri von Rütte、Bernhard Schölkopf、Thomas Hofmann、Aran Raoufi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：掩码/均匀扩散在单次腐蚀中抹去词元身份，噪声态无法显式表示多个候选仍皆可能的未解不确定性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：二元离散扩散：噪声态为每位置候选词元集合（二元成员向量），经增删渐进改变候选集，使信息可丢失又可恢复、扩散态本身表示部分不确定性
- 📌 **结论**：Sudoku-Extreme/Boxoban/图着色上对称表述在匹配架构与训练采样预算下持续超强调优的掩码/均匀基线（最难实例增益最大），语言建模保持竞争力——腐蚀核实质改变反向过程学到的计算

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Discrete diffusion models iteratively denoise a noisy sequence of categorical data such as text, enabling any-order generation with self-correction and in-filling capabilities. This makes them promising candidates for reasoning, planning, and constrained-generation tasks like coding and math. Yet masked and uniform diffusion erase a token's identity in a single corruption event, so their noisy states cannot explicitly represent partially resolved uncertainty in which several alternatives remain plausible. We close this gap with Binary Discrete Diffusion, a family of continuous-time discrete-diffusion processes whose noisy state is a set of candidate tokens per position, represented as a binary membership vector. Binary diffusion changes candidate sets through additions and removals, allowing information to be lost and recovered gradually while the diffusion state itself represents partial uncertainty. On Sudoku-Extreme, Boxoban, and graph coloring, the symmetric formulation of binary diffusion consistently outperforms strong, well-tuned masked and uniform baselines under matched architectures and controlled training, sampling, and tuning budgets, with its largest gains on the hardest instances, while remaining competitive on language modeling (Text8, TinyStories, OpenWebText). These results support candidate sets as an effective intermediate representation for iterative reasoning and show that the corruption kernel can materially change the computation learned by the reverse model.

</details>

### 46. Large Flow Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28559)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`流语言模型`、`8B 扩展`、`自投机解码`

👤 **作者**：Arnav Chavan、…、Jinwoo Kim

- 🎯 **研究动机**：连续流语言模型提供并行生成与离散扩散没有的控制加速工具，但十亿参数级的有效构建技术尚不成熟
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Lafa：前缀因果注意力+块内双向异步流的分块流模型，适配预训练 AR 高效训练、前缀缓存与块并行解码快速推理；0.5B/7B/8B 三规模 <30B 词元训练
- 📌 **结论**：指令遵循、推理与编码上匹配或超越 AR 质量；流训练经多词元预测效应保持甚至提升 AR 能力，自投机解码（流草稿 AR 验证）达 2.54 倍吞吐

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Continuous flow language models offer a path to parallel generation with tools for control and acceleration unavailable to discrete diffusion, yet effective techniques for building them at the billion-parameter scale remain less established. We present Lafa (Large flow language model), a fast, high-performing flow language model scaled to 8B parameters in a data-efficient manner. Lafa is a blockwise flow model with causal attention on the prefix and bidirectional, asynchronous flow within each block. This design enables efficient training by adapting pretrained autoregressive (AR) models and fast inference via prefix caching and blockwise parallel decoding. We train Lafa at 0.5B, 7B, and 8B scales with fewer than 30B tokens, more efficient than previous diffusion models, and evaluate it on instruction following, reasoning, and coding, where the learned flow matches or surpasses the quality of AR and can be distilled into flow maps for one- and few-step generation. Notably, flow training preserves or even improves pretrained AR capabilities via a multi-token prediction effect, which we leverage in a self-speculative decoding scheme that verifies flow-based drafts autoregressively within a single model. This yields 2.54x the throughput of an AR model at comparable quality, outperforming multi-token prediction and discrete diffusion methods. Our findings show that continuous flows can serve as a scalable foundation for language modeling.

</details>

### 47. Beyond the Sequentiality Bottleneck: A Hybrid Diffusion-Autoregressive Framework for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28584)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`混合扩散-自回归`、`非对称规划者-执行者`、`奖励引导去噪`

👤 **作者**：Wei Cai、Jian Zhao、Yuchen Yuan、Xuelong Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自回归模型推理受串行瓶颈，扩散语言模型并行但缺语义连贯，需混合框架兼得
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CoRAL：DLM 规划者并行生成 K 条多样推理轨迹，大型 ARM 执行者选最优并合成终答；RDC 把结果奖励积分入扩散过程引导目标导向推理
- 📌 **结论**：视觉语言基准上显著超先前扩散方法并与强 AR 竞争，兼顾效率与质量

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Autoregressive models (ARMs) face a "Sequentiality Bottleneck" in reasoning, while diffusion language models (DLMs) offer parallelism but lack semantic coherence. To addresses both limitations, we propose Collaborative Reasoning Asymmetric Learning (CoRAL), a hybrid framework via an Asymmetric Planner-Executor (APE) architecture: a DLM Planner generates K diverse reasoning trajectories in parallel, while a large ARM Executor selects the optimal plan and synthesizes the final answer. To train the Planner effectively, we introduce Reward-Guided Denoising for CoT (RDC), which integrates outcome-based rewards into the diffusion process to steer it toward goal-directed reasoning. Experiments on vision-language benchmarks show that CoRAL significantly outperforms prior diffusion-based methods and achieves competitive performance against strong ARMs, achieving both superior efficiency and quality.

</details>

## 常规收录

### 48. H2DF: Calibrated Target Bands for Controllable Language-Model Unlearning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26864)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`机器遗忘`、`梯度上升`、`目标带校准`

👤 **作者**：Jupiter Yao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：梯度上升遗忘基线在有效遗忘发生后仍持续加大 forget loss，无界压力损害保留性能
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出 H2DF 校准目标带：低于数据依赖下限时施加 GA 式压力、到达目标区后抑制或反转，目标由匹配非成员样本预先估计，仅每十步做一次稀疏保留回放
- 📌 **结论**：在 TOFU 与 MUSE-News 上匹配或超过 GradDiff/NPO/SimNPO 且变异更低，上界目标减少过冲，但对随机种子与校准质量敏感

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Gradient ascent (GA) is a simple language-model unlearning baseline, but it continues increasing forget loss after useful forgetting has occurred. This unbounded pressure can damage retained utility and make forgotten examples abnormally high-loss. We introduce H2DF, a calibrated target-band objective that applies GA-like pressure below a data-dependent lower target and suppresses, or reverses, that pressure after the target region is reached. The targets are estimated from matched non-member examples before training, so the base objective requires one forget-batch forward/backward pass per optimization step; our reported TOFU variant adds sparse retain replay every tenth step. We instantiate a one-sided objective for TOFU question answering and a two-sided objective for MUSE-News language modeling. Across three TOFU seeds, H2DF obtains a forget NLL of 1.528 with margin 0.049 and retain NLL of 1.165 with margin 0.047, matching GradDiff while using retain replay only every tenth step and showing substantially lower variability than GA. On MUSE-News, H2DF improves retention over GA and NPO and is competitive with SimNPO, but does not dominate it. Ablations show that the upper target reduces overshoot and that validation-based checkpoint selection is important. These results support calibrated target bands as a practical mechanism for controlling forgetting strength, while also exposing sensitivity to random seed and calibration quality.

</details>

### 49. High-dimensional layer-state trajectories reveal the internal dynamics of transformer inference

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26866)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`机制可解释性`、`层状态轨迹`、`干预控制`

👤 **作者**：Heng Zhao、Yufei Wang、Ruidian Song、Weisheng Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机制研究把功能定位于参数、注意力头或神经元，但未说明单次推理如何展开
- 🔬 **研究方法**：将有序层状态轨迹视为经验过程，检验层序是否定义可计算动力学：端点保持洗牌对照、局部转移致动器与置信门控制
- 📌 **结论**：实现层序路径对端点弦的比对随机序高 1.6-2.24 倍，置信门使 87 条 held-out 轨迹中 17 条跨过全词表 top-1 边界且不改变 203 条非目标输出，安全匹配的动作重定向表现相近

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Mechanistic studies of large language models localize function in parameters, attention heads or neurons, but those parts do not specify how one inference unfolds. We treat the ordered layer-state trajectory as the empirical process and test whether layer order defines a measurable computation. Across representative Qwen, Llama and Gemma checkpoints, realized order produced paths 1.60--2.24 times as aligned with their endpoint chord as endpoint-preserving shuffles. The order effect transferred to a lexical task, but was stronger in three random initializations of the same Qwen architecture, identifying an architecture-compatible scaffold rather than a training-specific law. Direct hidden states provided stronger mechanism coordinates than vocabulary readbacks, and a task-defined coordinate made candidate competition computable. After additive interventions failed, local transition actuators selectively controlled internal trajectories across checkpoints. A confidence gate then drove 17 of 87 held-out Qwen closure trajectories across the full-vocabulary top-1 candidate boundary while leaving all 203 non-closure top-1 outputs unchanged. Safety-matched action reassignment performed similarly, locating the gain in selective gating and the bounded actuator family rather than fine-grained dose assignment. Layer order exposes an observable, computable and behaviourally consequential process of transformer inference without establishing a complete theory of LLM dynamics or control of answer correctness.

</details>

### 50. Steerable Pluralistic Policy Alignment from Anonymized Crowd Feedback

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26914)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多元对齐`、`偏好学习`、`个性化策略`

👤 **作者**：Akansha Kalra、Ellen R. Novoseller、Vernon J. Lawhern、Daniel S. Brown

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多元对齐要求从可能冲突的群体偏好中学习并个性化，但现有方法依赖标注者身份与强预训练表征
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出 P3L：从匿名异质群体反馈中联合学习轨迹嵌入与偏好方向的 PCA 共享基，测试时快速个性化奖励学习并做策略优化
- 📌 **结论**：在多个具身控制任务上实现可转向的个性化对齐，能泛化到分布外新用户并大幅降低计算成本

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Pluralistic alignment requires AI systems to learn from diverse populations whose preferences may reflect heterogeneous or even conflicting objectives, and to personalize their behavior to individual users. Existing approaches in pluralistic alignment often assume access to annotator identities and rely on powerful pretrained representations to model individual preference variations. We propose Preference-PCA Pluralistic Learning (P3L), a novel framework for pluralistic alignment that jointly learns trajectory embeddings and the shared basis of preference directions via PCA decomposition from heterogeneous crowd feedback without requiring annotator identities. At test time, our approach enables rapid and accurate personalized reward learning and downstream policy optimization. Experiments across multiple embodied control tasks demonstrate that P3L enables steerable personal alignment that generalizes beyond the offline dataset to out-of-distribution new users and attains competitive RL and test performance, while substantially reducing computational cost and supporting variable adaptation query budgets.

</details>

### 51. Do LLMs Really Lack Difference Awareness? Uncovering Heuristic Substitution in Group-Differentiating Fairness

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26961)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`公平性`、`启发式替代`、`神经元探针`

👤 **作者**：Jingshen Zhang、…、Yuexian Hou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 在群体区分公平任务上表现差被归因于缺乏差异意识，作者提出替代解释
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出公平启发式替代假设：模型以更易得的形式平等启发式替代复杂差异判断；设计两阶段区分-决定框架 TDD 并做神经元级对比探针
- 📌 **结论**：四个基准十个模型上 TDD 显著优于单阶段基线，探针揭示形式平等的紧凑局部协同表征结构，为假设提供机制证据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In this paper, we investigate the issue of difference awareness in LLMs, defined as a model's ability to recognize and acknowledge meaningful differences between social groups in fairness decision-making. Previous studies have consistently shown that models perform poorly on such tasks, a finding commonly attributed to a lack of difference awareness. We propose an alternative account, which we term Fairness Heuristic Substitution: difference awareness is not necessarily absent, but the complex demand of simultaneously identifying relevant group differences and determining their implications for fairness decisions may lead models to substitute the simpler, more accessible equal-treatment heuristic, i.e., formal equality. Based on this hypothesis, we introduce TDD, a Two-stage Distinguish-Decide framework that explicitly decomposes fairness reasoning into first distinguishing contextually relevant group differences and then making the corresponding decision, thereby discouraging reliance on formal-equality shortcuts. (i) Across four benchmarks and ten models ranging from billions to trillions of parameters, TDD significantly outperforms one-stage baselines, even one strengthened through simulated test-set leakage. (ii) Combining TDD with neuron-level contrastive probing provides mechanistic evidence consistent with the Fairness Heuristic Substitution hypothesis by revealing two signatures of the formal-equality: a compact, locally coordinated representational structure and a pronounced activation bias under default reasoning. (iii) TDD's stage-wise decomposition uncovers overlooked failure modes in fairness reasoning, including heuristic displacement and misaligned stage dynamics.

</details>

### 52. Does Multi-Agent Collaboration Reduce Hallucination in Clinical Tasks?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27027)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多智能体系统`、`临床幻觉`、`评测有效性`

👤 **作者**：Won Seok Jang、Harry Bhaskar Joshi、Hong Yu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多智能体临床系统任务性能提升常被解读为更可信，但医生依据的是陈述推理而非仅结论，需测量协作是否降低推理中的幻觉
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四种系统（单 LLM 与三种拓扑）×四模型×三临床任务，在事实性与忠实度分类下对系统生成的理由内部评分幻觉并随任务指标一同报告
- 📌 **结论**：协作换取任务性能但不必然降低幻觉，36 组比较中 12 组可分辨（4 降 8 升），决定水平与方向的是模型而非拓扑；自我纠正实验与该结论一致

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent LLM systems improve performance on clinical tasks, and that improvement is often read as evidence of a more trustworthy system. However, the two are not the same claim. A clinician acts on the stated reasoning and not only on the conclusion, so whether collaboration reduces hallucination, and what decides when it does, has to be measured in the reasoning too. Yet most evaluations score only the final answer. We evaluate four systems (a single-LLM baseline and three multi-agent topologies) across four models and three clinical tasks, scoring hallucination, under a factuality and faithfulness taxonomy, inside the rationale each system generates and reporting it alongside the task metric. We report that collaboration buys task performance but not lower hallucination as a rule, and no topology is reliably better than another. The effect differs by model: of the $36$ model--system--task comparisons, $12$ are large enough to resolve --- four decreases and eight increases, with no model on both sides. What sets the level, and the direction of any change, is the model rather than the topology or the number of agents. A self-correction experiment is consistent with this: shown its own flagged errors, every model except Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct reduces hallucination; the one model for which collaboration lowers hallucination gains the most, and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, the model whose hallucination it amplifies most, does not reduce its hallucination at all. We find that collaboration redistributes what its models can already do; it does not supply what they cannot.

</details>

### 53. Robustness of Internal Signals for Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27041)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`视觉语言模型`、`幻觉检测`、`内部信号`

👤 **作者**：Allison Chen、William Yang、Salma Abdel Magid、Jonathan Williams、Olga Russakovsky、Esin Tureci

- 🎯 **研究动机**：利用 VLM 内部信号的幻觉检测方法可能依赖捷径而非真实视觉证据，需检验其鲁棒性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证明词元位置朴素基线即具竞争力，分析检测方法对位置的依赖及对语言/视觉模态幻觉的敏感度，并测试方法集成
- 📌 **结论**：强检测表现常由语言型幻觉驱动；依赖位置的方法视觉接地更弱且幻觉出现越早鲁棒性越差，弱方法加入集成仍有增益

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When given an image and a text prompt, vision-language models (VLMs) can produce seemingly sophisticated text responses. However, they are also prone to hallucinate objects in their output, which can have detrimental consequences in safety-critical applications. To detect these hallucinations, certain methods interestingly leverage internal signals from the VLM as it generates the text response; potentially, these methods use the same signals that led to a hallucination to detect it. We conduct an in-depth analysis to understand the robustness of these detection methods. First, we show that a naive baseline of a generated word's token position in the VLM text response achieves competitive performance. Further, we reveal how some detection methods rely on token position and use this heuristic as a shortcut. Second, we explore detection methods' sensitivity to language and vision modalities. Our experiments suggest that strong detection performance is often driven by detecting language-based hallucinations, and when methods rely on token position, they tend to be less visually grounded and exhibit reduced robustness when hallucinations occur earlier in a text response. Lastly, we demonstrate that different detection methods can carry complementary signals; when ensembling methods together, adding weaker methods to an ensemble of higher performing ones still offers performance improvements. Altogether, while there may be useful internal VLM signals to detect hallucinations, our investigation reveals important limitations of the current methods that try to leverage them.

</details>

### 54. ECHO: Revealing Review-to-Submission Linkage in Anonymous Peer Review

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27044)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`匿名性攻击`、`同行评审`、`写作风格链接`

👤 **作者**：Rong Wang、Mulong Luo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：匿名评审保护评审人免受报复，但越来越多 venue 要求作者参与评审，评审与投稿在同一周期内高度重叠可能削弱匿名性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出 ECHO：跨评审、论文与反驳结合技术专长与写作风格信号，构造 R2S-Bench 真值基准评估评审-投稿链接的可利用性
- 📌 **结论**：ECHO 在候选投稿中 Top-1 命中评审人本人投稿 34.6%（随机 0.5%），加强重排后达 56.4%，证明仅用常规发布的评审工件即可大幅缩小评审人匿名集

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Anonymous peer review protects reviewers from retaliation, yet reviewer anonymity may also be weakened by artifacts released through the review process itself. This risk is growing as many venues require submitting authors to review, creating substantial overlap between authors and reviewers within the same review cycle. We identify review-to-submission linkage, where an anonymous review can be associated with the reviewer's own submission, substantially shrinking the reviewer anonymity set. To assess whether this linkage is practically exploitable, we develop ECHO, which combines technical-expertise and writing-style signals across reviews, papers, and rebuttals, and evaluate it on R2S-Bench, a ground-truth benchmark we construct. ECHO correctly identifies the reviewer's own submission in Top-1 for 34.6% of reviews, in the Top-5 for 54.0%, and in the Top-10 for 63.4%, compared with 0.5%, 2.6%, and 5.3% under random ranking, from the candidate submissions. With a stronger Stage-2 reranker, ECHO further reaches 56.4% Top-1 and 66.4% Top-10. These results demonstrate that review-to-submission linkage is feasible using only routinely released peer-review artifacts. Code and benchmark are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 55. ClockMark: Adaptive and Host-Dependent Watermarking for Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27079)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM 水印`、`流式生成`、`编辑鲁棒性`

👤 **作者**：Yupeng Qiu、Han Fang、Ee-Chien Chang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 水印在流式生成中嵌入载体（分布）随每个已采样词元持续变化，经典水印的自适应嵌入原则需重新表述
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出自适应且依赖宿主的嵌入框架 ClockMark：保留先前采样决策状态、以当前分布约束词元选择与状态更新，并结合消息扩散在每个水印词元嵌入完整载荷
- 📌 **结论**：理论上证明保持原序列分布，实测对文本编辑的鲁棒性超过 12 个基线最多 16.08 个百分点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language model (LLM) watermarking operates on the next-token distribution that evolves during generation. In streaming generation, each sampled token becomes part of the text and affects subsequent distributions. Embedding therefore proceeds with a changing host, unlike classical watermarking, which adapts embedding to an existing host signal. To accommodate this difference, we formulate a novel embedding framework for LLM watermarking based on two principles. We first argue that embedding should be adaptive, retaining the state of previous sampling decisions to better guide subsequent choices. We further argue that embedding should be host-dependent, using the current LLM distribution to constrain both token selection and state updates. Guided by these principles and the two requirements of distribution preservation and robustness to text editing, we propose ClockMark. It combines adaptive and host-dependent embedding with message spreading, embedding the full payload in each watermarked token. Theoretically, we prove that ClockMark preserves the original LLM sequence distribution, thereby maintaining generation quality. Empirically, extensive experiments against 12 baselines demonstrate improved robustness to text editing, with gains of up to 16.08 percentage points in bit accuracy over the strongest tested baseline.

</details>

### 56. Mind Your Boundaries: Boundary-Aware Model Obfuscation against Cross-node Attack

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27135)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`模型混淆`、`跨节点攻击`、`对抗迁移`

👤 **作者**：Yuyang Zhang、Yihui Jin、Kangjie Chen、Leyang Wu、Run Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：边缘设备被攻破后攻击者可白盒逆转 app 获得同源模型知识并发起跨节点攻击，一台失陷危及全网同源模型
- 🔬 **研究方法**：基于零空间约束把参数分解为边界位置与边界曲率分量，向曲率分量注入噪声实例化决策边界差异化的模型（BMO）
- 📌 **结论**：四类对抗攻击×四个部署模型上平均防御成功率 68.68%，远超最竞争基线，同时保持良性精度

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

With the advancement of powerful AI chips, on-device machine learning is gaining traction for its low latency and privacy protection. However, when edge devices are compromised, attackers can obtain white-box access to the deployed models by reversing the apps directly to obtain the knowledge of peer devices, which is known as Cross-Node Attacks, like performing efficient adversarial attacks. Most existing defenses target known vulnerabilities, yet white-box access still enables attackers to uncover more unknown weaknesses. This creates a critical risk for edge-device deployments, where one compromised device can endanger all models distributed from the same source. Motivated by findings that the model robustness is primarily influenced by the curvature of the decision boundary, we investigate how model parameters shape its curvature. In this paper, we propose a novel method, BMO, that obfuscates model parameters to diversify the decision boundary further resisting cross-node attack while preserving its benign accuracy. Specifically, we explore decomposing model parameters into boundary-position and boundary-curvature components based on null-space constraints, then models with differentiated decision boundaries are instantiated by injecting noise into the boundary-curvature component. Across all four major types of adversarial attacks with four widely-deployed on-device models, BMO achieves an average success rate of 68.68% in defense, far exceeding the most competitive baseline, which attains only 22.32% in defending against cross-node attacks. Moreover, BMO achieves comparable performance in benign accuracy compared with baselines.

</details>

### 57. Model Exposure on One Device Endangers Others: Rethinking Edge Neural Network Security across Devices

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27152)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`边缘模型暴露`、`跨节点攻击`、`模型多样化`

👤 **作者**：Yuyang Zhang、Kangjie Chen、Yihui Jin、Run Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：app 内嵌的端侧模型可被拆包暴露，集中分发生态中一台设备上构造的对抗样本可迁移到同模型的其他节点
- 🔬 **研究方法**：VeilNN 通过网络单元混淆与层组装在架构与参数两级做隐蔽且不可逆的混淆，引入受控模型多样性
- 📌 **结论**：对四类对抗攻击平均防御成功率 49.19%（最高 81.19%），较现有防御提升 33.16% 以上，适用于 MLP/CNN/ViT

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Neural networks (NNs) are increasingly deployed on edge devices such as mobile and IoT platforms, offering low latency and improved privacy compared to cloud-based deployment. However, on-device models embedded in mobile applications can be unpacked and exposed to adversaries, enabling cross-node attacks in centrally distributed ecosystems, where adversarial examples crafted on one compromised device transfer effectively to other nodes sharing the same models. To address this threat, we propose VeilNN, a defense framework that reduces adversarial transferability across on-device models via model obfuscation. The core idea of VeilNN is to introduce controlled model diversity while preserving original task functionality. Specifically, VeilNN applies stealthy and irreversible obfuscation at both the architectural and parameter levels through network cell obfuscation and layer assembling, and is applicable to diverse NN architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, and Vision Transformers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VeilNN effectively defends against cross-node attacks, achieving an average defense success of 49.19% and up to 81.19% across four adversarial attack types, with more than 33.16% improvement over existing defenses.

</details>

### 58. SITO: Sifting a Commercial Text-Safety API for Missed Harm and False Flags in English, Ukrainian and Surzhyk

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27178)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`内容安全 API`、`独立评测`、`假阳性`

👤 **作者**：Andrii Tuzhykov

- 🎯 **研究动机**：托管年轻用户的平台用商用文本安全 API 筛查消息，但其在英语之外的漏报与误报率缺乏独立证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：构建 SITO 基准：英语/乌克兰语/混语 2332 条合成案例，六类伤害各配同题无害硬负例与最小编辑对照，实测 Tuteliq Detect API
- 📌 **结论**：英语伤害召回 84.9-94.1%、无害误报 0-4.3%；第三方伤害报告的漏检率远高于施害者消息（63.6% vs 95.1% 召回），3.2% 案例重复运行判定不同，平台应自测假阳性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Platforms that host young users screen messages with commercial text-safety APIs, but independent evidence on how often such systems miss harm or flag harmless text is scarce, especially outside English, and the documentation of the one studied here leaves its false-positive rate unquantified. We evaluated one such API, Tuteliq Detect as deployed under the release identifier tuteliq-detect-v5.20261011 (not the separately published v6 model), on six harm categories: authorised push-payment fraud, money-mule recruitment, social engineering, romance scams, gambling harm and bullying. We built SITO (Synthetic Independent Text-safety benchmark with Overflagging tests; sito is Ukrainian for 'sieve'), 2,332 synthetic cases in English, Ukrainian and mixed Ukrainian, with each harmful case matched by hard harmless ones: same-topic messages, minimal-edit contrasts, warnings and retrospective accounts. On English text the system flagged 84.9-94.1% of harmful cases and 0-4.3% of harmless ones per category. It missed third-party reports of ongoing harm far more often than perpetrators' messages (63.6% against 95.1% recall, all languages), when sent without the settings the API offers for reports of harm. Ukrainian and mixed-language input was detected at least as often as English, recall was lower on text from a held-out generator family, so headline recall may be optimistic, and 3.2% of cases received a different decision in repeated runs. A platform should measure false positives on its own traffic and check how bystander reports are handled. The datasets, generation prompts and code are released.

</details>

### 59. The Culture Within: Towards Mechanistic Understanding of Cultural Reasoning in LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27202)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`文化推理`、`机制分析`、`检索可信度`

👤 **作者**：Samee Arif、…、Rada Mihalcea

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 跨文化部署但评估多停留在输出层，需要机制层面理解文化信息的使用
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CultureLens 覆盖 75 国 9261 场景，系统变化国家、价值、真实规范与矛盾规范证据；机制上阻断模型内规范注入测试
- 📌 **结论**：矛盾规范使对齐答案下降 32-47 点，失败非知识缺失（阻断后多数恢复）；模型按呈现方式而非真假接受规范，最易被检索内容误导，SFT 可提升至 85-93%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate across culturally diverse settings, yet existing evaluations largely measure cultural behavior at the output level. We introduce CultureLens, a reference-grounded benchmark and mechanistic study of how LLMs use cultural information. CultureLens contains 9,261 scenarios across 75 countries and systematically varies country, value, true-norm, and contradictory-norm evidence. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-3-12B, and Qwen-3.5-27B, stating a contradictory norm lowers aligned answers by 32-47 percentage points, even when the model answers correctly from the country alone. Mechanistically, models default to Anglophone norms when no country is given. The failure under a contradictory norm is not one of missing knowledge, as blocking the norm inside the model restores the correct answer in most cases. Models accept a stated norm according to how it is presented and not whether it is true, which leaves them most exposed to errors in retrieved content. Supervised fine-tuning with contradictory-norm examples raises aligned answers under a contradictory norm to 85-93%, including on unseen countries, at a small cost under a true norm.

</details>

### 60. When Models Know but Can't Linearly Say: Relational Encoding Limits Linear Vulnerability Classification in Code LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27257)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`代码漏洞检测`、`线性探针`、`关系编码`

👤 **作者**：Rui Melo、Andre Catarino、Claudia Mamede、Corina Pasareanu、Rui Abreu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：代码 LLM 能检测漏洞，但漏洞信息在表征中的编码形式不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：对 8 个代码模型（6.7B-15B）在三个数据集上比较同表征的两种诊断：代码实例化模式识别与漏洞判定
- 📌 **结论**：漏洞信息更易作为成对关系信号（A 是否比 B 更危险）获取而非全局可分标签，表征空间中安全与漏洞样本分布重叠，解释了单样本分类器的精度局限

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models can detect vulnerable code, yet the form of vulnerability encoding in their representations remains unclear. We analyze 8 code models spanning 6.7B-15B parameters on C programs using three datasets (DELTASECOMMITS, SVEN, and PRECISE-BUGS), comparing two diagnostic tasks on the same representations: identifying what patterns the code instantiates, and determining whether it is vulnerable. Models encode the first with high fidelity. Under the studied paired C-code datasets and linear-probing setup, vulnerability information is more accessible as a paired relational signal-whether code A is riskier than code B-than as a globally separable categorical label. This structural difference has implications for classification: the model reliably ranks which code is riskier but standard classifiers on isolated samples achieve limited accuracy because vulnerable and secure samples form overlapping distributions in representation space.

</details>

### 61. Auditing Safety Preference Supervision with Recorded Derivations

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27266)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`偏好标签审计`、`可复现推导`、`安全监督`

👤 **作者**：Chenhan Lyu、Amir Rahmani

- 🎯 **研究动机**：对齐训练所依赖的偏好标签，其背后的判断难以检查或验证
- 🔬 **研究方法**：符号偏好标注：预训练 LM 分类预定义属性、显式规则赋判定、固定判定排序决定偏好标签，每个标签的推导可由存储的属性赋值重算而无需重跑模型
- 📌 **结论**：200 提示中 192 个产出有效结构化输出（8 个失败全在自伤类），DPO 训练后良性拒答 40.82%、毒性拒答 84.98%，区分了标签可复现性与属性正确性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Preference labels align model training, but the judgments behind them are difficult to inspect or verify. In this paper, we examine how preference construction can be checked when intermediate decisions are recorded, and which of them remain uncertain. We present Symbolic Preference Labeling, a method in which a pretrained language model classifies predefined attributes of prompts and responses. Explicit rules assign response verdicts, and a fixed verdict ranking determines the preference label. Each preference label has a recorded derivation: the response verdicts and preference label can be recomputed from the stored attribute assignments, rules, and verdict ranking without rerunning the language model. On 200 prompts annotated by human raters, the language model produces valid structured outputs for 192 prompts. All eight parsing failures occur in the subset of self-harm prompts marked as harmful. Using 465 preference pairs, we train one policy model with direct preference optimization across five training seeds. Mean measured benign refusal is 40.82 percent on 1,319 benign prompts that resemble unsafe ones, and mean measured toxic refusal is 84.98 percent on 655 toxic prompts. This study distinguishes the reproducibility of preference labels from attribute correctness. The recorded derivations make preference construction open to inspection, while attribute correctness and semantic safety require separate validation.

</details>

### 62. African Languages AI Safety Readiness Report

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27269)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多语言安全`、`非洲语言`、`准备度报告`

👤 **作者**：Israel Abebe Azime、Jesujoba O. Alabi、Tajuddeen Gwadabe、David Ifeoluwa Adelani

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 安全研究与开发集中于高资源语言，非洲语言是否被充分代表缺乏系统评估
- 🔬 **研究方法**：从 AI 相关资源（含文本数据）、国家 AI 政策支持、既有基准表现与说话人口四因素评估 readiness 并分级
- 📌 **结论**：将非洲语言分为完全就绪/基本就绪/新兴/需基础支持四类，为安全基准纳入非洲语言与数据投资提供战略指引

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

With the widespread adoption of large language models (LLMs) across languages and domains, AI safety has emerged as a critical global concern. However, due to resource constraints, research and development efforts tend to prioritize a limited number of high-resource languages, leaving many others, especially those spoken in Africa, underrepresented. While existing research on language "resource-ness" has largely examined whether sufficient resources are available to develop language technologies, the question of whether languages are adequately represented in AI safety research has received attention only more recently. This report examines the readiness of African languages for inclusion in AI safety discussions, drawing on existing literature and data. It considers four factors: the availability of AI-relevant resources, including text data; national AI policy support; performance on established text benchmarks; and speaker population. Based on these factors, languages are grouped into four readiness categories: fully ready, mostly ready, emerging, and foundational support needed. This classification is intended to inform the inclusion of African languages in AI safety benchmarks, highlight varying levels of preparedness, and guide strategic investment in data development, policy, and community engagement for underrepresented languages.

</details>

### 63. What Would Removing This Corruption Change? Failure Attribution for Vision-Language-Action Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27277)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA 模型`、`失效归因`、`反事实估计`

👤 **作者**：Carson Sobolewski、Anjali Parashar、Fei Chen、Yingke Li、Chuchu Fan

- 🎯 **研究动机**：知道某视觉损坏通常有害不能确定移除它对当前回合是否有益，多种损坏共存时归因困难且配对 rollout 代价高
- 🔬 **研究方法**：因子化风险模型分别学习场景与损坏表示，从当前图像与任务指令经潜在干预估计移除收益，无需干净图像或额外反事实 rollout
- 📌 **结论**：OpenVLA 与 π0.5 仿真及真机上归因误差约为前沿 VLM 的 1/3，可适配执行上下文与损坏强度并用于在线失效监控

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models are sensitive to visual corruptions, but knowing that a corruption is typically harmful does not establish whether removing it would benefit the current episode. Furthermore, when several corruptions co-occur, deciding what to correct requires understanding which ones contribute to failure risk and how much their removal would help. We define this problem as failure attribution. Detecting corruption presence alone cannot answer this question, as corruptions can be present but relatively harmless, while directly measuring removal benefits requires costly paired policy rollouts with and without the corruption present. To address this, we introduce a factorized risk model that learns separate representations of the scene and corruptions, allowing us to estimate removal benefits through latent intervention from the current image and task instruction, without paired clean images or additional counterfactual rollouts. We evaluate our approach on OpenVLA and $\pi_{0.5}$ in simulation and $\pi_{0.5}$-DROID on a Franka robot. Our method outperforms monolithic and vision-language-model (VLM) baselines, including achieving approximately 3x lower attribution error than frontier VLMs. We also demonstrate our method adapts to execution context and corruption intensity, and can be used for online failure monitoring on hardware.

</details>

### 64. Vulnerability Analysis for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27293)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`安全 MARL`、`推理时攻击`、`隐匿攻击`

👤 **作者**：Taozheng Zhu、Shixiong Jiang、Fanxin Kong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：安全多智能体强化学习专注优化期安全，其推理时脆弱性基本未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出 MinMax 攻击：联合选择被攻陷 agent 的对抗目标动作并建模良性 agent 的协同抵抗，推导安全代价与攻陷预算、扰动预算的关系
- 📌 **结论**：多个安全关键多智能体基准与 SOTA SMARL 算法上诱导显著更高安全代价同时保持任务奖励，降低可检测性

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (SMARL) aims to learn cooperative control policies under prescribed safety constraints. Al- though existing SMARL methods focus on enforcing safety during policy optimization, their vulnerability remains largely unexplored. This paper investigates inference-time observation attacks against SMARL, where an adversary perturbs the observations of a subset of agents, thereby altering the actions produced by their policies. We propose MinMax Attack, an attack framework that jointly selects adversarial target actions for compromised agents while accounting for the Cooperative Resistance of benign agents. We further derive a bound on the induced safety cost and characterize its relation- ship with the Compromised-Agent Budget and the Perturbation Budget. Experiments across multiple safety-critical multi-agent benchmarks and representative state-of-the-art SMARL algorithms show that MinMax Attack induces substantially higher safety costs than existing attack baselines while preserving task reward, thereby reducing its detectability and achieving both attack effectiveness and stealthiness.

</details>

### 65. Evaluating Context Compaction on the Questions That Come Later

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27301)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`上下文压缩`、`评测有效性`、`证据保持`

👤 **作者**：Junwei Yao、…、Wenbo Ye

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 为当前问题压缩上下文，但压缩后的上下文还要服务之后的问题，现有评测只看当题
- 🔬 **研究方法**：四个人工标注多问题证据语料与记录轨迹上，把每种方法与同预算随机删除比较，并用保留证据重新计分
- 📌 **结论**：为当题选句的方法在后续问题上不优于随机删除；按问题无关的词级删除（LLMLingua-2）反而更好，FEVER 上标签准确率会高估保留文本实际支持的内容

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model agents compact their context for the question at hand, but the compacted context must also serve questions that come later. We evaluate compaction on these later questions, using four corpora that annotate the evidence of several questions per text and recorded agent trajectories, and compare every method with random deletion at the same budget. On MultiRC, keeping the sentences that gpt-6-luna (through the OpenAI Decisions API) judges necessary for the current question raises its accuracy on that question by 11.4 points at 25% of the tokens. On the later questions, however, no method that selects sentences for the current question does detectably better than deleting sentences at random. Deleting words instead of sentences does better: LLMLingua-2, which ignores the question, beats random sentence deletion at the same token count by 12.5 points on the later MultiRC questions. It keeps about twice random deletion's share of the evidence's content words, and at equal content-word retention its gain shrinks to 7.3 points (exploratory). On FEVER, label accuracy can overstate what the kept text supports: the gain of sentence selection is no longer detected when answers are scored against the kept evidence, and the 21.0-point gain of LLMLingua-2 is not shown to rest on the kept text. The ranking also depends on the setting: on Evidence Inference sentence selection beats LLMLingua-2 at a nominal 25% budget, and on $\tau$-bench trajectories, whose later tool calls need exact identifiers, it beats random deletion while LLMLingua-2 shows no detected gain. Scores on the question a compaction was made for do not reliably show how it serves later ones.

</details>

### 66. Steering Vision-Language Models for Visual-Contextual Privacy in Location Disclosure

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27323)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`视觉隐私`、`激活转向`、`粒度控制`

👤 **作者**：Wenyue Chen、…、Yiran Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLM 能从细微视觉线索推断地理位置，回应披露超出视觉上下文适当粒度构成隐私风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证明上下文适当粒度可从生成前隐藏状态恢复且沿共同方向有序；提出推理时路由-转向框架：序数粒度路由器映射隐私表示到披露路线，路线特定隐藏状态干预调控解码
- 📌 **结论**：四个 VLM 骨干改善视觉上下文感知披露对齐、平衡过度与不足披露，通用能力基准仅轻微变化并可跨数据分布迁移

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language models (VLMs) can infer sensitive information such as geolocation from subtle visual cues, creating privacy risks when responses disclose more detail than the visual context warrants. We study visual-contextual privacy in geolocation disclosure, where a VLM should reveal location information only at the granularity appropriate to the visual context depicted in the image. We first show that this contextually appropriate granularity is recoverable from pre-generation hidden states, even when generated responses are misaligned, with disclosure levels exhibiting an ordered structure along a common hidden-state direction. Motivated by this, we propose an inference-time route-and-steer framework: an ordinal granularity router maps visual-contextual privacy representations to a disclosure route, and a disclosure-level-conditioned steering module applies route-specific hidden-state interventions during decoding. Across four VLM backbones, our method improves visual-context-aware disclosure alignment, better balances over- and under-disclosure, and incurs only modest changes on general capability benchmarks. It further transfers to another dataset with a distinct data distribution and privacy-risk taxonomy.

</details>

### 67. On the Limits of Scale: Inverse Scaling in Vision-Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27360)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`逆标度律`、`视觉语言模型`、`子任务失效`

👤 **作者**：Rohit Saxena、Alessandro Suglia、Marek Rei、Pasquale Minervini

- 🎯 **研究动机**：标度律暗示规模单调改进，但 VLM 可能在子任务级出现越大越差
- 🔬 **研究方法**：19 个模型×4 家族×8 个多模态基准共 55.8 万次模型-问题评估，做逐问题成对分析识别逆标度组合
- 📌 **结论**：识别 375 个子任务-模型对逆标度（4342 条小模型对大模型错的查询），跨家族复制说明任务驱动；多图空间推理、视觉感知与大学 STEM 最严重，差距超 20 点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Scaling laws suggest that increasing model size, training data, and compute generally yields monotonic performance improvements. While this often holds at the aggregate level, we present systematic evidence that vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit inverse scaling at the subtask level: specific question categories on which larger models perform worse than smaller counterparts within the same model family. We evaluate 19 models from four distinct model families across eight multimodal benchmarks (nine evaluation splits), answering 29,386 questions per model, 558,334 model-question evaluations in total. Using per-question pairwise analysis across all model pairs in each family, we identify 375 subtask-model-pair combinations exhibiting inverse scaling, spanning 4,342 unique queries where a smaller model answers correctly but a larger model fails, and we find that these patterns replicate across independently developed architectures. We identify 13 subtask-dataset pairs that show inverse scaling across multiple families, consistent with the phenomenon being task-driven rather than model-specific. The most affected domains in our study include multi-image spatial reasoning, visual perception, and university-level STEM knowledge, with accuracy gaps exceeding 20 percentage points in several cases, while diagram understanding appears comparatively resistant. These findings challenge the assumption that increasing model size uniformly improves multimodal capabilities and suggest that model selection should be task-aware.

</details>

### 68. Correcting Attribution Patching with a Few Exact Measurements

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27393)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`归因补丁`、`精确测量校正`、`机制可解释性`

👤 **作者**：Luyang Zhang、Minxing Zheng、Jialu Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：归因补丁用一次梯度估计激活补丁效果但误差可能很大，逐个验证又恢复成本
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Guided-AP 利用误差结构：单提示内误差多共享符号，少量精确测量可得统一校正；跨提示时组件被相似误估，几个测量提示即知该验证什么并校正平均效应
- 📌 **结论**：单提示最多降 26% 误差，多提示下事实补全平均效应误差降 75-79% 且成本在长分析中回本

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Mechanistic interpretability asks which language model components matter for a behavior, and activation patching answers by changing one component's activation and measuring its effect, at a cost growing with components and prompts. Attribution patching estimates these effects from one gradient pass, but its estimates can be far off, and verifying each one restores the cost. Can the structure of these errors reduce this cost? We propose Guided-AP, which uses a few exact measurements to reveal attribution patching's errors and correct unmeasured estimates. On a single prompt, many components' errors share one sign, so a small exact sample yields one correction for the rest. Over many prompts of one task, components are misestimated similarly, so a few measured prompts show what to verify and how to correct each component's average effect. Our analysis shows that choosing what to verify needs error size, while correction also needs sign. On a single prompt, the correction lowers error by up to 26% across three behaviors at the compute of verifying the largest estimates and also on larger models. Over many prompts, learned corrections cut the error of average effects on factual completion by 75% to 79%, repaying their cost in long analyses.

</details>

### 69. More Memory, Worse Agents: Error Reproduction and Anti-Persistence in LLM Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27406)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent 记忆`、`错误复现`、`反持久化`

👤 **作者**：Ruhan Wang、Kishan Panaganti、Dongruo Zhou

- 🎯 **研究动机**：agent 从自身执行中存储抽象经验再利用，但存储抽象中的错误会经检索复用并塑造后续轨迹
- 🔬 **研究方法**：定义错误复现机制：错误经抽象-执行-再抽象循环再生；提出反持久化记忆设计：只积累轨迹与结果、按需重建任务指导并在执行后丢弃
- 📌 **结论**：跨基准与骨干取得更高 held-out 成功率且 LLM 调用成本最低，支持把轨迹留存与抽象重建分离的记忆设计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLM agents can improve across tasks without updating model parameters by storing experience from their own executions in external memory and reusing it to guide later decisions. A central design question is whether to retain only observed trajectories and outcomes or also the rules, summaries, and skills inferred from them. Retaining these abstractions allows agents to reuse strategies without repeatedly deriving them from raw trajectories. However, errors in stored abstractions can propagate to subsequent decisions through retrieval and reuse. They can also shape execution trajectories, which then become the basis for new abstractions that reproduce the original errors. We term this feedback mechanism error reproduction: errors are regenerated through a cycle of abstraction, execution, and re-abstraction, extending their persistence beyond direct reuse of the original faulty knowledge. We propose Anti-Persistence, a memory design that accumulates trajectories and outcomes while reconstructing task-specific guidance on demand. An adaptive selector uses prior outcomes to decide whether and how to synthesize guidance, which is discarded after execution. This prevents its direct reuse as a stored object, although indirect feedback through retained trajectories remains possible. Across different benchmarks and backbones, Anti-Persistence achieves higher overall held-out success and the lowest average LLM-call cost among the evaluated memory designs. These results support separating experience accumulation from abstraction persistence in memorybased self-improvement.

</details>

### 70. Decision Sufficiency of Learned Representation Interfaces for Safe Generative Control

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27446)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`决策充分性`、`SAE 特征审计`、`安全生成控制`

👤 **作者**：Jipeng Li、Yanning Shen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：控制器靠选定内部特征决定何时协助/澄清/拒绝，性能差无法区分信息缺失与决策规则缺陷，需要判定表征是否决策充分
- 🔬 **研究方法**：定义决策充分性：同一策略与评价下使用该表征的最优控制器能否达到全状态信息的最小平均损失；精确计算最优损失并分离三类超额损失来源
- 📌 **结论**：审计 Qwen2.5/SmolLM2/Phi 的 SAE 特征：SmolLM2 上仅两个开关特征即使政策已知也使最小平均损失增加 0.0345-0.1166，两个独立训练的自编码器一致

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A controller can guide a language model toward safe and useful responses by choosing when to assist, ask for clarification, or refuse. When the controller uses selected internal features, poor performance alone cannot distinguish missing information from limitations of its decision rules or training. We study decision sufficiency: whether the best controller using a representation can match the minimum average loss attainable with full state information, under the same strategies and evaluation criteria. For interfaces with finitely many outputs, we compute the optimal loss exactly and separate the three sources of excess loss. Pairwise checks can miss information loss: states with the same controller input may share an optimal strategy in every pair but not across the whole group. We bound the number of states needed to reveal a conflict and characterize the fewest outputs an unrestricted interface needs to avoid all conflicts. We audit selected features from sparse autoencoders, which encode model activations using relatively few active features, in Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, and Phi. On SmolLM2 responses scored by two human raters, restricting the controller to two selected on/off features increases its minimum average loss by 0.0345--0.1166 relative to full state information, even with the governing policy supplied. Both independently trained autoencoders show the increase on both evaluation sets. The audit identifies decision failures that require changing the controller's input, not merely its architecture or training.

</details>

### 71. Detecting Errors in Large Language Model Responses: The Incremental Predictive Value of Hidden States

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27455)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`隐藏状态`、`错误检测`、`增量价值`

👤 **作者**：Ningzhi Li、Xiaojun Ma、Xiaofei Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：内部状态被期待为幻觉检测提供更丰富信号，但研究常只报独立判别性能且输出侧基线较弱
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把推理时可得的输出侧信息整合进组合基线，在相同数据划分与规则下比较加入隐藏状态前后的检测表现
- 📌 **结论**：两类任务 AUROC 仅提升约 0.004/0.039：隐藏状态确有增量信息，但强独立性能不等于显著边际增益

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Although large language models support a wide range of tasks, hallucinations remain a major obstacle to user trust. When a model generates an answer, both its internal processing and final output vary with the question. Internal states are often expected to offer richer signals for hallucination detection. However, studies using these states typically report their standalone discrimination performance, while output-side comparisons often use simple baselines with weaker modeling capacity. We integrate output-side information available at inference time into a combined baseline and compare detection performance before and after adding hidden states under identical data splits and evaluation rules. Experiments cover open-ended generation and constrained multiple-choice tasks across different models. Adding hidden states improves AUROC by approximately 0.004 and 0.039 on the two tasks, respectively. Hidden states thus provide additional predictive information, but strong standalone detection performance can still yield only limited additional gains.

</details>

### 72. When Health Assistants Agree Too Readily: A Benchmark of Time-Critical Self-Diagnosis

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27518)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`谄媚`、`健康助手`、`配对评测`

👤 **作者**：Marilyn Zhang、Tianfeng Chen、Patricia M Pechter、Mark E. Whiting

- 🎯 **研究动机**：每天超 4000 万人向 ChatGPT 提健康问题，对用户自我诊断的不当迎合可能延误急症
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SelfDx 配对基准：100 对谨慎案例（严重替代可能仍在）与同意案例（用户证据排除替代），测 27 个前沿模型的恰当谨慎率并试提示与微调缓解
- 📌 **结论**：模型倾向不当同意用户自我诊断，最好模型时危案例恰当谨慎率仅 77.0%；提示提升谨慎却使恰当同意降 9-35%，128 例均衡 SFT 可同时改善两者

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Every day, more than 40 million people ask ChatGPT a health question, such as whether laptop posture explains a stiff neck and days of pounding headache. Posture is plausible, but meningitis is a time-critical possibility. We introduce SelfDx, a paired benchmark for whether models have the appropriate response behavior, a form of epistemic independence, in health questions where misplaced agreement with the user's self-diagnosis could delay urgent or emergency care. Each of 100 pairs contrasts a caution case, where a serious alternative remains possible, with an agreement case, where the user supplies evidence that rules out the alternative and it is safe for the model to agree. We find across 27 frontier models that they tend to inappropriately agree with the user's self-diagnosis in cases that require caution. Even the best current-generation model reaches only a 77.0% appropriate caution rate on time-critical condition cases. To address this gap, we experiment with three prompt variants that increase appropriate caution but reduce appropriate agreement by 9-35%, indicating excessive caution. In contrast, supervised fine-tuning on 128 balanced examples improves both rates together across three runs. We recommend post-training to improve appropriate caution without making models cautious on every case, with paired evaluation to verify both response behaviors.

</details>

### 73. KNOX: Hardware-assisted Regulatory Sandbox for Generative Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27543)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`监管沙盒`、`TEE`、`合规证明`

👤 **作者**：Vasisht Duddu、Adam Caulfield、Xi He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：法规要求 AI 模型部署前在监管沙盒中接受行为监测，但未给出技术机制；沙盒需同时满足合规、权重保密、可证明与可扩展
- 🔬 **研究方法**：KNOX 在 TEE 内运行 AI 负载：为给定提示导出经证明的策略清单（法规、预期工具调用、允许的系统调用），执行器动作前由经证明引用监视器比对，动作与裁决记入经证明执行日志
- 📌 **结论**：首个监管沙盒设计，在 agent 负载上演示合规检查与多方监管者可验证证据的兼顾

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Regulations require companies to run AI models in a regulatory sandbox, an isolated environment that enables monitoring model behavior by regulators, prior to deployment. But, they do not spell out the technical mechanisms to design such a sandbox. Ideally, a regulatory sandbox should show (i) compliance (checks against regulations, and deviations from intended behavior, i.e., unauthorized tool and system calls); (ii) confidentiality (protects model weights from regulators); (iii) attestability (produces tamper-resistant evidence of model behavior for monitoring); and (iv) scalability (evidence is verifiable by multiple regulators). Several prior works (information-flow control, prompt-injection defenses, control-flow integrity, and traditional sandboxing) address specific compliance aspects but lack confidentiality, attestability, and scalability. TEEs meet some of these requirements, but simply running models inside a TEE is insufficient as we need compliance mechanisms. We present KNOX, the first regulatory sandbox, built by running AI workloads inside TEEs. We demonstrate KNOX using agentic workloads. For compliance, we derive an attested policy manifest (with the applicable regulations, the expected tool calls, and the permitted system calls) for a given user prompt. Before an executor performs an action, an attested reference monitor compares it against the attested policy manifest and allows it only if it matches. Every action and verdict is recorded in an attested execution graph, enabling regulators to verify the model's execution and its compliance. We show that KNOX meets all requirements, and can check for compliance with regulations and deviations from intended behavior (e.g., due to prompt injection); and incurs low overhead (<1%).

</details>

### 74. When LLMs Manipulate: Measuring Behavioral, Social, and Temporal Effects

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27573)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`操纵行为`、`行为脆弱性`、`社会传播`

👤 **作者**：Sadia Asif、Israel Antonio Rosales Laguan、Shumaila Asif、Haris Khan、Muneeb Asif、Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 可经细微语言线索影响用户信任与决策，但操纵输出的行为效应缺乏量化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：基于 DarkPatterns-LLM 用操纵梯度下降生成针对权威/紧迫/社会证明偏见的受控刺激，120 名被试上用 MSIAN 建模跨用户影响与时间传播模型刻画持续性
- 📌 **结论**：操纵框架显著改变信任校准与依从（p<0.01）并跨社会情境传播，呼吁安全评测从模型输出扩展到可测行为后果

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) can influence user trust and decision-making through subtle linguistic cues, yet the behavioral effects of manipulative outputs remain poorly quantified. We introduce a behavioral computing framework for measuring \textit{behavioral vulnerability}, defined as users' susceptibility to manipulative AI responses. Building on DarkPatterns-LLM benchmark, we generate controlled manipulative stimuli using Manipulation Gradient Descent (MGD), targeting biases associated with authority, urgency, and social proof. We analyze participant responses using the Multi-Stakeholder Impact Assessment Network (MSIAN) to model cross-user influence and the Temporal Harm Propagation Model (THPM) to characterize the persistence of behavioral effects over time. In a study involving 120 participants, manipulative framing produced significant changes in trust calibration and compliance ($p < 0.01$), with effects also propagating across social contexts. These findings highlight the need to extend LLM safety evaluation beyond model outputs to measurable behavioral consequences and provide a computational framework for quantifying individual, social, and temporal risks arising from manipulative AI interactions.

</details>

### 75. ABench: Adversarily Benchmarking Large Language Models across Taxonomized Values

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27611)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`价值对齐`、`对抗评测`、`中文价值观`

👤 **作者**：Liping Yang、Yongqi Leng、Jingting Zheng、Yuqi Ren、Shaolin Zhu、Deyi Xiong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：现有对齐基准英语中心或止于判别任务，难以评测特定文化语境下的对抗诱导鲁棒性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ABench 以 158 个价值子类层级分类法手工构造 2895 道开放题，用角色扮演与逻辑陷阱探测对抗诱导下立场稳定性，配问题特定评分标准的加权自动评测
- 📌 **结论**：16 个主流 LLM 在精细价值对抗诱导下多数显著脆弱（低于 60 分），凸显文化特定的鲁棒对齐需求

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Ensuring the alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) with diverse cultural values is critical for their safe global deployment. However, existing alignment benchmarks are predominantly English-centric or confined to simple discriminative tasks. Moreover, they often fail to effectively evaluate model robustness against complex, adversarial inductions within specific cultural contexts. To address these limitations, we propose ABench, a fine-grained, handcrafted benchmark specifically tailored to taxonomized values in Chinese. Grounded in a rigorous hierarchical taxonomy comprising 158 derived value subcategories, ABench consists of 2,895 open-ended questions. These questions employ techniques such as role-playing and logical traps to probe the stability of models' stances in adversarially induced contexts. Furthermore, we propose a weighted automated evaluation framework that utilizes question-specific scoring criteria for nuanced assessment. Extensive experiments on 16 mainstream LLMs reveal that most models exhibit significant vulnerabilities when subjected to sophisticated adversary attacks on values (scoring below 60). This underscores the need for more robust, culturally specific alignment strategies. Our data will be publicly released to promote further research on taxonomized value alignment.

</details>

### 76. CUA-Recovery: Benchmarking Error Recovery in Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27645)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`计算机使用 agent`、`错误恢复`、`基准`

👤 **作者**：Xu Hu、…、Feng Chen

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长程计算机使用中未检出错误会静默腐蚀持久状态并级联失败，现有基准靠人工注入错误、偏离真实策略失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ReRail 从持久桌面环境合成长程任务，收集多策略经确认失败并转为验证恢复轨迹；CUA-Recovery 测试套 100 任务 2250 个错误状态
- 📌 **结论**：六 agent×六接管深度错误察觉率至多 44.0%、Pass@3 至多 25.5%，agent 既难识别继承错误也难完成恢复，微调后改善

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As computer-use agents (CUAs) move to long-horizon workflows spanning multiple applications, undetected errors can silently corrupt persistent state and cause cascading failures in subsequent actions. Reliable error detection and recovery have become critical bottlenecks for building robust CUAs. However, existing benchmarks focus primarily on end-to-end task performance, providing limited insight into whether agents can detect and recover from errors during execution. The few benchmarks that evaluate recovery rely largely on manually injected errors, which may not reflect the types or horizons of failures induced by real agent policies. We introduce CUA-Recovery, a benchmark comprising test and training suites across multiple desktop applications. We construct it using ReRail, a framework for synthesizing long-horizon tasks and trajectories in a persistent desktop environment. ReRail composes MyPCBench tasks into new long-horizon workflows with executable ground truth, collects verifier-confirmed failures from multiple policies, and turns them into verified recovery trajectories. CUA-Recovery comprises a human-verified test suite of 2,250 erroneous states over 100 tasks and a training suite of 8.6K trajectories over 377 tasks. Evaluation on the test suite shows that agents struggle both to recognize inherited errors and to complete the task. Across six agents and six takeover depths, Error Awareness Rate (EAR) reaches at most 44.0% and Pass@3 at most 25.5%. After fine-tuning Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on the training suite, its average EAR rises from 19.9% to 35.2% on held-out recovery states, and its Rubric Score goes from 5.0% to 16.1%. It also gains 15.3 points on MyPCBench and 10.8 points on Odysseys for Pass@3, showing that the training trajectories help both with recovery and with completing tasks from a clean start. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 77. Does Multi-Agent Collaboration Pay When Agents Serve Users? Testing Agent Scaling in Dual-Control Settings

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27655)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多智能体评测`、`双控设置`、`预注册研究`

👤 **作者**：Ethan Yang、Xiangcheng Shi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：能力饱和法则（单 agent 45% 交叉点）来自无用户参与的自主基准，在 agent 服务用户的双控场景是否成立未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：τ²-bench 航空/零售/电信三域，六模型×同模型构建的集中式 MAS vs 单 agent，约 7950 次模拟对话，预注册并公开变更日志，每回合记录完成词元账
- 📌 **结论**：固定模拟用户下 13 个有效格中 MAS 12 负 1 平（加权均值 -28 分，95%CI[-44,-12]），法则预测获益的两格 MAS 输 13/22 分，无预算上限时最多追平但耗 5.5 倍词元

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multi-agent systems (MAS) built from language models are deployed on the premise that collaboration beats a single-agent system (SAS), yet when it pays is unclear. The most systematic study to date gives a capability-saturation rule: coordination helps while the SAS is weak and hurts once it is strong, with the crossing near 45% single-agent accuracy. That rule was derived on autonomous benchmarks, where no user takes part. We test it where agents now work: in conversation with a user under a written policy, on $\tau^2$-bench, the dual-control benchmark. On its airline, retail and telecom domains we compare the stock SAS with a centralized MAS built from the same model for six models from three families, over about 7,950 simulated conversations, with one completion-token ledger per episode per system. The study was pre-registered with a public change log; the MAS is reported as built, with one validity bar unmet (its planner never saw the tool schemas). Under a fixed simulated user no cell shows a MAS benefit: the MAS is worse in 12 of the 13 valid cells and tied in the thirteenth, and the fitted gain is negative across the observed capability range (weighted mean -28 points, 95% CI [-44, -12]). On the two cells where the rule predicts a benefit, the MAS loses by 13 and 22 points, and without a budget ceiling it at best draws level at 5.5 times the SAS's token spend. The one gain appears on the cell nearest 45%, under a second simulator. Whether the gain declines with capability is unresolved (slope -0.41, 95% CI [-1.51, +0.68]), because model-by-domain effects dominate. The MAS does not detectably reduce wrongful database writes; it converts other failures into budget exhaustion and inaction. For agents that serve people, the SAS is the default; a multi-agent design must show compute-matched evidence from the setting itself. We release all trajectories, ledgers, prompts and code.

</details>

### 78. Reusing Assurance Evidence in AI Pipelines: Validity Conditions, a Fail-Closed Release Gate, and a Replay of 714 Public Data Version Control Pipelines

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27672)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`保障证据复用`、`发布门`、`失效封闭`

👤 **作者**：Atanu Mukhopadhyay、Shravya Reddy Nalla

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 管线每次变更后重跑全部治理控制代价高昂，复用旧结果只在依赖声明完备且发布门校验证据归属时安全
- 🔬 **研究方法**：显式定义签名证据记录对候选有效的条件与变更局部重执行达到同一发布决策的充分条件；Ed25519 参考门实现检查，回放 714 个公开 DVC 管线 38083 提交
- 📌 **结论**：参考门拦截 8/9 操纵场景（信任规划器者为 1）；按声明依赖复用可省 83.4% 阶段执行，但 3.3% 提交复用了已变更代码/配置（31% 管线受影响），评测阶段暴露最大，加静态导入闭包降至 0.4%

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Rerunning every governance control after every change to an artificial intelligence (AI) pipeline is expensive, so teams reuse earlier results. Reuse is safe only if each control's declared dependencies cover everything that can change its outcome, and only if the release gate checks that reused evidence describes the candidate being released. This article makes both conditions explicit and measures how often the first fails in practice. It defines when a signed evidence record is valid for a candidate and gives sufficient conditions under which change-local re-execution reaches the same release decision as rerunning every control. A reference gate implementing these checks with Ed25519 digital signatures blocks eight of nine manipulation scenarios, against one for a gate that trusts the planner and three for a gate that only verifies signatures; a dependency the policy never declared passes every gate. A replay of 38,083 commits from 714 public Data Version Control (DVC) pipelines measures that failure mode. Reusing stage results according to declared dependencies avoids 83.4% of stage executions, but in 3.3% of commits it reuses a stage whose imported code or referenced configuration changed, and 31% of pipelines are affected. A manual audit confirms the undeclared dependency in 36 of 40 sampled events. Evaluation stages, whose outputs are the evidence, are the most exposed. Adding each stage's static import closure lowers stale reuse to 0.4% while still avoiding 81.6% of executions; sampled shadow re-execution removes far less, and rerunning everything after unexplained changes avoids only 45.3%.

</details>

### 79. How Fine-Tuning Objectives Shape Layer-Wise Information in LLM Hallucination Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27711)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`幻觉检测`、`微调目标`、`层间信息`

👤 **作者**：Kaiyang Wan、Forrest Sheng Bao、Amin Ahmad、Yuxia Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：分类头微调与 LLM 裁判式微调在幻觉检测上表现差异的表征成因不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：静态与训练动态分析揭示 cls 学到判别方向形成双簇几何、s2s 依赖预训练语言建模头几乎不重组表征；发现 InfoWindow 窄带及上层平台
- 📌 **结论**：据此截断 InfoWindow 之上的层：Qwen3-4B/8B 上剪掉 31% 网络精度在种子噪声内不变、时延降 1.4 倍，深度比例可跨模型迁移

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Under matched conditions, classifier-head fine-tuning (\textsc{cls}) paradigm consistently outperforms the LLM-as-Judge sequence-to-sequence (\textsc{s2s}) on binary hallucination detection. We trace this gap to the representations induced by the two objectives. Static analysis shows that \textsc{cls} learns a task-specific discriminative direction, pushing hidden states into a clear two-cluster geometry with stronger class separation and linearly decodable label information. By contrast, \textsc{s2s} relies on the pretrained language-modeling head to make decisions. This minimizes the pressure to reorganize representations, leaving the hidden states much closer to the pretrained backbone. Our analysis also reveals that the \textsc{cls} signal saturates before the final layer, after which upper layers form a plateau of statistically interchangeable and largely redundant representations. However, endpoint redundancy alone does not show whether these layers are dispensable during training. Further training-dynamics analysis reveals 1) a lower pretrained substrate, 2) a narrow upper-middle \textbf{InfoWindow} where task information emerges, and 3) a later-synchronizing upper plateau that mirrors this signal. This mechanism motivates us to remove layers above the InfoWindow. Truncating 31\% of the network leaves accuracy unchanged within seed noise on Qwen3-4B and 8B and cuts latency by $1.4\times$. The same depth fraction, fixed before any analysis of the target models, transfers to Llama-3.2-3B and Mistral-7B-v0.3, and the layer structure behind it reappears there and on QNLI and SST-2. We hope this helps guide efficient task-specific LLM design.

</details>

### 80. Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring for Diffusion Concept Erasure

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27719)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`概念擦除`、`扩散模型`、`注意力锚定`

👤 **作者**：Nattanat Chatthee、Tagon Sompong、Ekapol Chuangsuwanich、Supasorn Suwajanakorn

- 🎯 **研究动机**：概念擦除应对隐私、有害内容与版权风险，但启发式强度缩放导致非目标生成质量退化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：PPAA 在交叉注意力空间用有界余弦相似约束做推-拉注意力锚定，以相对相似率取代绝对差实现跨层统一的自适应擦除阈值
- 📌 **结论**：名人、艺术风格、裸露与知识产权多类别上于 SD1.4 取得有利权衡并可迁移到 FLUX

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Rapid advances in diffusion models have raised concerns about privacy, harmful content, and copyright violations. Concept erasure methods aim to address these risks by removing undesirable concepts from pretrained models without full retraining. However, existing approaches often degrade non-target generation quality through heuristic strength scaling (e.g., classifier-free guidance or negative prompt scaling), leading to uncontrolled and excessive semantic drift. To address this, we propose Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring (PPAA), a mechanism in the cross-attention space that displaces target representations while anchoring the scale of erasure via bounded cosine-similarity constraints. By using a relative similarity ratio instead of an absolute difference, our adaptive threshold represents a meaningful percentage of semantic retention, facilitating a single value to be applied uniformly across layers. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple concept categories, including celebrities, artistic styles, nudity, and intellectual property. Our method achieves favorable trade-offs against prior approaches on Stable Diffusion v1.4 and demonstrates its applicability to modern architectures such as FLUX.

</details>

### 81. Make the Adversary Spend More Energy: Generative Deception against Language-Model Attackers in Critical Infrastructure

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27739)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`生成式欺骗`、`能耗不对称`、`关键基础设施`

👤 **作者**：Vlad Mihal、Hosam Alamleh、Alessandro Cantelli-Forti

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LM 攻击者探索目标消耗算力，网络欺骗可借机施加成本，但攻防交换是否利于防御者需双侧测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：330 场会话×八模型对×两攻击者画像×三反注入级别，双侧同 GPU 并在空闲之上测能耗
- 📌 **结论**：关思考时 48 配置中 41 个（排除攻击者诊断调用）攻防能耗比 95% 区间大于 1；词元比却在两倍内——词元计数掩盖能耗不对称；启用思考使防御方能耗增 8-17 倍，提出带私有物理状态的 SCADA 欺骗架构

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Language-model attackers consume computational resources as they explore and interact with a target, creating an opportunity for cyber deception to impose costs. Whether such engagements favor the defender requires measuring both sides. We evaluate an autonomous language-model attacker against a decoy that uses a defending language model to analyze attacker commands and select defensive responses. Across 330 sessions, eight model pairs, two attacker profiles and three counter-injection levels, both sides run on identical GPUs and their energy is measured above idle. With runtime thinking disabled, the attacker-to-defender energy ratio has a 95% interval above one in 41 of 48 configurations under an allocation excluding attacker diagnostic calls, and in all 48 when those calls are included. Token ratios remain within approximately a factor of two in configurations without generation-cap truncation, showing that token counts can obscure substantial energy asymmetry. Counter-injection increases attacker tokens by approximately 9% per level without a detectable pooled change in energy consumption. Enabling thinking on both sides increases defender energy by 8--17 times and reverses the exchange for one model pair in the original campaign. We use these findings to motivate a proposed SCADA deception architecture with private physical state per identified adversary and deterministic protocol and process emulation.

</details>

### 82. Detection of Memorization in Diffusion Models Through Thermodynamic Singularity

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27754)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`扩散模型记忆化`、`热力学奇异性`、`检测判据`

👤 **作者**：Iljung Kim、Kangwoo Park、Yung-Kyun Noh、Daniel D. Lee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：扩散模型的记忆化（反向过程学到单样本扩散而非密度）是关键失效，需要分析性刻画与检测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：解析刻画记忆化反向过程，提出检测相关热力学奇异性的判据
