- 📌 **结论**：在一系列促进/缓解记忆化的网络上可靠区分正常学习与记忆化，服务生成隐私风险检测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In diffusion models, proper distributional learning entails reversing a diffusion process over underlying density functions, whereas a key failure is memorization, in which the reverse process instead learns diffusion from individual training samples. We provide an analytical characterization of such memorized reverse processes, along with a criterion that detects the associated thermodynamic singularity. The proposed criterion reliably distinguishes between properly learned and memorizing networks across a range of neural network models designed to promote or mitigate memorization.

</details>

### 83. RITE: Evaluating End-to-End Resilience to Silent Tool Errors in E-Commerce Agentic Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27777)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`静默工具错误`、`agent 韧性`、`基准`

👤 **作者**：Wenshuo Zhang、…、Yifeng Rong

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工具调用预期输出在开发与执行间可能漂移，返回貌似正确但错误的值且无失败信号，会沿后续决策传播
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RITE 从工业工作流与生产日志构建 167 匹配单元 501 个静默错误/正确值/显式失败实例，冻结工具行为、错误注入固定前缀后让 agent 自由续跑；另设轻量 State Checker 干预
- 📌 **结论**：十个 LLM 上静默错误约使任务成功率减半且一致难于显式失败；识别、利用验证路径与整合证据构成逐级瓶颈

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Tool-using LLM agents increasingly answer e-commerce queries through multi-step tool interactions. The expected output of a tool call can shift between agent development and execution, allowing a plausible but incorrect value to be returned without an explicit failure signal. These silent errors may propagate through subsequent decisions into final answers. To study agents' resilience across this end-to-end process, we formulate Silent-error Resilience Task, an interactive task that evaluates whether they complete the original query after a silent error corrupts an intermediate state. We introduce RITE, a benchmark built from human-authored industrial workflows and production-log-derived inputs. To enable controlled and reproducible evaluation, we use a semantic error taxonomy to inject errors into fixed workflow prefixes, freeze recorded tool behavior, and let agents continue freely. It contains 167 matched units across nine e-commerce workflows, yielding 501 silent-error, correct-value, and explicit-failure instances. Across 10 recent LLMs, silent errors roughly halve task success and are consistently harder than explicit failures. Recognition, use of an available verification path, and integration of the resulting evidence form successive bottlenecks. We also propose a light State Checker as an intervention to test whether resilience can be improved.

</details>

### 84. Are Linguistic and Arithmetic Circuits Dissociated in LLMs?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27790)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`电路分离`、`语言学-算术`、`机制可解释性`

👤 **作者**：Riku Kisako、Tatsuki Kuribayashi、Ryohei Sasano

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 兼具语言知识与借助语言的任务能力，其电路是否像人脑一样分离未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受神经语言学分离研究启发，用受控材料与电路发现方法提取五个 LLM 的语言学与算术电路，检验重叠与因果效应
- 📌 **结论**：电路相似性主要由任务域（语言学 vs 算术）解释而非输入输出词表或句法相似，提示能力基模块化组织

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) exhibit both knowledge of language, rendered by, for example, grammatical judgment ability, and abilities to perform tasks through language, such as math-word problem solving. Motivated by neurolinguistic studies of their neural dissociation in human brains, in this study, we investigate the overlap of their circuits in large language models (LLMs). Specifically, we examine the overlap and causal effects between the circuits extracted for linguistic and arithmetic tasks, using controlled materials and circuit discovery methods from the mechanistic interpretability field. Our experiments with five LLMs show that circuit similarity is mostly explained by task domains (linguistic vs. arithmetic), rather than by their input and output vocabularies or by the syntactic similarities between natural-language sentences and arithmetic expressions. This finding suggests that LLMs may process linguistic and arithmetic information using largely separate neural circuits, highlighting an ability-based modular organization of circuits within these models.

</details>

### 85. RoboCasa-Shift: Stage-Aware Robustness Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Policies in Long-Horizon Household Manipulation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27861)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA 鲁棒性`、`阶段感知诊断`、`配对回放`

👤 **作者**：Haocheng Wang、…、Ben Fei

- 🎯 **研究动机**：VLA 鲁棒性被聚合成功率掩盖，看不出个体执行在分布偏移下如何变化及长程任务失败出现在何处
- 🔬 **研究方法**：RoboCasa-Shift：单因子受控偏移、共享设置的干净-扰动配对回放与仿真器接地的阶段感知诊断；GR00T N1.5 四长程任务×四扰动因子×三强度共 1040 次正式回放
- 📌 **结论**：鲁棒性随因子与任务大幅变化且非单调退化；相同端到端退化可对应不同执行深度画像（同 ΔSR=0.188 下首阶段失败率 19.0% vs 36.4%），应作执行多维属性评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) robustness is commonly summarized by aggregate task success, which obscures how individual executions change under distribution shifts and where failures emerge in long-horizon tasks. We introduce \textbf{RoboCasa-Shift}, a diagnostic robustness benchmark built on RoboCasa365 that combines controlled single-factor shifts, matched clean-perturbed rollouts in which each pair shares the same underlying settings and differs only in the target shift, and simulator-grounded stage-aware diagnostics. We evaluate GR00T N1.5 on four long-horizon tasks that pass a predefined clean-performance gate under four perturbation factors and three calibrated severity levels, including conditions that drive the evaluated policy to complete task failure, totaling 1,040 formal rollouts. Robustness responses vary substantially across factors and tasks, and increasing severity does not always produce monotonic degradation. For example, one camera condition yields $\Delta\mathrm{SR}=0$ despite 8 of 80 matched episodes changing outcome. Stage-aware analysis further shows that the same end-to-end degradation can correspond to different execution-depth profiles. Camera $L_2$ and Gaussian blur $L_1$ both yield $\Delta\mathrm{SR}=0.188$, yet their first-stage failure rates are 19.0\% and 36.4\%, respectively. Our findings suggest that VLA robustness should be viewed as a multi-dimensional property of execution rather than a single end-to-end performance measure, motivating evaluation protocols that jointly capture outcome degradation, paired outcome sensitivity, and failure progression. The code is publicly available at this https URL.

</details>

### 86. From Tokens to Files: Multi-Granularity Detection and Localization of AI-Involved Code

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27863)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AI 代码检测`、`多粒度定位`、`混合署名`

👤 **作者**：Yue Xu、Xiyang Wang、Hui Liu、Bolei He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：需区分纯人写代码与 AI 参与代码（文件中任何部分由模型生成或大幅修改）并定位可归因行块
- 🔬 **研究方法**：冻结代码 LM 配两个低秩适配器分别在人写/机写文件上训练，词元对数似然比作局部证据，轻量头在行块级聚合上下文后汇总为文件判定
- 📌 **结论**：11773 个 AI 参与文件×六生成机制对 2541 人写文件达 72.4 macro F1（1% FPR 工作点），零适配在外部 Human-AI 共写基准上 62.5 行级 F1

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The increasing use of large language models for code generation calls for methods that distinguish purely human-written code from AI-involved code: files in which any part, however small, was generated or substantially modified by a model. Beyond flagging such files, we aim to localize the lines or blocks attributable to AI. Existing approaches compress a file into a single score, from surface stylistic features or a global code representation, which averages away the evidence when only a few blocks are AI-involved. We propose a framework that models AI authorship at the token level and predicts at the line, block, and file levels. A frozen code language model is equipped with two low-rank adapters, trained separately on machine-written and human-written files; their per-token log-likelihood ratio provides local evidence of AI authorship. Lightweight heads aggregate this evidence within lines and blocks, using the surrounding code context, and their local predictions are aggregated into a file-level decision. We train on mixed-authorship files built from human originals and their AI-generated or AI-modified counterparts, including continuation and fill-in-the-middle scenarios. On an internal test set of 11,773 AI-involved files across six generation regimes against 2,541 human files, our method reaches 72.4 macro F1 at a 1% FPR operating point. With no adaptation, it reaches 62.5 line-level F1 on HybridCodeAuthorship, an external human-AI co-writing benchmark with no training split, above the 56 its own paper reports as the best line-level result. On 19,445 human-written files from repositories created before 2022, the false-positive rate stays between 0.55% and 0.81% under the transported 1% threshold, so the operating point holds on real human code.

</details>

### 87. Elicitation or Expansion? Demystifying Gains from Agent Harness Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27889)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`harness 优化`、`引出vs扩展`、`pass@k 分析`

👤 **作者**：Younghyun Kim、…、Wonjun Kang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：harness 优化在不动权重下带来大幅 agent 增益，但朴素成功率无法区分是引出已达能力还是扩展可解任务边界——该区分对 RSI 与演化搜索至关重要
- 🔬 **研究方法**：借 RL 的 pass@k 分析比较优化前后 harness：可达任务覆盖变化、与模型放大及推理时算力的对比、增益随难度的分布
- 📌 **结论**：静态与动态任务上 harness 的 pass@1 优势在大尝试预算下可反转、聚合增益掩盖难题短板，需细粒度分析界定优化收益

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent studies in harness optimization have shown substantial performance gains in language model agents without updating model weights. A well-designed harness more effectively leverages the underlying model's capabilities by shaping context, tool use, and execution workflows. However, naïve success rates alone do not fully characterize whether optimized harness yields more reliable success on tasks already within reach (elicitation) or a broader range of solvable tasks (expansion). This distinction is crucial for recursive self-improvement and evolutionary search, as expansion may provide a broader range of successful experiences to push the capability boundaries. Despite the growing significance, this distinction remains underexplored in harness optimization. We conduct a systematic analysis to study the effect of gains from harness optimization. Building on pass@k analyses in the reinforcement learning (RL) literature that compare models before and after RL training, we compare optimized harnesses with their pre-optimization counterparts. Specifically, we assess changes in reachable task coverage, compare harness optimization with model scaling and increased inference-time compute, and examine how gains vary with task difficulty. Across static prediction and dynamic tasks, we find that pass@1 advantages of harness over stronger interventions can reverse at larger attempt budgets and aggregate gains conceal shortfalls on hard tasks, underscoring the need for a fine-grained diagnosis.

</details>

### 88. Lost in Recovery: Demystifying Recovery Behavior of Computer-Use Agents via Goal-Oriented Action Replay

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27954)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`计算机使用 agent`、`恢复行为`、`动作重放`

👤 **作者**：Byeonghun Bang、Sangwoo Park、Jinheon Baek、Kangsan Kim、Youngwan Lee、Sung Ju Hwang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长程 CUA 中错误累积且动作留副作用，环境重置不可行、动作重放过贵、API 回滚限 web，基于模型的恢复未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ReTrace 框架：形式化转移一致恢复（恢复态与目标态的相同后续动作须同果），经受控错误注入后的目标导向动作重放运行
- 📌 **结论**：首个系统性自主恢复研究：理想恢复可解锁潜在完成能力，当前 CUA 未能利用之；准确的模型级恢复带来可测端到端收益

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent advances in large (vision) language models have enabled computer-use agents that automate digital workflows via fine-grained keyboard and mouse operations. However, such agents remain fragile in long-horizon settings, where errors accumulate and executed actions leave unintended side effects, making the ability to recover from failures critical. Yet, existing remedies remain inadequate: environment resets are infeasible in proprietary settings, action replay is prohibitively expensive over long horizons, and API-based rollback is limited to web environments, leaving model-based recovery largely unexplored. To address this gap, we present ReTrace, a scalable evaluation framework for systematically studying recovery behavior in computer-use agents. We formalize Transition-Consistent Recovery: identical downstream actions from the recovered and target states must yield identical outcomes. We operationalize this criterion through goal-oriented action replay following controlled error injection. Using ReTrace, we present the first systematic study of autonomous, model-based recovery, revealing that (i) ideal recovery can unlock model's latent task completion capabilities, (ii) current computer-use agents fail to exploit their latent recovery capabilities, and (iii) accurate model-based recovery yields measurable end-to-end gains. Together, these findings establish recovery as a fundamental capability for building reliable computer-use agents. Our code and dataset is available at this https URL.

</details>

### 89. Beyond the Frame: Redundancy, Not Trajectory, Carries the Privacy Leak in Video Unlearning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27982)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`视频遗忘`、`隐私泄露`、`跨帧冗余`

👤 **作者**：Kavyansh Tyagi、Paolo Russo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：视频分割类遗忘以逐帧检查泄露会大幅低估：攻击者对两秒片段的输出场取平均即可恢复被遗忘类
- 🔬 **研究方法**：时间打乱对照证明可恢复信号来自跨帧冗余而非目标轨迹；提出 PACE：精确置换不变的池化对抗判别器加双侧时间一致性直接抑制冗余
- 📌 **结论**：VSPW/Virtual KITTI 2 上把池化攻击者 AP 压到 18.79/12.10/9.11，在保留可用效用的 verdict-safe 方法中最强抑制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Class unlearning for video segmentation aims to ensure that a removed category cannot be reconstructed from a deployed model's outputs. Current leakage evaluation algorithms inspect one frame at a time, and we show that this understates leakage by a wide margin: an attacker that averages a model's output field over a two-second clip recovers the forgotten class from every method we evaluate on VSPW and most on Virtual KITTI~2, including models whose per-frame predictions are visually indistinguishable from a retrain oracle (a model trained from scratch without the forgotten class). We attribute the recoverable signal to cross-frame redundancy rather than object trajectory, establish this with a time-shuffled control that leaves leakage intact, and introduce PACE, an unlearning method that suppresses redundancy directly using an exactly permutation-invariant pooled adversarial discriminator and a two-sided temporal consistency objective. Across VSPW and Virtual KITTI~2, PACE lowers pooled attacker AP to $18.79$ (\texttt{person}) and $12.10$ (\texttt{car}) on VSPW and $9.11$ (\texttt{car}) on Virtual KITTI~2, achieving on the latter the strongest suppression among verdict-safe methods retaining usable utility, while retaining $19.8$ mIoU on VSPW \texttt{car} against at most $8.3$ for any baseline achieving comparable erasure.

</details>

### 90. Contextual Candour: Role-Aware Graduated Disclosure for LLMs over Mixed-Sensitivity Contexts

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28067)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`分级披露`、`访问控制`、`信息泄露`

👤 **作者**：Devansh Srivastav、David Pape、Samira Abedini、Lea Schönherr

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 在混合敏度上下文上推理时须只披露用户有权获得的内容，现有访问控制是全答或全拒的二值决策，导致过度拒绝或意外泄露
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把分级披露建模为披露感知生成问题：奖励优化框架使回应向级别恰当的抽象塑形，惩罚含改写与近似在内的未授权事实泄露；企业与临床基准上对比提示、门控与监督基线
- 📌 **结论**：显著降低信息泄露同时保持正确性与效用，披露控制须内建于生成过程本身

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

LLMs increasingly operate over mixed-sensitivity contexts, where they must reason over information ranging from public to confidential while disclosing only what the user is authorised to receive. Existing approaches enforce access control as a binary decision: either fully answering or refusing, without regulating how much or what kind of information is revealed if access is granted, leading to over-refusal or unintended leakage. We introduce graduated disclosure, an access-control formulation in which the level of detail and sensitivity in a model's response is calibrated to the user's authorisation rather than treated as a grant-or-deny decision. We model graduated disclosure as a disclosure-aware generation problem and propose a reward-based optimisation framework that shapes responses toward level-appropriate abstractions while penalising unauthorised factual leakage, including paraphrases and approximations. We evaluate our approach on enterprise and clinical benchmarks and compare it with prompt-based, gating, and supervised baselines. Experiments show that our method substantially reduces information leakage while preserving correctness and utility. These results suggest that disclosure control must be integrated into the generation process itself to enable secure yet useful LLM deployments.

</details>

### 91. On the Vulnerabilities and Data-Free Removal of Watermarks in Batch Normalization Layers

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28076)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`水印移除`、`BN 层`、`免数据攻击`

👤 **作者**：Elena Rodríguez-Lois、Fernando Pérez-González

- 🎯 **研究动机**：BN 层是白盒水印常用嵌入点（FL 所有权验证与参与者识别），但其安全影响未被探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：识别 BN 水印方案的根基漏洞并提出免数据攻击，无需代表性训练数据即可移除水印
- 📌 **结论**：两个代表性 FL 水印方案上水印被一致移除且模型性能影响极小；强调 DNN 水印评测应遵循 Kerckhoffs 原则的威胁模型而非隐匿式安全

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Batch Normalization (BN) layers have become a common choice for embedding white-box watermarks in Federated Learning (FL), providing a simple and effective mechanism for model ownership verification and participant identification. However, previous works have focused on robustness against generic model modifications, leaving the security implications of this design largely unexplored. In this paper, we identify fundamental vulnerabilities of BN-based watermarking schemes and propose a practical data-free attack that exploits them. Experimental results on two representative FL watermarking schemes show that the proposed attack consistently removes the watermark with minimal impact on model performance, even without access to representative training data. More broadly, our findings highlight the importance of evaluating DNN watermarking schemes under threat models consistent with Kerckhoffs's principle, rather than relying on security through obscurity.

</details>

### 92. WildAIGC-Bench: Can AI Image Detectors Handle the Wild? A Benchmark of Generated and Edited Images in Online Circulation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28087)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`AIGC 检测`、`野外基准`、`在线流传`

👤 **作者**：Zijian Yu、Hangwei Zhang、Jun Lan、Huijia Zhu、Weiqiang Wang、Jianfu Zhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：关联新闻事件的 AI 生成图像可误导公众、助力欺诈，但检测器在保留压缩/截图/重拍等在线条件的真实表现未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WildAIGC-Bench 经新闻网站、事实核查源与社媒周期采集，407 张人审标签图像各带来源链接与证据摘要；评测专用检测器、多模态 LLM 与感知编码器基线
- 📌 **结论**：相对既有基准存在显著召回缺口，提供现实检测挑战与可持续扩充的溯源验证基础

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI-generated images linked to news events and online claims can mislead audiences, enable fraud, or cause public controversy. We introduce WildAIGC-Bench, built through recurring collection across news websites, fact-checking sources, and related social media content. The pipeline combines broad coverage, timely updates, and human-verified labels through scheduled discovery, automatic screening and preliminary annotation, and human review of image-specific evidence. The benchmark contains 407 verified AI-generated or edited images, each accompanied by a source webpage link and an evidence summary. Images retain the conditions encountered online, including compression, screenshots, and recapture. Evaluation of dedicated visual detectors, multimodal large language models, and a simple Perception Encoder baseline reveals substantial recall gaps relative to existing benchmarks. WildAIGC-Bench provides both a practical detection challenge and a basis for continued data expansion and source-based verification. The dataset is released at this https URL.

</details>

### 93. Attractive Faces Skew Text-to-Image Reward Models and Leaderboards

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28195)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`偏好模型偏差`、`人脸吸引力`、`排行榜失真`

👤 **作者**：Harvey Mannering、Zhiwu Huang、Adam Prügel-Bennett

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人类偏好现已主导文生图的评测、引导、微调与排名，其对生成图像中偏差的易感性未探
- 🔬 **研究方法**：受控用户研究证明吸引力压倒质量判断（真实无吸引力脸 vs AI 生成有吸引力脸）；编辑 40 个标注面部属性隔离测试五个偏好模型；模拟 Elo 与偏好对齐生成
- 📌 **结论**：吸引力是 PickScore/MPS/HPSv2.1/HPSv3 中四个的最强得分相关物，可反转排行榜；对齐生成使面部趋年轻女性化并可覆盖明确提示——偏好模型需解缠质量与吸引力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Human preference is now central to the evaluation, guidance, finetuning, and ranking of text-to-image models. However, the susceptibility of human preferences to bias in image generation remains underexplored. We identify a key observation: for human faces, perceived attractiveness overrides image quality in preference judgments, even when comparing real photographs of identical fidelity. In a controlled user study, annotators rank more attractive faces as higher quality, even when unattractive real faces are compared with attractive AI-generated ones. We examine the downstream consequences of this bias. Firstly, simulating Elo rankings from user study data shows attractiveness can invert a leaderboard's ranking, elevating synthetic attractive faces over real, unattractive ones. Secondly, the same bias reappears in the human preference models. Editing 40 annotated facial attributes in isolation, we find that attractiveness is the strongest correlate of score for four of five human preference models (PickScore, MPS, HPSv2.1, HPSv3), and the strongest predictor of score in regression analysis. We further show that preference aligned generation shifts faces toward younger, more feminine faces, and can override explicit prompts for unattractive faces, thereby limiting user control. Finally, we explore mitigation strategies, both for data collection and post training on human preference models. These findings highlight the need for preference models that disentangle image quality from human cognitive bias, enabling more reliable evaluation.

</details>

### 94. MaD-RSI: Model-as-Data for Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Harnesses

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28201)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RSI 方法`、`模型即数据`、`harness 优化`

👤 **作者**：Yu Cui、…、Cong Zuo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：harness 的 RSI 与目标 LLM 强耦合、难跨模型泛化且重训昂贵；模型差异通常只当事后泛化判据而非优化证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：MaD-RSI 把模型当数据、把 harness 放到优化中心：跨异质 LLM 直接优化共享 harness，把各模型失败轨迹的多样性纳入训练，并给出两个互补功效度量
- 📌 **结论**：跨模型知识共享提升 harness 泛化与优化效率，为 RSI 的评估与扩展提供新范式

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recursive self-improvement (RSI) of agent harnesses has emerged as an effective approach to enhancing the capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents. By searching the harness space for a specific LLM on a given dataset, RSI can discover a harness tailored to the capabilities and behavioral characteristics of that model. However, such optimization often creates a strong coupling between the learned harness and its target LLM, limiting generalization across models and requiring substantial computational resources to adapt to new LLMs. Moreover, existing RSI methods typically optimize a harness using feedback from a single model and evaluate its generalization to other models only after training. This paradigm treats model differences primarily as a criterion for evaluating generalization rather than as valuable evidence for optimization. In this paper, we introduce a new perspective that treats models as data and places the harness at the center of optimization. Building on this perspective, we propose \textsc{MaD-RSI}, a framework that directly optimizes a shared harness across multiple heterogeneous LLMs by incorporating their diverse failure trajectories into the training process. Rather than assessing model differences only after optimization, \textsc{MaD-RSI} leverages these differences as training evidence to guide the self-improvement of a shared harness. Furthermore, we introduce a new evaluation framework with two complementary metrics designed to assess the effectiveness of harness optimization across models. Experiments across multiple datasets and LLMs demonstrate that \textsc{MaD-RSI} improves generalization over existing approaches while reducing training costs.

</details>

### 95. Forty Questions: Identifying a Language Model from One-Word Answers

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28281)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`模型指纹`、`API 审计`、`一问一词`

👤 **作者**：Tapan Parikh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：API 背后的模型身份可通过其对日常问题的一词回答识别，为端点审计提供廉价手段
- 🔬 **研究方法**：构建 94 模型的答案画像（各问题给各答案的频率），按最大似然归属新答案；40 题即可 92.5% 精确识别（随机 1.1%）
- 📌 **结论**：OpenRouter 96 端点中 92 个自证与宣称一致；一个端点以 Llama 4 Maverick 名义供 Gemma 4 并按其计费，Grok 4.7 一行问题计 1247 输入词元——发现隐藏系统提示与套牌服务

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A model behind an API can be identified from its one-word answers to everyday questions such as "Name a sound" or "Name a poet". A model's profile records how often it gives each answer to each question. We attribute new answers to the model most likely to have given them. On a panel of 94 models, 40 answers identify the exact model 92.5% of the time, against 1.1% by chance. Profiles built through OpenRouter identified all ten models served by DeepInfra under blinded labels. A system prompt lowers accuracy by 6 to 16 points. We applied the method to 96 endpoints of 13 open models on OpenRouter, and 92 identify as the model they claim. Of the other four, one matches the claimed model with reasoning turned off. One serves Gemma 4 under the name Llama 4 Maverick: it names itself Gemma 4, and it is billed Gemma 4's token counts on every message. Two remain unresolved. Billing records also show context added to first-party models. Grok 4.7 is billed 1,247 input tokens for a one-line question that costs GPT-4o only 12 tokens, and five models state the current date without being billed for it. The questions come from a public census of model defaults, and the method costs cents per endpoint.

</details>

### 96. Every Model Leaves a Trace: Multi-Resolution LLM Fingerprinting

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28434)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM 指纹`、`多分辨率`、`蒸馏/LoRA辨析`

👤 **作者**：Mohammad Reza Modarres、Armin Tourajmehr、Artin Tavasoli、Yadollah Yaghoobzadeh、Mohammad Taher Pilehvar

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模型在生成文本中留下特异痕迹，但指纹能多精细地区分相近模型、明显线索减少后哪些信号仍有效不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：考察模型分辨率（跨家族→蒸馏→LoRA 适配）与信号分辨率（行为/文体/词汇/语义），逐步压制表层线索、改写输出、变化文本量并测跨提示分布与语言泛化
- 📌 **结论**：指纹在蒸馏模型乃至同底座 LoRA 适配上仍可检测；表层与词汇线索改变后归因减弱但大量模型特异信号存留，语义表征跨语言最稳健

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models leave model-specific traces in their generated text, but it remains unclear how finely these fingerprints can distinguish related models and which signals remain informative as obvious cues are reduced. We study this through fingerprint resolution, considering both model resolution, from different families to distilled and adapted variants, and signal resolution across behavioral, stylometric, lexical, and semantic evidence. We progressively suppress surface cues, paraphrase outputs, vary the amount of available text, and test generalization across prompt distributions and languages. Our results show that fingerprints persist beyond broad family differences, remaining detectable in distilled models and even LoRA adaptations of the same base model. Although attribution weakens as surface and lexical cues are altered, substantial model-specific signal remains. These signals transfer across prompt sources and languages, with semantic representations proving most robust across languages. These findings show that model identity is recoverable at multiple resolutions, but that recoverability depends on model similarity and the evidence available in the text.

</details>

### 97. Present but Hard to Read: Probing Latent Reasoning with Minimal Pairs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28498)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`潜在推理探针`、`最小对`、`预注册研究`

👤 **作者**：Aur Shalev Merin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：潜在推理探针研究常在答案起始处读状态，把探针失败当作数值缺席的证据——该推断未经验证
- 🔬 **研究方法**：用仅差一位数字的提示对照，在循环模型 Ouro-1.4B 与 Qwen2.5-1.5B 上测该数字在末提示词元的可读性；给出含合成空对照的协议区分计算值与输入副本/混合
- 📌 **结论**：数字确实存在（共享码读出约 0.9）但单提示探针几乎读不出（0.36/0.19）；预注册研究在 Ouro 写答案末位前找到 a+b 中间值的小信号但其符号部分可由混合产生，保持该值不移动所写数字——可读不等于被使用

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Probing studies of latent reasoning often read a model's state where the answer begins and take a failed probe as evidence that a value is absent. We test this with a digit copied from the question, in the looped model Ouro-1.4B and the standard transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B. Comparing prompts that differ only in that digit shows it is present at the last prompt token, in a code shared across questions (readout about 0.9). A probe that sees one prompt at a time, the usual setting, barely reads it: 0.36 in Ouro and 0.19 in Qwen even after 5,249 training questions. The digit's change is small rather than crowded: random noise lands on equally busy directions, while a random word in its place moves the state far more and is readable. Since every varied token leaves a trace, a successful probe is ambiguous too. We give a protocol, including a synthetic null for generic mixing, for testing computed values against copies and mixtures of the inputs. In pre-registered studies of two-step problems it finds a small signal for the unwritten intermediate a+b just before Ouro writes the answer's last digit, and its sign follows the operation, though mixing alone produces much of that sign-following part. Holding that value at another problem's from the first loop on does not move the written digit toward the other problem's, while the same patch of the answer does: the value is readable but not used.

</details>

### 98. DecisionPivot: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Decision Models in LLM Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28522)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`决策模型评测`、`对照组精度`、`校准`

👤 **作者**：Xialie Zhuang、…、Chuan-Wei Kuo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：决策模型支撑 LLM 系统的路由、护栏与输出验证，但条目精度不能证明跨受控变化决策正确或置信可靠
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DecisionPivot：48 任务 40033 条、七种决策角色；核心轨 13 任务族生成对照组成对且答案由独立求解器验证，评 40 决策模型与 14 通用模型的条目/对照精度、概率排序、校准与选择性覆盖
- 📌 **结论**：两个 27B 模型参考轨仅差 2.6 分但对照精度 70.1% vs 44.2%；Perplexity Decider 二元对照对排序正确 92.3% 而双题全对仅 74.1%——概率排序不等于决策正确

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Decision models support routing, guardrails, and output verification in LLM systems. However, item accuracy alone cannot establish whether models make correct decisions across controlled variations or assign reliable confidence to their predictions. We introduce DecisionPivot, a diagnostic benchmark comprising 48 tasks and 40,033 items across seven decision roles. Its core track contains 7,800 items from 13 generated task families, organized into contrast groups with computed answers verified by independently implemented solvers. The complementary reference track contains 32,233 items from 35 public test sets, preserving their original evaluation metrics. We evaluate 40 decision models and 14 general models using documented, model-specific protocols, assessing item and contrast-group accuracy, probability ordering, calibration, and empirical selective coverage. Our results reveal substantial differences obscured by aggregate scores: two 27B models separated by only 2.6 points on the reference track achieve contrast-group accuracies of 70.1% and 44.2%. Across 2,400 binary contrast pairs, Perplexity Decider correctly ranks the positive member above the negative member in 92.3% of pairs but answers both members correctly in only 74.1%, exposing a gap between probability ordering and decision correctness. Models with similar item accuracy also differ in the reliability of their high-confidence predictions. On a separate 1,296-item subset, GLM-5.3 achieves 93.5\% contrast-group accuracy at a higher reasoning setting, compared with 81.5% at its lowest effort. By linking errors to pivotal details and difficulty levels, DecisionPivot provides fine-grained diagnostics of decision failures that aggregate evaluation alone cannot reveal.

</details>

### 99. Humans Correct Their Judgments Through Debate, but Weaker AI Models Do Not

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28539)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`辩论监督`、`AI Safety via Debate`、`弱模型裁判`

👤 **作者**：Maria Victoria Carro、…、Maria Vanina Martinez

- 🎯 **研究动机**：辩论协议依赖裁判（人或弱模型）判胜负以监督超能 AI，但人类判断非中性且先进 AI 可能学会利用偏见
- 🔬 **研究方法**：多种协议配置评测辩论与咨询基线对人/LLM 裁判先验信念的修正，并考察法官数量与多样性、审议与投票等群体机制
- 📌 **结论**：辩论提升人类裁判相对先验的准确率而单专家咨询不提升；但弱 LLM 裁判多数设置无 comparable 收益甚至降低准确率，加权投票对人最优而对 LLM 无一群体机制显著改善

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

How can we effectively oversee AI systems that surpass human intelligence? AI Safety via Debate proposes that two AI systems argue opposing positions and a judge, either a human or a weaker model, decides which is correct. By facilitating supervisors in providing high-quality training signals, debate aims to encourage truthful and safe behavior from AI systems whose capabilities exceed our own. However, human judgment is not neutral: people often rely on biases which advanced AI systems may learn to exploit. Drawing on insights from collective intelligence and social cognition, this paper investigates whether debate can guide human and LLM supervisors toward the truth despite their prior beliefs. We evaluate several protocol configurations. We also explore setups inspired by the wisdom of the crowd such as the number and diversity of judges, as well as interaction mechanisms including deliberation and voting. Overall, we find that debate increases accuracy relative to prior beliefs for human judges, whereas the consultancy baseline, in which a single expert argues for one answer, does not. For weaker language models, however, the results are mixed: debate provides no comparable gains in most settings and can even reduce accuracy. Among multi-judge protocols, weighted voting produces the highest accuracy for human supervisors, whereas for LLMs none of them significantly improves group accuracy, even as the number of judges increases. Notably, the behavior of minority judges in non-diverse deliberation protocols suggests that, under symmetric settings, incorrect positions could be more persuasive than correct ones. These findings support debate as a viable path toward scalable human oversight, but cast doubt on its benefits for weaker AI supervisors, raising concerns about fully automated training and control pipelines in high-stakes settings.

</details>

## 扩展视野

### 100. Measuring Consensus in AI Agent Networks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24833)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多智能体系统`、`共识度量`、`协同决策`

👤 **作者**：Jin Gao

- 🎯 **研究动机**：联网 agent 群体需要协商与联合决策，但缺乏系统性评估其观点收敛与信息支撑的方法
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把共识形成建模为公开立场向共享区域演化的过程，跟踪位置轨迹、群体离散度与消息信息量，在 1872 次运行中考察成员变动与解释共享的影响
- 📌 **结论**：共享解释与更准确的群体决策相关，而在需调和重复信息与取消合同的任务上即使聚合答案正确仍存在持续分歧，框架将决策精度与有界分歧关联到可用信息

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

As AI agents become increasingly connected through networks and take on tasks that require negotiation and joint decision-making, a systematic approach is needed to assess how their views converge and what information supports their decisions. We present an evaluation framework that treats consensus formation as the evolution of publicly stated positions toward a shared region, using accumulated communication history as context. The framework tracks position trajectories, group dispersion, and information expressed in public messages. Two case studies examine staffing decisions based on distributed facts and decisions requiring agents to reconcile duplicate information and a cancelled contract. Results from 1,872 completed runs associate sharing explanations with more accurate group decisions, while the second task reveals persistent disagreement despite accurate aggregate answers. Membership-change experiments provide a complementary evaluation. By separating convergence among agents from progress toward a task's full-information answer, the framework relates decision accuracy and bounded disagreement to the information available during the exchange.

</details>

### 101. Evidence-Grounded LLM-Assisted Android Mobile Forensic Reporting

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26946)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM 取证`、`原子断言`、`证据引用`

👤 **作者**：Madhab Chandra Das、Sajid Ahmed、Tasnia Tabassum、Qingzhong Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 辅助取证报告的流畅输出不等于证据可靠性，需要原子断言级评估框架
- 🔬 **研究方法**：让 Gemini/Llama/Qwen 为两个受控案件生成报告，将 612 条断言对照冻结的人工审定权威做内容标注、引用校验、事实回忆与独立复核
- 📌 **结论**：公开案件三模型均恢复全部 27 条报告级事实，但引用有效率差异巨大（Llama 为 0）；零事实边界的实体设备案件三模型仍生成 65 条断言（38 条无支撑），证明事实回忆完整不等于断言有据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models can assist forensic report drafting, but fluent output does not ensure evidentiary reliability. This study presents an evidence-grounded framework for evaluating LLM-assisted Android forensic reports at the atomic-claim level. Gemini, Llama, and Qwen each generated reports for two controlled cases: a public Android 13 dataset and a physical LG device with a zero-reportable-fact boundary. The six accepted reports were decomposed into 612 claims and evaluated against frozen, human-reviewed authorities through content labeling, citation validation, fact-recall analysis, independent rechecking, and dispute resolution. The final corpus contained 424 supported, 163 unsupported, four contradicted, four overstated or incomplete, and 17 excluded claims. In the public case, all three models recovered all 27 authorized report-level facts. Citation validity nevertheless differed substantially: Gemini produced 168 valid citations from 189 required citations, Qwen produced 145 from 189, and Llama produced none from 135. In the physical device case, the models generated 65 claims despite the zero reportable-fact boundary, including 38 unsupported and one contradicted claim. These results show that evidence restriction and complete fact recall do not guarantee claim support or valid evidence attribution. The framework provides an auditable method for detecting unsupported content, preserving claim-to evidence lineage, and retaining investigator authority over final forensic reports.

</details>

### 102. SZED at ArGuard Shared Tasks: Multi-Task Label-Attention and Cross-Modal Fusion for Arabic Hateful Meme Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26972)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`仇恨模因检测`、`阿拉伯语`、`共享任务`

👤 **作者**：Selma Khelili、Zohra Abdeli

- 🎯 **研究动机**：阿拉伯语仇恨模因检测需要跨模态融合与细粒度多标签方案
- 🔬 **研究方法**：A1 融合 MARBERTv2 与 CLIP 嵌入比较拼接/自注意力/集成；A2 用多任务标签注意力与级联校准辅助头
- 📌 **结论**：A1 macro-F1 0.7597、A2 0.3142，报告了可复现性与长尾类错误模式

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We present SZED's submission to ArGuard 2026 Track A: Arabic hateful meme detection. The task has two parts: binary classification (A1) and fine-grained multi-label tagging (A2). For A1, we fuse MARBERTv2 and CLIP ViT-L/14 embeddings, comparing concatenation, self-attention, and ensemble heads. For A2, we use a multi-task label-attention architecture with per-class query vectors and an auxiliary head for cascade calibration. We reach macro-F1 = 0.7597 on A1 and 0.3142 on A2 (averaged over 10 active classes). We report reproducibility across runs, per-class error patterns, and the effect of cascade calibration on the long-tailed A2 label space.

</details>

### 103. TRACE: Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation for Text-Vision Embedding Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26993)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`嵌入模型`、`鲁棒性评测`、`训练感知`

👤 **作者**：Pume Tuchinda、Tinnakit Udsa、Peerat Limkonchotiwat

- 🎯 **研究动机**：文本-视觉嵌入模型的聚合分数掩盖了性能来自通用能力、任务适配还是困难任务鲁棒性
- 🔬 **研究方法**：将 168 个任务重组为通用/适配/挑战三类，对两个嵌入模型做受控训练实验
- 📌 **结论**：通用+适配数据提升适配表现 7 点以上且保持通用性能，但挑战类得分始终低于 5.0，多语言与视觉文档是主要弱点，数据适配不足以解决

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing benchmarks for text-vision embedding models report aggregate scores that obscure whether performance comes from general capabilities, task-specific adaptation, or robustness to challenging tasks. We introduce TRACE, a Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation framework that reorganizes 168 tasks from various sources of datasets into three categories: General, Adaptability, and Challenge. Through controlled experiments on two text-vision embedding models, we show that combining general and adaptation data improves Adaptability performance by 7.67 and 7.09 points, respectively, while largely preserving General performance. However, Challenge performance remains below 5.0, with limited improvements from additional training data. Further analysis identifies multilingual and visual-document tasks as major weaknesses. Our findings demonstrate that data adaptation alone is insufficient to address these challenges, highlighting the need for methodological advances beyond data-centric improvements.

</details>

### 104. How to get the best and fairest performance out of your ASR system using activation steering

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26994)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`激活转向`、`语音识别`、`人口公平`

👤 **作者**：Felix Herron、Alex Colagrande、Solange Rossato、François Portet、Alexandre Allauzen、Chuyuan Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：激活转向在 ASR 性能与人口公平性上的作用缺乏探索
- 🔬 **研究方法**：比较配对激活相减与监督优化两种转向向量形式，考察不同个人数据需求下的向量训练
- 📌 **结论**：监督优化导出的转向向量同时提升整体 ASR 性能与性别、年龄、方言公平；声学相似说话人训练的向量可零样本适配未见说话人

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Activation steering for ASR systems is an underexplored technique in improving both ASR performance and demographic fairness. We investigate two forms of activation steering: paired activation subtraction, and supervised optimization. We find the former to be ineffective at ameliorating ASR performance whatsoever, while the latter is quite effective at deriving steering vectors (SVs) that improve at once overall ASR performance and demographic fairness along such diverse axes as gender, age, and dialect. We also explore what types of data---from the most to the least personal---are necessary for calculating or training SVs. First, we show significant performance in performance from training SVs using an eclectic group of speakers from the same dataset. Furthermore, we find that vectors trained using acoustically similar speakers leads to ASR performances that are competitive or superior to vectors trained on the same speaker, or speakers from the same labeled demographic group. This finding shows that we can harness activation steering for few-shot adaptation given access to a speaker's demography, or zero-shot adaptation to unseen speakers without the need for any labels at all.

</details>

### 105. Fluent but Fallible: Benchmarking Hallucination and Factual Reliability in Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27034)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`幻觉评测`、`事实可靠性`、`基准`

👤 **作者**：Osama Shabih、Dayam Nadeem、Shahab Saquib Sohail、Mohammad Nadeem

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 的幻觉与响应偏离威胁学术场景可靠性，需要跨模型对比基准
- 🔬 **研究方法**：70 条四个复杂度等级的学术提示，十款模型由三位评审按准确率/精确率/召回/响应偏离指数/幻觉指数五指标独立评估
- 📌 **结论**：Perplexity 与 DeepSeek V3 综合准确率最高，LLaMA 3 以保守生成抗幻觉，高级提示对所有模型最难

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced natural language processing and artificial intelligence applications through their capabilities in reasoning, text generation and knowledge retrieval. However, LLMs are still vulnerable to two important challenges: hallucination, generating factually incorrect or fabricated content, and response deviation, meaning systematic deviations from ground-truth responses. These limitations raise serious concerns regarding reliability and trustworthiness in academic and research environments. This study offers a thorough comparative analysis using a variety of metrics of ten advanced LLMs ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude~3.5~Sonnet, LLaMA~3, Mistral, Grok, Perplexity, DeepSeek~V3, Falcon, and Gemma based on a benchmark consisting of 70 carefully designed academic prompts across four complexity levels: Basic, Explanation, Advanced, and Tricky, evaluated in February 2025 on specific model versions. Each response was independently evaluated by three reviewers using five metrics: Accuracy, Precision, Recall, Response Deviation Index, and Hallucination Index. Perplexity and DeepSeek~V3 achieved the highest overall accuracy, while Gemini demonstrated strong recall and a well-balanced performance profile across all five metrics. LLaMA~3 showed strong resistance against hallucinations through a conservative method of generation. Advanced-level prompts proved most challenging across all models, while Tricky prompts were handled more reliably owing to their binary true/false structure. Falcon achieved perfect precision and zero hallucinations, but at the cost of the lowest recall and the highest response deviation. The results show a fundamental trade-off between response completeness and factual reliability, intensified by prompt complexity.

</details>

### 106. Omni-Woodpecker: Hallucination Correction via Evidence-Prior Rebalancing for Omni LLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27103)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`全模态幻觉`、`表示再平衡`、`免训练`

👤 **作者**：Zijian Liu、Chaoyou Fu、Yan Yang、Caifeng Shan、Ran He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：全模态 LLM 的跨模态幻觉源于接地证据与学习先验在融合表示中的纠缠，输出级缓解不足
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Omni-Woodpecker 从模态特有视图识别跨模态冲突，用正交分解分离接地证据与竞争先验，解码前自适应再平衡融合表示
- 📌 **结论**：以 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 为骨干在 CMM/AVHBench 提升 12.9/8.6 点，跨架构泛化到 VideoLLaMA2-AV 且通用推理基准不受损

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent Omni LLMs (OLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in joint audio-visual reasoning, yet cross-modal hallucinations remain a major challenge. Existing mitigation methods mainly operate at the output level, overlooking the entanglement of grounding evidence and learned priors within fused representations. To address this issue, we propose Omni-Woodpecker (OWP), a training-free framework that shifts hallucination mitigation from output-level manipulation to conflict-conditioned representation rebalancing. Specifically, OWP first identifies cross-modal conflicts from complementary modality-specific views. It then employs orthogonal decomposition to separate grounding evidence from competing priors within the hidden states. Finally, OWP adaptively rebalances fused representations by suppressing misleading priors and restoring ignored evidence before decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and strong cross-architecture generalization of OWP. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the backbone, OWP improves performance by 12.9 and 8.6 points on CMM and AVHBench, respectively, while consistently achieving gains of 0.67-2.25 points across four general OLLM reasoning benchmarks. Furthermore, OWP generalizes effectively to VideoLLaMA2-AV, improving performance by 4.6 and 3.9 points on the same benchmarks. Code is publicly available at this https URL

</details>

### 107. GRID: Modeling Health Influencer Discourse as Argument Networks for Credibility Assessment

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27149)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`虚假信息`、`论证网络`、`可信度评估`

👤 **作者**：Nishanth Sridhar Nakshatri、Eylon Caplan、Dan Goldwasser

- 🎯 **研究动机**：健康类网红内容通常被孤立评估，忽略其所在话语网络
- 🔬 **研究方法**：GRID 把每个视频表示为连接断言与中心论点的论证图，跨视频关联断言与论点、接地科学证据，在网络上联合推断可信度
- 📌 **结论**：569 条网红视频上考虑话语网络改善内容验证，并揭示高低可信内容在论证策略与道德框架上的差异

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Health-related discussions on social media are increasingly shaped by influencers who offer diverse, and often competing perspectives on the same issue. Yet, such content is typically evaluated in isolation, without considering the broader discourse in which it appears. We introduce GRID (Grounded Reasoning over Interconnected Discourse), a framework for evaluating influencer content through collective inference over the surrounding discourse. GRID represents each video as an argumentation graph connecting claims to its central message, or takeaway, links related claims and takeaways across videos, grounds claims in scientific evidence, and jointly infers credibility over the resulting network. We evaluate GRID on an end-to-end health-related content validation task using 569 influencer videos grounded in expert assessments. Our results show that accounting for the broader discourse improves influencer content validation. Finally, we use GRID-inferred credibility to demonstrate how argumentative strategies and moral framing differ between low- and high-credibility content.

</details>

### 108. On the Fragility of Tasks under Composition: Implicit Budgeting in Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27190)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`任务组合`、`隐式预算`、`评测有效性`

👤 **作者**：Chenpan Li、Thies Brockmoeller、Stefanie Jegelka、Sharut Gupta、Nikolaos Karalias

- 🎯 **研究动机**：模型在单提示内组合多任务时即使能各自可靠求解也大幅出错，该退化无法由独立误差累积解释
- 🔬 **研究方法**：跨开源模型与 Claude Opus 5 测量组合/孤立条件下的每任务词元数与错误率，按脆弱性分组操纵隐式预算
- 📌 **结论**：组合时每任务词元显著减少，孤立需更长解迹的任务更脆弱；按脆弱性分组可在保精度下减少 43% 词元

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Modern language models are increasingly asked to handle several tasks within a single prompt. Doing so requires solving each task correctly while integrating the resulting answers into a coherent response. In this paper, we identify a fundamental limitation in this process, where models make substantially more errors when tasks are composed, even when they can solve every constituent task reliably in isolation. This degradation cannot be fully explained by the accumulation of independent errors. Across both open-weight models and the frontier model Claude Opus 5, our findings instead point to an implicit thinking budget underlying this effect. In particular, under composition, models use substantially fewer tokens per task than in isolation, even when the full sequence remains well within the context window. Tasks that require longer solution traces in isolation are consequently more $\textit{fragile}$ under composition, i.e., models make more errors on prompts containing these tasks. In isolation, non-fragile tasks receive more reasoning than they need and waste compute. However, by grouping tasks according to their fragility, the implicit budgeting can be manipulated to reduce token usage by $43$\% across a range of math and linguistic tasks while also preserving accuracy.

</details>

### 109. CARLAR Influence: A Structured Hallucination Risk in Multimodal Cheque Extraction and an Exploratory Compressed-Image Countermeasure

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27192)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多模态幻觉`、`金融文档抽取`、`置信度校准`

👤 **作者**：Joong Ho、Choi、Aditya Paul、Nidhi Singh

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多模态 LLM 在支票抽取中存在 CARLAR 影响这一结构性幻觉：两金额字段返回相同值
- 🔬 **研究方法**：十种视觉提示条件下评估 GPT-4.1 与 Claude 4.5 Sonnet 共 243 张主支票与 598 条严格不匹配，测试压缩图像少样本干预
- 📌 **结论**：遮蔽条件下 CARLAR 达 73.7%，预测一致时置信度仍高（ECE 达 0.93）；干预效果模型依赖，需考虑视觉上下文与字段依赖的验证方法

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multimodal large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for structured extraction from financial documents, but their behavior under visual ambiguity is poorly understood. We study CARLAR (Courtesy Amount Recognition and Legal Amount Recognition) influence: a model returns the same amount for both cheque fields even though their annotations differ, including cases with a blank annotation encoded as zero. Across ten visual prompting conditions, we evaluate GPT-4.1 and Claude 4.5 Sonnet on 243 primary cheques (124 with two observed unequal amounts) and 598 strict mismatches from two additional datasets. Under courtesy redaction, GPT-4.1 CARLAR reaches 73.7 percent (95 percent bootstrap interval: 68.3 to 79.0; p below 0.001 versus baseline). Model-reported confidence can remain high when predictions agree; expected calibration error reaches 0.93 among CARLAR cases. We also test a compressed-image few-shot intervention that presents examples as one visual table without model fine-tuning. On the 243-case cohort, Claude's Control condition increases paired joint extraction accuracy by 11.5 percentage points (95 percent interval: 6.2 to 16.9; adjusted p = 0.00054), but its strict-124 gain is smaller and not significant after correction. The intervention's effect on predicted-field agreement is model-dependent: it decreases for Claude and increases for GPT-4.1. These results motivate validation methods that account for visual context and dependencies between extracted fields.

</details>

### 110. Profile-DP: Differential Privacy for User-Controlled Profile Release in Personalized LLM Inference

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27213)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`本地差分隐私`、`用户画像`、`个性化推理`

👤 **作者**：Mingchen Li、…、Yunhe Feng

- 🎯 **研究动机**：个性化 LLM 推理依赖画像条件化，暴露用户画像本身及共享偏好
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Profile-DP 策略感知本地 DP 框架：DP-SLOT 独立扰动受保护属性，DP-BLOCK 用公共语义决策协调属性联合采样，先私有化画像再与指令组合生成
- 📌 **结论**：在 PEEP 上 DP-BLOCK 于全部 13 个结构化预算下领先 DP 方法效用组合，26 个格式-预算设置中 18 个最接近理想隐私-效用点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Personalized large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on profile-conditioned inference, pairing public requests with rich, user-specific facts. This paradigm exposes the user profile itself, together with the user's explicit sharing preferences. We introduce Profile-DP, a policy-aware local differential privacy framework designed specifically for user-profile release in personalized LLM inference. Profile-DP provably bounds the release of user-withheld facts while treating public instructions, profile schemas, and authorized facts as public side information. By generating a privatized profile that composes with the public instruction prior to answer generation, our framework decouples formal privacy guarantees from downstream model behavior. We develop two finite-domain mechanisms satisfying Profile-DP: DP-SLOT independently perturbs protected attributes, while DP-BLOCK uses public semantic decisions to coordinate their release and jointly sample related attributes. A positive, public compatibility prior connects these attributes while preserving a profile-level privacy bound. On PEEP, DP-BLOCK leads the utility composite among DP methods at all 13 structured budgets and is closest to the ideal privacy--utility point in 18 of 26 format--budget settings under our composite weighting. Direct measurements reveal complementary strengths: DP-BLOCK discloses less, while DP-SLOT retains more authorized facts. These results make policy-aware profile release a practical basis for studying privacy and personalization.

</details>

### 111. Deep catastrophic-risk-aware reinforcement learning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27214)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`尾部风险`、`CVaR`、`极值理论`

👤 **作者**：Carlos Pérez Octavio Mendoza、Frédéric Godin

- 🎯 **研究动机**：置信水平升高时仅少量观测贡献 CVaR 经验估计，极端风险估计在 RL 中愈发困难
- 🔬 **研究方法**：将极值理论 peaks-over-threshold 表示与方法矩估计扩展到高维神经策略，得到可微尾部风险目标并在深对冲问题验证
- 📌 **结论**：正常条件下与经验 CVaR 策略行为相似，极端情景评估下 EVT 训练策略对冲损失更低，为极端风险观测稀缺时的实用替代

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Estimating extreme risk in reinforcement learning becomes increasingly difficult as the confidence level increases, since only a small number of observations contribute to empirical estimates of Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR). In this paper, we extend an Extreme Value Theory (EVT) approach for CVaR optimization to high-dimensional neural-network policies. Using a peaks-over-threshold representation and method-of-moments estimation, we obtain a differentiable tail-risk objective and show that, conditional on the selected threshold, its finite-sample gradient can be computed directly through automatic differentiation. We first validate the approach in a controlled experiment with a known optimal solution and then investigate its performance in a deep-hedging problem with rare extreme market movements. Our results show similar hedging behavior for EVT- and empirical-CVaR-trained policies under normal conditions, while EVT-trained policies exhibit lower hedging losses under the extreme-scenario evaluation across the training sample sizes considered. These results suggest that EVT can provide a practical alternative for training neural policies when the observations relevant to extreme risk are scarce.

</details>

### 112. Neighborhood-Aware Unlearning for Generative Recommendation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27232)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`生成式推荐`、`机器遗忘`、`邻域影响`

👤 **作者**：Pierre Lubitzsch、Yubao Tang、Maarten de Rijke、Sebastian Schelter

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式推荐中语义 ID 词元跨相邻物品共享，遗忘一条交互可能意外波及其邻域预测
- 🔬 **研究方法**：证明该效应局部结构化且被全局指标掩盖，提出邻域感知遗忘 NAU：在交互级遗忘之上显式控制被遗忘物品邻域的概率质量
- 📌 **结论**：三数据集四种分词器上恢复垃圾注入带来的曝光至重训参考并保留 95% 以上效用，指出局部概率重分配是重要失效模式

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative recommendation predicts items by autoregressively generating semantic identifiers (SIDs), whose tokens are shared across semantically related items. With this paradigm gaining adoption, the question of how to support unlearning in it has become pressing. If training interactions must later be removed, such as injected spam or interactions with items a user no longer wants, this sharing lets an unlearning update unintentionally affect the predictions for the interactions' neighbors. We show that this effect is locally structured and hidden by global metrics: unlearning can leave recommendation utility on the whole test set nearly unchanged while substantially degrading it on neighborhoods of the forgotten interactions' SIDs. We propose Neighborhood-Aware Unlearning (NAU), which augments interaction-level unlearning with explicit control over the probability mass assigned to the forgotten items' neighborhoods. Across three datasets and four SID tokenizers, NAU restores spam-induced exposure toward the retrained reference while retaining more than 95% of retrained utility, and recovers the neighborhood utility that forget-and-repair unlearning loses at no global cost. We identify local probability redistribution as an important failure mode of unlearning in SID-based generative recommenders and show that explicitly controlling this redistribution can mitigate its effects.

</details>

### 113. Predicting Linear Relational Decodability Without the Relation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27241)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`线性关系嵌入`、`忠实性`、`雅可比视角`

👤 **作者**：Jonas Rohweder、Adrian Mülthaler、Farid Mehri、Sebastian Gerstner、Hinrich Schütze

- 🎯 **研究动机**：线性关系嵌入对关系的忠实性从近零到上限差异巨大，其成因不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：提出实体镜头（新型雅可比镜头），用与关系无关的读出预测 LRE 忠实性
- 📌 **结论**：Olmo-3-7B 28 个关系上关系无关得分与忠实性相关 ρ=0.7355，说明线性可解码性主要是主体表征已有内容的属性，并指出现有关系数据集的缺陷

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Linear relational embeddings (LREs) approximate a language model's subject-to-object mapping with an affine function. Their faithfulness varies enormously across relations, from near zero for person_father to the ceiling for country_capital_city. We investigate the factors that contribute to this variation and demonstrate that it can be significantly predicted from a readout that is not aware of the underlying relation. To do so, we propose a novel form of the Jacobian lens, the entity lens. Whereas the Jacobian lens is estimated across all tokens and concepts, the entity lens EL_t is trained to predict what the model will output for an entity of type t under the neutral prompt "{} is known for"; e.g., we train ELs for countries and persons. After estimating an entity lens, we measure how highly it ranks the model's answer for a relation without using any relation-specific information in estimation and prediction. Across 28 relations of the LRE dataset on Olmo-3-7B this relation-agnostic score correlates with LRE faithfulness at \rho = 0.7355 (p < .01). This suggests that linear decodability is largely a property of what a subject's representation already carries, rather than of the relation being decoded. Moving the unit of analysis from the relation to the individual fact, we conclude that new relation datasets and analysis methods are needed to address several problems in existing datasets, most prominently semantically weak first tokens (e.g., most predicted universities' first token is "University") and reference competition (e.g., the LLM confuses obscure actor names with competing non-actor entities that have the same last name). Our full pipeline is available at this https URL

</details>

### 114. Target-Aware Curation of Hallucination Stress Tests for Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27247)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`幻觉压力测试`、`定向构造`、`模型特异性`

👤 **作者**：Jiho Lee、Bingquan Zhang、Lin Ting Liang、Soroush Vosoughi、Weicheng Ma

- 🎯 **研究动机**：幻觉压力测试在模型间平均分相似，但不同提示在各模型上诱发的幻觉不同
- 🔬 **研究方法**：迭代编辑种子提示定向暴露目标模型幻觉：LLM 编辑器依据目标回答与裁判的类别化评分及解释修订提示，目标模型也获得上轮反馈
- 📌 **结论**：编辑显著提升裁判评级严重度（强幻觉桶 77.2%/74.2%），五模型平均分相近但成对相关仅 0.04-0.19，提示复用价值取决于来源有效性与模型特异失效

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Hallucination stress tests can yield similar average scores across language models even when different prompts elicit hallucinations in each model. We develop a procedure that iteratively edits source-grounded seed prompts to expose hallucinations in a target model. An LLM editor revises each prompt in response to the target's answer and a judge's category-specific score and explanation. In the main setting, the target also receives feedback on its previous response. Experiments cover 500 seeds from seven datasets and ten topics, with intrinsic and extrinsic hallucinations evaluated across open-weight and closed-source models. Editing substantially increases judge-rated severity over the seeds. In the baseline configuration, retained responses fall in the judge's strong-hallucination bin in 77.2% of runs targeting extrinsic hallucination and 74.2% targeting intrinsic hallucination. Later editing rounds account for more than half of the mean score increase. Among 219 prompts that pass model-assisted source-validity checks, 89 elicit responses containing unsupported claims. Across five models, 200 curated prompts yield similar average severity scores, although pairwise Spearman correlations between prompt-level scores are only $\rho=0.0387$ to $0.1897$. Curated prompts can be reused across models, but their diagnostic value depends on source validity and the failures they expose in each model.

</details>

### 115. The Differential Effects of AI Abstention on Advice Acceptance

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27258)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`弃答`、`建议接受度`、`人机交互`

👤 **作者**：Nicholas Wolczynski、Yotam Liel、Maytal Saar-Tsechansky、Lior Zalmanson

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 建议系统开始选择性保留意见（弃答），但用户如何响应弃答系统知之甚少
- 🔬 **研究方法**：预注册实验中参与者在两种建议制度（选择性建议 vs 每次建议）下做重复金融决策
- 📌 **结论**：同一弃答制度对不同用户产生分化效应：提高高专业感知用户的建议接受、倾向于降低低专业感知用户接受，机制在于反复调和建议与自身判断的认知负担被缓解

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

AI advising systems are typically designed to always provide a response. Recent work has focused on developing systems that withhold advice in some instances, such as when advice quality is questionable, to improve the overall quality of guidance when it is given; a design paradigm we refer to as AI abstention. While substantial work has focused on optimizing when models should abstain, little is known about how users respond to systems that abstain. In a preregistered experiment, participants made repeated financial decisions, assisted by one of two advising regimes: an AI that advises selectively or one that advises on every decision. We find that the same AI abstention regime produces differential effects depending on the user: it increases advice acceptance among users with high perceived expertise and tends to decrease it among users with low perceived expertise. This differential effect is accounted for by the cognitive burden users face when repeatedly reconciling AI recommendations with their own judgment; AI abstention appears to relieve this burden among users with high perceived expertise, and in doing so increases their receptivity to advice. Further, AI abstention mitigates the decline in advice acceptance observed among expert users over repeated interactions. Exploratory evidence also suggests it increases appropriate acceptance where advice would improve the decision, increasing the downstream accuracy of perceived experts. The AI abstention regime is thus a first-order design property of AI advisory systems, and one that must be calibrated to the users a system serves.

</details>

### 116. When Does Hate Transfer? A Large-Scale Study of Cross-Lingual Hate Speech Detection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27285)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`跨语言迁移`、`仇恨言论检测`、`低资源语言`

👤 **作者**：Zahra Bokaei、Walid Magdy、Bonnie Webber

- 🎯 **研究动机**：一种语言的训练数据能否以可比性能支持另一语言的仇恨言论检测是核心实践问题
- 🔬 **研究方法**：14 种语言大规模研究，测量语言相似度、时间重叠与数据规模差等因素与迁移效果的关联并做定性分析
- 📌 **结论**：语言相似度是最强预测因子，文化线索与交流规范可能解释部分剩余案例，为多语言仇恨检测系统设计提供依据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Effective detection of hate content is essential for maintaining healthy online communities, yet performance can vary substantially across languages. This work investigates a core practical question: Can training data from one language support hate-speech detection in another language with comparable performance? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale study across 14 languages. \change{We observe consistent patterns in which certain language pairs transfer more effectively than others. Among the factors we measure, linguistic similarity is the strongest predictor of these patterns, with temporal overlap and dataset-size difference playing smaller roles. These measurable factors do not fully explain the observed patterns; a complementary qualitative analysis suggests that shared cultural cues and communicative norms may be associated with some of the remaining cases, particularly within several clusters. We present this cultural interpretation as a hypothesis from qualitative inspection rather than a measured cause.}{} Our analysis includes low-resource languages, offering practical insights into when cross-lingual transfer can support them and where its limitations emerge. These findings inform the design of more reliable multilingual hate-detection systems.

</details>

### 117. Stronger artificial intelligence breakthroughs hasten their own exhaustion

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27296)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`科研生态`、`注意力集中`、`思想空间`

👤 **作者**：Beichen Lu、Honglin Bao、Qi Huang、Qingguo Meng、James A. Evans

- 🎯 **研究动机**：AI 进步被默认随投入规模扩展，但发现难度可能位于思想空间且多样性注意力可被集体集中耗尽
- 🔬 **研究方法**：分析 36 年计算机科学 720 万篇论文、7.3 万评审与 370 万研究者的主题迁移与评审信心，结合结构生物学对照与仿真
- 📌 **结论**：Transformer 后领域扩张但注意力急剧收窄，过半迁移研究者转入 AI；越大突破越完全捕获注意力并加速耗尽后续思想，互惠评审等政策进一步收窄接受面

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Progress in artificial intelligence is widely assumed to scale with effort: more researchers, papers and investment should accelerate discovery. Theories of technological growth instead locate the difficulty of discovery in the space of ideas. Here we show that neither scale nor space is enough: breakthroughs depend on diverse attention, which collective concentration can deplete. Across 36 years of computer science, we analyze 7.2 million papers, 73,089 peer reviews and 3.7 million researchers and their topic shifts. The field expanded while its attention narrowed sharply after the Transformer. Over half of topic-shifting researchers moved into AI, especially mid-career ones, and reviewer confidence fell by a full point on a 5-point scale. Each successive breakthrough, from AlexNet to large language models, raised the field's carrying capacity for ideas but was followed by steeper exhaustion of those ideas. Bigger breakthroughs capture attention more completely, depleting the diversity their successors may draw upon. Structural biology shows the same cycle after cryo-EM and AlphaFold. Simulations show how well-intentioned and widely used policies such as reciprocal reviewing empower prolific authors and further narrow what the field accepts. We call for institutions that protect diverse attention in artificial intelligence rather than reward concentration on the winning paradigm.

</details>

### 118. SHAM-Bench: A Span-level Hallucination Benchmark Across Multiple Tasks

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27370)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`span 级幻觉`、`检测基准`、`分布偏移`

👤 **作者**：Sicong Huang、Ian Lane

- 🎯 **研究动机**：span 级幻觉检测评测碎片化于任务、标注格式与指标，难以判断方法是否跨域泛化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：SHAM-Bench 统一六类 NLG 任务 6461 例到共同 schema 与协议，评测微调检测器与提示式方法
- 📌 **结论**：微调检测器域内好但分布偏移下降级，带推理的提示式方法更鲁棒；细微无支撑内容仍难检测，span 边界歧义与标签噪声持续挑战评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Span-level hallucination detection pinpoints unsupported content in LLM outputs, enabling actionable feedback and targeted mitigation. Existing evaluations for span-level hallucination detection methods are fragmented across tasks, annotation formats, and metrics, and most span-level datasets cover only a single NLG domain, making it difficult to assess whether a detection method actually generalizes across tasks and domains. In this paper, we introduce SHAM-Bench (Span-level Hallucination Benchmark Across Multiple Tasks), a unified benchmark for span-level hallucination detection spanning six NLG tasks, aggregated from existing human-annotated datasets totaling 6,461 examples under a common schema and evaluation protocol. Using SHAM-Bench, we evaluate existing hallucination detection methods and find that fine-tuned detectors often perform well in-domain but degrade under distribution shift, whereas prompt-based methods with reasoning are more robust across tasks. Our analysis further shows that subtle unsupported content remains difficult to detect and span-level evaluation is still challenged by ambiguous span boundaries and noisy labels. We release the dataset, evaluation code, and a public leaderboard to support future research on broadly generalizable hallucination detection.

</details>

### 119. AgenticVulHunter: Bringing Local Agentic Vulnerability Hunting to Your Terminal

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27383)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`智能体安全评审`、`开源模型`、`本地部署`

👤 **作者**：Nishant Abhishek、Ping Charoenwet、Kla Tantithamthavorn

- 🎯 **研究动机**：依赖外部商用 LLM 的安全代码评审无法本地化，且一次性灌入大量安全知识效果不佳
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AgenticVulHunter 用本地开源 LLM 做智能体安全评审，聚焦相关安全知识；在 SCRBench 144 个漏洞上评估
- 📌 **结论**：Qwen3-Coder-30B 与 DeepSeek V3.1 Terminus 分别达 18.4%/23.8% 正确定位与类型匹配，超过用 Claude Sonnet 4 的 AgenticSCR（17.5%）

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Finding vulnerabilities early, before they can affect the production system, is important for reducing the risk of any software attacks. However, detecting vulnerabilities during secure code review is still difficult because the vulnerable code logic may depend on lines found across different repository files. Recent approaches such as AgenticSCR have shown that LLM agents can explore repository files and use security knowledge to improve secure code review. However, these approaches mainly rely on external commercial LLMs and can provide a large amount of security knowledge to the agent at once. This creates an important question of whether agentic secure code review can also be performed effectively using locally deployed open-weight LLMs with more focused and relevant security knowledge. In this paper, we propose AgenticVulHunter, an agentic secure code review approach and tool that can run with a locally deployed open-weight LLM. We evaluate AgenticVulHunter on 144 vulnerability cases from SCRBench using Qwen3-Coder-30B and DeepSeek V3.1 Terminus. We measure what percentage of generated review comments correctly identify the vulnerable location, match the vulnerability type, and provide a relevant security review comment together. AgenticVulHunter achieves an accuracy of 18.4% with Qwen3- Coder-30B and 23.8% with DeepSeek V3.1 Terminus, compared with 11.8% for our AgenticSCR-style reproduction using Qwen3-Coder-30B and 17.5% for AgenticSCR using Claude Sonnet 4.

</details>

### 120. Looking Into The Past: The Impact of Context on Detecting Deceptive Statements

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27407)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`欺骗检测`、`上下文依赖`、`隐藏角色游戏`

👤 **作者**：Alex Clay、Ernesto Jimenez-Ruiz、Pranava Madhyastha

- 🎯 **研究动机**：欺骗检测通常孤立处理消息，而游戏历史等前置信息对预测可能至关重要
- 🔬 **研究方法**：在 Avalon 隐藏角色游戏中测量前置对话上下文对欺骗消息检测的影响
- 📌 **结论**：加入游戏上下文使 LLM 欺骗检测准确率提升最高 31 点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Deception detection typically considers messages in isolation. Previous works in claim verification and role detection have displayed that additional information such as gameplay history is crucial to successful predictions. In this work, we demonstrate the value of preceding gameplay dialogue when applied to deceptive message detection within the hidden role game of Avalon. The findings show that LLMs can see improvement across a number of metrics, including accuracy by up to 31\% with the addition of gameplay context for the target message.

</details>

### 121. The Router Already Knows

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27421)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`MoE 路由`、`专家对齐字典`、`稀疏自编码器`

👤 **作者**：Andrew Bistras、Marty Johnson

- 🎯 **研究动机**：MoE 模型每个词元都做稀疏专家选择，稀疏字典是否应复用该选择
- 🔬 **研究方法**：NR-SAE 给每个被路由专家配小字典、只开冻结路由器选中的字典；Gemma 4 26B-A4B 块 15 上与秩寻址、候选匹配、架构匹配随机化三对照比较
- 📌 **结论**：专家对齐自编码器解释 0.518 方差且三种变体均领先对照，部分优势是架构性的（命名专家即命名子空间），语义可解释性与因果忠实性未测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models already make a sparse choice for every token: which experts should process it. We ask whether a sparse dictionary should reuse that choice. NR-SAE gives each routed expert a small dictionary and opens only those the frozen router selects. At block 15 of Gemma 4 26B-A4B, after ten million C4 training tokens and across three seeds, the expert-aligned autoencoder explains 0.518 of the variance in router-weighted expert outputs on a fixed evaluation capture. A rank-addressed control with the same parameters but sixteen times as many candidates reaches 0.481, a candidate-matched control 0.458, and an architecture-matched control with randomized expert identity 0.387. A transcoder variant also leads all three controls: 0.432, against 0.347, 0.371, and 0.118. Part of this advantage is architectural: each expert writes into a subspace of at most a quarter of the model width, so naming the expert names the subspace. How much it explains, we have not measured. At this layer and budget, the router already knows something a dictionary would otherwise have to learn. Our implementation did not train faster, and semantic interpretability, downstream loss, and causal faithfulness remain untested.

</details>

### 122. Securing Agentic Commerce Through Behavior-Aware Fraud Detection and Cybersecurity for Trusted AI-Agent Payment Authorization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27483)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent 商务`、`欺诈检测`、`行为分析`

👤 **作者**：Kayode L. Ogunsusi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：自主商务 agent 带来冒充、未授权支付、被攻陷与授权滥用等新风险
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ACAGS 组合监督学习、行为分析、agent 专属授权控制与可解释 AI，在合成交易数据与五种对抗场景上评估
- 📌 **结论**：行为特征使 F1 从 52.12% 升至 67.49%，冒充/恶意委托/越权检测率 99% 以上，被攻陷 agent 检测 72.27%，尚需真实交易验证

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The growing use of autonomous artificial intelligence agents in digital commerce creates new security concerns involving agent impersonation, unauthorized payments, compromised agents, and misuse of delegated authority. This research introduces the Autonomous Commerce Agent Guard System (ACAGS), a cybersecurity framework combining supervised machine learning, behavioral analytics, agent-specific authorization controls, and explainable AI. The framework was evaluated using synthetic financial transaction data augmented with simulated agent characteristics and five adversarial scenarios. XGBoost was selected following comparisons with alternative classifiers. Across ten experimental runs, behavioral features improved mean F1-score from 52.12% to 67.49%, with agent-specific features increasing performance to 69.46%. The framework achieved detection rates of 99.77% for abnormal agent behavior, 99.73% for impersonation, and 100% for malicious delegation and authorization-scope violations. Compromised-agent detection reached 72.27%. ACAGS also supported APPROVE, REVIEW, and REJECT authorization outcomes, with SHAP explanations providing model interpretability. The results demonstrate the potential of combining behavioral intelligence and delegated authorization controls to strengthen simulated agentic-commerce payment security, while highlighting the need for validation using real-world transactions.

</details>

### 123. HATCH: Hardware-Attested Task Chains for Verifiable Manipulation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27485)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`可验证评测`、`机器人操纵`、`硬件证明`

👤 **作者**：Shiza Charania

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机器人操纵能力声明不可验证，现有榜单封闭、仿真或可博弈
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HATCH 机器人密室：开放可复现、传感器计分、不可预演；任务链式衔接（一任务传感器确认解锁下一任务），入场者提交经证明的自跑记录（未剪视频、传感器日志与种子）
- 📌 **结论**：五技能类×三难度、具身无关计分与当日种子随机摆位，把长程评测绑定到物理仪器而非人工判断

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Capability claims in robot manipulation are unverifiable, and existing leaderboards are each closed, simulated, or gameable. We propose HATCH, a robot escape room: an openly specified, physically replicable, sensor-scored, and unrehearsable benchmark. Tasks span five skill categories at three difficulty levels and are chained so that the sensor confirming one task's completion unlocks the next, turning a room into a single long-horizon evaluation. Success is confirmed by instrumentation rather than human judgment, scoring is embodiment-agnostic, and object placements are randomized by a run-day seed. Entrants submit attested self-runs (uncut video, sensor logs, and the seed), with occasional organized events for calibration. Task specifications, build guide, bill of materials, and scoring rubrics are released under an open license.

</details>

### 124. The More the Better? A Comparison of Single- and Multi-Agent LLM Setups on a Financial Review Task

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27488)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多智能体评测`、`单对多对比`、`受控实验`

👤 **作者**：Pei Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：实务界认为多 agent 分工审阅文档更好，需在受控条件下检验
- 🔬 **研究方法**：300 份合成信贷备忘录上每项要求有真值，单 agent 一次查六项 vs 三 agent 同模型同话术各查两项，测量漏检与误报
- 📌 **结论**：团队多捕获 0.08-0.10 带解释缺陷但四个模型中三个误报显著增加，均衡精度变化 ≤0.02 且耗 2.2-2.4 倍词元；单 agent 采样三次多数标注反而更优

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Practitioner guidance holds that a large language model reviews a document better when the work is divided among agents. We test a narrow form of it in a controlled comparison of single-agent and multi-agent setups on a financial task, reviewing credit assessment memos against requirements. One agent checks six requirements in one call. A team of three on the same model checks the same memo, two requirements each, in the same words, with no roles or later stage. Labels are known for every requirement on 300 synthetic memos, so misses and false alarms are measured separately. Two primary predictions are tested on four models at a Bonferroni-corrected level. Under an instruction to decide each requirement once, the team catches 0.08 to 0.10 more of the defects that carry an explanation on the three models whose single agent misses some, at the corrected level on two. It raises more false alarms on all four, at the corrected level on three. Balanced accuracy, an exploratory measure, changes by 0.02 or less, and the team uses 2.2 to 2.4 times the tokens. Showing each agent the requirements it was not given reduces the added false alarms on the two Claude models, at the corrected level on one. In exploratory comparisons, sampling the single agent three times and flagging on any draw gives higher balanced accuracy than the team on those models, and the team's results depend on the instruction.

</details>

### 125. Judging the Justification: Decision Certificate Supervision for Generative Reward Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27528)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`生成式奖励模型`、`理由监督`、`位置偏差`

👤 **作者**：Guiquan Sun、Bing Bai、Martin Renqiang Min、Dongjin Song

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式奖励模型正确判决不保证合理依据，而参考解释获取成本高
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DeCert 无需参考解释即评估理由有效性与判决一致性，并转化为兼顾判决正确与理由质量的偏好监督
- 📌 **结论**：两模型家族多个分布内外基准上超越强生成式裁判基线并提升理由质量，位置一致准确率提升至多 14.8 点

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Generative reward models (GenRMs) generate natural language rationales to justify preference judgments, yet a correct verdict does not guarantee a sound justification. Existing approaches often rely on reference explanations to supervise rationales, but obtaining such references can be costly and does not necessarily establish whether generated rationales support the resulting verdict.
