To address these limitations, we propose DeCert, a framework that evaluates the validity and verdict consistency of generated rationales without requiring reference explanations. Crucially, DeCert translates these assessments into preference supervision that accounts for both verdict correctness and justification quality. In particular, when multiple sampled judgments share the same correct verdict, differences in certificate quality enable DeCert to construct rationale preference pairs that distinguish stronger from weaker justifications. Extensive empirical studies across two model families and multiple in- and out-of-distribution benchmarks demonstrate that DeCert consistently outperforms strong generative judge baselines while improving rationale quality. Moreover, DeCert effectively mitigates positional bias, improving position-consistent accuracy by up to 14.8 points. These results highlight the importance of supervising not only which verdict is correct, but also how that verdict is justified.

</details>

### 126. EviDial: When Correct Depression Predictions Lack Evidence

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27541)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`证据充分性`、`临床评测`、`标签-证据解离`

👤 **作者**：Junjie Xu、…、Liang He

- 🎯 **研究动机**：抑郁对话基准以预测标签对临床量表计分，但对话可能缺乏支撑该判断的足够证据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EviDial 382 段对话 2744 个参与者-症状单元，用概化理论、多面 Rasch 与随机效应潜类分析校准证据参照，分为无/部分/冲突/充分证据
- 📌 **结论**：82.8% 单元缺乏足够文本证据，各模型 69.2-96.9% 被标签判对的预测缺乏充分证据支撑，标签评测系统性高估循证能力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Depression dialogue benchmarks commonly assess models by comparing predicted symptom labels with clinical scale scores. However, a prediction may agree with a benchmark label even when the dialogue lacks enough evidence to justify it. We introduce EviDial, a benchmark that distinguishes label agreement from evidence-grounded assessment. EviDial comprises 382 depression dialogues from three sources and 2,744 participant-symptom cells, each classified as having absent, partial, conflicting, or sufficient evidence. Because evidence sufficiency lacks a directly observable gold standard, we derive calibrated evidence references from multiple judgments using generalizability theory, many-facet Rasch measurement, and random-effects latent class analysis. Our audit reveals that 82.8% of participant-symptom cells lack sufficient textual evidence for the corresponding symptom assessment, with substantial variation across sources. Across model families and prompting strategies, 69.2%-96.9% of predictions judged correct by benchmark labels are nonetheless unsupported by sufficient dialogue evidence. Further analyses show that strong agreement among LLM evaluators need not reflect independent evidence judgments, underscoring the need to calibrate automated reference panels. These findings demonstrate that label-based evaluation can overestimate evidence-grounded performance by conflating agreement with clinical labels and support in the observed dialogue. EviDial provides a measurement layer for separating these constructs and auditing LLM-based depression assessment more faithfully.

</details>

### 127. Learned Readout Tokens for Extracting Large Language Model Confidence

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27566)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`置信度探针`、`读出词元`、`可靠性`

👤 **作者**：Nima Seifi、Martin Ester

- 🎯 **研究动机**：探针法估 LLM 置信度通常读取已有词元的隐藏状态，位置选择本身可学习
- 🔬 **研究方法**：ScratchProbe 学习新读出词元并以其隐藏状态训练探针，扩展多词元读出
- 📌 **结论**：多数据集多 LLM 上超过现有词元读出，且作为即插即用改进施加于 SOTA 探针后在域内外所有骨干提升 AUROC

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used within everyday applications where their responses have significant downstream impacts. For responsible deployment of such systems, we desire insight into how much we can trust their individual outputs. A popular approach for estimating LLM confidence is to train probes on the hidden states of the LLM's tokens to predict the correctness of the model's output. These probes typically read hidden states from tokens that are already in the model's vocabulary. In this work, we introduce a new approach to confidence probes that learn a new readout token and utilize its hidden states to train the probe. We show that our learned readout approach ScratchProbe outperforms its existing-token counterpart on a variety of datasets and LLMs. We also extend our method to a multi-token approach which we show provides further performance improvements. Finally, since the learned readout changes only the position a probe reads from, any existing probe can adopt it. Applied to a state-of-the-art probe, it improves AUROC on every backbone, in domain and out of domain.

</details>

### 128. WORTH Benchmark: Evidence Discovery under Not-Fully-Cooperative Information -- A Two-Episode Pilot

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27597)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`证据发现`、`非合作信息`、`诊断评测`

👤 **作者**：Bo Zou、Chao Xu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：现有推理评测把任务相关信息当作前提提供，很少考察模型自主发现证据与叙事编排对判断的影响
- 🔬 **研究方法**：WORTH：读无标注线索的悬疑叙事、自选关键线索句、指认案件参与者并解释推理；两回合 70 次正式运行分别记录线索命中与参与人判断
- 📌 **结论**：线索命中与参与人判断不同步，37 个可计分运行中 31 个同时选中同一错误参与人组合，指向性/开脱性句子选择 25:0，展示组件化诊断的价值

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Existing reasoning evaluations typically provide task-relevant information to models as available premises, rarely examining concurrently how models discover evidence autonomously and how information presentation within narratives guides or obscures judgment. This paper proposes the WORTH Benchmark: models read original mystery narratives without clue annotations, autonomously select key clue sentences, identify case participants, and explain their reasoning process. The narrative materials are designed by professional authors such that evidence facilitating reasoning coexists with narrative arrangements that delay the revelation of truth. We report a pilot experiment under full-story conditions across two episodes, conducting a total of 70 formal runs on models, of which 67 runs are content-scorable. Scoring separately records hits on author-designated key clue sentences and participant selections; natural language explanations are used for case diagnosis and are not scored independently. The results show that clue hits and participant judgments are not synchronized; across 37 content-scorable runs in Episode 02, 31 runs simultaneously selected the same set of incorrect participants, while adjacent suspicion-oriented and exoneration-oriented sentences were selected 25 times and 0 times, respectively. These phenomena illustrate the diagnostic value of component-wise analysis and suggest that not-fully-cooperative informational relationships may influence evidence selection and judgment. This study only implements full-story conditions for two episodes, and does not establish stable model rankings or infer general mechanisms on this basis; human player records have not yet been cleaned, and this version does not report human-model comparisons.

</details>

### 129. Speak vs. Know: Balancing Behavior and Knowledge Distributions of Large Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27648)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`知识-行为分布`、`评测有效性`、`意见分布`

👤 **作者**：Yang Liu、Masahiro Kaneko、Chenhui Chu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：言语化分布比 log 概率分布更贴人类观点分布，但部署时实际输出由后者支配——模型知道的与说出的不一致
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三个 LLM 在世界价值观调查上，37% 问题两分布的最可能答案不同；提出可学习组合：跟随行为分布众数同时保留知识分布形状
- 📌 **结论**：单参数权衡行为保真与分布质量，六模型三数据集上行为一致性从 71.6% 提升，评测应分别报告两种分布

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent work reports that the distribution verbalized directly in the output text aligns far better with human opinion distributions than the next-token log-probability distribution of LLMs. However, the verbalized distribution reflects what LLMs know about a group's opinions (knowledge distribution), while what they actually output at deployment is governed by their log-probability distribution (behavior distribution). We find that when prompting LLMs to predict the opinion of a specific group, the most likely answer under the verbalized distribution differs from that under the log-probability distribution for 37% of World Values Survey questions, averaged over three LLMs. Thus, LLMs' knowledge does not match their behavior. This matters for evaluation: a verbalized distribution can score well against a group while its most likely answer is one the model never outputs, so the score no longer describes the model. The alignment scores for these two distributions reflect only the model's behavior or its knowledge, separately. We therefore propose a learnable combination of the two distributions that follows the most likely answer of the behavior distribution while preserving the shape of the knowledge distribution, with a single hyperparameter trading off behavioral fidelity against distribution quality. Our experiments on three opinion survey datasets and six models show that the balanced distribution improves behavioral consistency over the verbalized distribution from 71.6% to 95.6% on average, while the total variation distance to the human opinion distributions rises only slightly from 28.8% to 33.8%, and it remains effective under temperature scaling and cross-dataset settings.

</details>

### 130. CAESAR: Mixed Reality Search-and-Rescue Platform for Cognitive Attack Evaluation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27654)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`混合现实`、`认知攻击`、`评测平台`

👤 **作者**：Mingyu Zhu、…、Bin Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：搜救训练等安全关键协作中用户依赖虚拟元素感知协调决策，易受扭曲视觉反馈的认知攻击，但缺乏标准化多用户测试床
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CAESAR 端到端多用户 MR 框架集成四类认知攻击于同步协作场景，配多模态数据采集管线；对延迟攻击做受控用户研究
- 📌 **结论**：延迟攻击对任务表现与用户行为产生可测影响并刻画主观体验，为系统性对抗实验提供标准化平台

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Mixed Reality (MR) enables immersive training by tightly coupling virtual content with the physical environment and supporting embodied interaction. In safety-critical collaborative tasks such as search-and-rescue, however, users rely heavily on virtual elements for perception, coordination, and decision-making, making them vulnerable to cognitive attacks that subtly distort visual feedback, virtual content, or system synchronization. While prior MR security research has examined individual attack mechanisms, evaluations are typically conducted in isolated or single-user settings, and there is a lack of standardized collaborative testbeds for systematic adversarial experimentation. In this paper, we develop CAESAR, an end-to-end multi-user MR framework for systematic evaluation of cognitive attacks in collaborative search-and-rescue training. CAESAR features a modular architecture that integrates four classes of cognitive attacks within a synchronized collaborative scenario and incorporates a multimodal data collection pipeline that captures behavioral, performance, and subjective measures under adversarial conditions. We conduct a controlled user study with an in-depth analysis of the latency-based attack, demonstrating measurable effects on task performance and user behavior while characterizing users' subjective experience under adversarial conditions.

</details>

### 131. Beyond Calibration: Temporal LLM Confidence Assessment Across Languages and Local Settings

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27702)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`置信度评估`、`时间维度`、`多语言`

👤 **作者**：Sahil Kale

- 🎯 **研究动机**：置信信号用于检索、自适应计算与选择性弃答，其在解题不同阶段的语义与英语之外可靠性未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：区分解前 FOK 与解后 JOL 信号，跨两个前沿 LLM、多推理域、翻译马拉地语基准与本地知识新基准评估
- 📌 **结论**：廉价解前信号弱校准、解后更强；翻译下校准趋势大体保留但本地知识显著退化，幻觉回答在生成后仍维持高置信

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models are expected to estimate their own uncertainty, with confidence signals used to guide retrieval, adaptive compute allocation, routing, and selective abstention. We investigate whether confidence signals measured at different temporal stages of problem solving encode distinct forms of epistemic information and remain reliable beyond English-centric settings. Inspired by classical meta-memory theory, we distinguish anticipatory pre-solve confidence (Feeling-of-Knowing, FOK) from retrospective post-solve confidence (Judgement-of-Learning, JOL). We evaluate these signals across two frontier LLMs, multiple reasoning domains, translated Marathi benchmarks, and a newly constructed benchmark of genuinely local Marathi factual questions. Across settings, the cheaper pre-solve signal remains weakly calibrated, while post-solve confidence generally provides stronger epistemic alignment despite its additional inference cost. Confidence behaviour transfers surprisingly well under direct language translation, with calibration trends largely preserved between English and Marathi, but degrades substantially on local Marathi knowledge, suggesting that linguistic transfer alone is insufficient for region-specific information needs. Finally, hallucinated responses maintain persistently elevated confidence after answer generation, highlighting important limitations of current LLM self-assessment mechanisms.

</details>

### 132. Tracing Recurrent and Emergent Hallucination Patterns in Decomposed Text-to-SQL Generation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27720)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`Text-to-SQL`、`幻觉溯源`、`轨迹级分析`

👤 **作者**：Hayden Moore、Abhinav Verma、Anshuman Dutt

- 🎯 **研究动机**：强基准分之下 schema 与查询逻辑错误仍存，仅评最终查询难判断错误是否在更早步骤已出现
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把查询分解为渐复杂可执行子查询，规则化幻觉类别沿生成路径检查，区分复现型（早现再现）与涌现型（终现无前兆）幻觉
- 📌 **结论**：五 LLM 上多类别有可观复现概率、子句滥用条件涌现更高；对话历史使复现升、涌现降，两条件测度不同向，需轨迹级评测补充终输出评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models can achieve strong Text-to-SQL benchmark performance while still producing schema and query-logic errors. Most evaluations characterize such failures only in the final generated query, making it difficult to determine whether a related error was also observed in an earlier, simpler step or appears only at the final stage. We present a drill-down evaluation framework that decomposes Text-to-SQL queries into progressively more complex executable sub-queries and corresponding natural-language questions, allowing rule-based hallucination categories to be examined across a generation path. We distinguish recurrent hallucinations, in which the same rule-based category is observed in an earlier step and again in the final query, from emergent hallucinations, in which the category is observed at the final step without an earlier detection in the same path. Across five LLMs evaluated on an expanded BIRD-mini benchmark, several categories show appreciable recurrence probabilities, while clause abuse shows comparatively higher conditional emergence. Under the conversational-history condition, recurrence probabilities are generally higher and emergence probabilities are generally lower. These observations indicate that the two conditional measures do not move uniformly in the same direction under additional context and motivate trajectory-level analysis in addition to final-output evaluation. Code and evaluation artifacts are available in the project repository. this https URL

</details>

### 133. HydraPoT: A Configurable Multi-Agent Framework for Cost-Aware LLM-Assisted Honeypots

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27722)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`LLM 蜜罐`、`多智能体路由`、`成本感知`

👤 **作者**：Kamolchanok Saengtong、Chutirat Saengyingyongwattana、Sasin Janpuangtong、Norrathep Rattanavipanon

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 提升蜜罐真实感但云模型成本高、轻量模型欺骗性弱，需在效能与预算间权衡
- 🔬 **研究方法**：HydraPoT 按命令复杂度可配置路由到传统仿真、轻量 LLM 与云 LLM 代理，共享会话状态保持跨代理一致性
- 📌 **结论**：运行时开销 <5%、云词元减少 72.6%、成本降 3.7 倍且保真接近纯云方案，使 LLM 蜜罐在有限预算下可实用

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) can improve honeypot realism by generating dynamic, context-aware responses, but powerful cloud models incur substantial cost and latency, while lightweight models may provide weaker deception. This forces practitioners to choose between operational efficiency and interaction fidelity. In this work, we present HydraPoT, a configurable multi-agent honeypot framework that integrates traditional emulation, lightweight LLMs, and powerful cloud LLMs. HydraPoT routes each attacker command to an appropriate agent according to a configurable complexity-based policy, while maintaining shared session state to preserve consistency across agent transitions. We evaluate HydraPoT through an architectural-overhead analysis, a cross-agent context-preservation benchmark, and a three-agent case study. HydraPoT introduces less than 5% runtime overhead, preserves context across heterogeneous agents, and achieves fidelity close to a cloud-LLM-only honeypot. Under our case-study policy, it reduces cloud-processed tokens by 72.6% and operational cost by 3.7x. These results demonstrate that configurable multi-agent routing can make realistic LLM-assisted honeypots more practical under limited operational budgets.

</details>

### 134. Anytime-Valid Runtime Monitoring of Learned Barrier Certificates

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27726)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`运行时监控`、`学习屏障证书`、`随时有效`

👤 **作者**：Zhipeng Cao、Taoran Wu、Weiru Cui、Luke Ong、Bai Xue

- 🎯 **研究动机**：学习屏障证书在部署动力学偏离训练时可能失效，需要监控其统计支撑是否流失
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把条件屏障漂移条件形式化为回合序贯假设检验，屏障增量变换为非负测试上鞅得任意停时下有限样本误报控制，并做确定性时间整形
- 📌 **结论**：监控器检测已部署屏障条件与观测动力学不一致的统计证据（而非预测不安全进入），有效/无效证书下报警行为符合设计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Learned barrier certificates provide a principled mechanism for establishing safety of stochastic systems, but their validity may deteriorate during deployment when the underlying dynamics differ from those used for training. We propose an anytime-valid runtime monitor that audits a learned barrier certificate using observed closed-loop trajectories. The monitor is intended to detect loss of statistical support for the deployed barrier condition; it does not by itself guarantee detection before unsafe entry or safety of a subsequent intervention. Specifically, the conditional barrier-drift condition is formulated as an episode-wise sequential hypothesis test, and observed barrier increments are transformed into a nonnegative test supermartingale, yielding finite-sample false-alarm control under arbitrary stopping times. Inspired by finite-horizon safety verification, we further introduce a deterministic temporal shaping of the monitoring boundary, making the monitor more conservative early in a prescribed deployment horizon while preserving anytime-validity. The monitor is designed to detect statistical evidence that the safety certificate is no longer consistent with the observed dynamics, rather than to predict unsafe entry. To assess the monitor empirically, we evaluate empirical alarm behavior under valid and invalid certificates. Code and data are available at this https URL.

</details>

### 135. Hearsay or Fact? How Framing and Elicitation Shape LLM Probability Updates

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27745)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`概率更新`、`框架效应`、`认识稳健性`

👤 **作者**：An-Sheng Jhang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 作判断助手与心理实验替身，单一新信息使其概率估计移动多少缺乏测量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：δ-SNLI 前提-假设配对加强化/弱化证据，分调用取先验后验并测方向性移动，变化陈述式（学到）vs 转述式（有人说）与单值 vs 五加权估计共七配置
- 📌 **结论**：直接陈述下七个配置均沿标注方向移动 18-28 点；转述一致降低移动并增加反向移动，多估计压缩极端值但两本地模型削减移动 43%/68%——使用 LLM 做认识主体需报告诱导框架

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judgment assistants and as stand-in participants in psychology experiments, yet little is known about how much their probability estimates move in response to a single new piece of information. We pair premise-hypothesis items from $\delta$-SNLI with one strengthener and one weakener each, elicit the prior and the posteriors in separate calls, and measure the directional movement s: the mean posterior change signed by the annotated direction, so that the prior cancels out. We vary whether the update is stated ("Here is something else you learned") or reported ("Someone said that"), and whether the model gives one probability or five weighted estimates. We test seven configurations: two local models (Qwen3-8B, OLMo-2-7B; 240 pairs) and five configurations run through agent command-line harnesses (120 pairs), with hypotheses and analysis rules fixed before the formal runs (not publicly registered). Under stated, direct elicitation all seven move in the annotated direction (s = 18-28 percentage points). Reporting lowers s in all seven point estimates (significantly in six, by 2-5 points) and raises the share of reverse movements in all seven: the effect is consistent but small. Five weighted estimates lower the share of responses at $\leq$5% or $\geq$95% in all seven, but in the two local models they also reduce s by 43% and 68%, whereas in the harnessed configurations the point estimates of the change lie between -3.7 and +0.5 points. An elicitation format that reduces extreme values thus need not preserve sensitivity to evidence; the two should be tested separately. Because $\delta$-NLI annotates only direction, we make no claim about how large an update should be. Code, data and the frozen plan: this https URL

</details>

### 136. How Much Do $\ell_1$ Attacks Need Concentrated Perturbations?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27773)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`l1 对抗攻击`、`扰动集中度`、`评测维度`

👤 **作者**：Melika Shirian、Kianoosh Vadaei、Arshia Hemmat

- 🎯 **研究动机**：全局 l1 预算限制改变总量而非分布，攻击成功在多大程度上依赖大坐标集中扰动未知
- 🔬 **研究方法**：给 APGD-l1/SLIDE/FMN-l1 加逐坐标上限并在环内精确投影，扫上限观察攻击保持率与扰动结构变化
- 📌 **结论**：≤32/255 的上限保留中位 97% 攻击成功、峰值幅度降 80%、有效支撑近三倍；环内约束优化比事后裁剪多保 88 点，对抗训练模型呈异质集中敏感——集中度是 l1 评测的补充轴

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

A global $\ell_1$ budget limits how much an adversary may change an image, not how that change is distributed. We ask how much of an $\ell_1$ attack's observed success remains when large coordinate changes are restricted. We add a per-coordinate cap to the budget, enforce the joint constraint by exact in-loop projection, and sweep the cap for APGD-$\ell_1$, SLIDE, and FMN-$\ell_1$ without altering their objectives. Across 18 standard-model settings, caps of at most $32/255$ retain a median $97\%$ of attack success relative to each method's native uncapped baseline, while median peak magnitude falls by $80\%$ and median effective support nearly triples. At matched realized $\ell_1$, capped perturbations remain structurally distinct; separate ImageNet comparisons also show lower LPIPS and DISTS at selected caps. Direct in-loop constrained optimization preserves up to $88$ percentage points more attack success than post-hoc clipping, highlighting the importance of constraint enforcement. Suitable caps nevertheless vary across attacks and models, and adversarially trained classifiers exhibit more heterogeneous concentration sensitivity. These results establish perturbation concentration as a complementary, model- and attack-dependent axis of $\ell_1$ attack evaluation.

</details>

### 137. Privacy-preserving inference on hosted models: a learned input-embedding transform for frozen Qwen3.8-27B

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27817)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`隐私推理`、`嵌入变换`、`托管模型`

👤 **作者**：Mahendra Roopa、Srikanth Roopa

- 🎯 **研究动机**：发给托管 LLM 的每条提示对运营方可读，能否让提示留在客户端而主机只收到替身向量
- 🔬 **研究方法**：本地查表把文本转向量，经 55.6M 参数小网络变换后出域；冻结 Qwen3.8-27B 在变换向量上推理，训练用前向 KL 效用项加推离原方向的辅助项
- 📌 **结论**：1490 道 held-out MMLU 上精度损失 1.5 点（80.7% vs 82.3%），30.1% 内容词元经最近邻反查不再解码为原词元

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Every prompt sent to a hosted language model is readable by whoever runs it. We study whether the prompt can remain on the client while the host receives transformed vectors that stand in for it. A language model converts text to vectors exactly once, with a public lookup table; we perform that step locally and pass the vectors through a small trained network (55.6M parameters) before they leave the perimeter. The host runs a frozen 27B model (Qwen3.8-27B) on the transformed vectors and never receives text or token ids. We train the transform against the frozen model as a fixed judge, combining a forward-KL utility term with an auxiliary term that pushes each vector away from its original direction. On 1,490 MMLU questions held out from training, the transform costs 1.5 points of accuracy (80.7% vs. 82.3%), breaking 68 of the 1,226 questions the unmodified model answers correctly (5.5%), while 30.1% of content tokens no longer decode to their original token under a nearest-neighbour lookup.

</details>

### 138. Robustness Analysis of Pareto-based Joint Evaluation of Fairness and Relevance in Recommender Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27850)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`公平性评测`、`帕累托前沿`、`推荐系统`

👤 **作者**：Theresia Veronika Rampisela、Maria Maistro、Tuukka Ruotsalo、Christina Lioma

- 🎯 **研究动机**：推荐系统的相关性与公平性或分别评测或单一指标合并，都不能同时兼顾两维度
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DPFR 以现有度量的相关性-公平性帕累托前沿为基准，到前沿距离作为联合得分；四推荐器×三公平重排器×六真实数据集验证
- 📌 **结论**：现有度量与 DPFR 关联不一致、非帕累托评测的可靠代理，为联合评测提供模块化方法

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recommender Systems (RSs) can be evaluated for many aspects, two of them being recommendation relevance and fairness. These two aspects can be evaluated either in isolation, using a measure for each aspect; or jointly with a single`relevance-aware' fairness measure that quantifies fairness with respect to relevance. Yet, neither approach provides a simultaneous evaluation of both relevance and fairness: the first approach may result in two different rankings of models, each corresponding to an evaluation aspect; the second approach often provides an imbalanced evaluation of the two aspects. To address this gap, we propose Distance to Pareto Frontier (DPFR), a new approach to jointly evaluate RS fairness and relevance. DPFR is based on a Pareto frontier of relevance and fairness scores from existing measures. The frontier is generated from user-item interaction data and represents various Pareto-optimal trade-offs between the two aspects. Thus, the distance from a point in the frontier provides a joint score of fairness and relevance. As DPFR can be used with existing measures, it is modular and intuitive. Our experiments with 4 recommenders and 3 fairness-aware re-rankers on 6 real-world datasets reveal inconsistent associations between existing measures and DPFR, showing that they are not reliable proxies for evaluations based on Pareto optimality. We further examine the robustness of DPFR as a joint evaluation approach for fairness and relevance by conducting experiments with artificial recommendations and with various fairness-relevance trade-offs, as well as by analysing DPFR's agreement to simple measures of fairness and relevance. These analyses show that DPFR is more robust than existing joint evaluation approaches. Finally, we provide guidelines for evaluating fairness and relevance jointly with DPFR. Our code: this https URL.

</details>

### 139. Hallucinations of Large Language Models in Cross-Language Code Translation: Phenomena and Mitigation

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27868)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`代码翻译幻觉`、`分类法`、`缓解框架`

👤 **作者**：Yali Du、Yunji Zhang、Ming Li

- 🎯 **研究动机**：跨语言代码翻译中小语义偏差可静默改变程序行为，其幻觉形态分布与缓解缺乏系统刻画
- 🔬 **研究方法**：三基准六语言对七 LLM 手工标注 13919 例幻觉，构建语义一致性冲突/事实知识冲突/项目上下文偏差三类法，提出 BM25 检索+分类感知反思的缓解
- 📌 **结论**：给出幻觉类型随模型、数据集与翻译方向的变化规律及执行验证的可靠基线

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Code translation converts source code from one programming language to another while preserving its functionality. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have improved code translation, their outputs still contain hallucinations: generated code that conflicts with the source program, violates target-language facts, or introduces contextually inconsistent dependencies. These failures are especially harmful in translation because small semantic deviations can silently change program behavior. Existing hallucination studies mainly focus on natural-language generation or code generation, leaving the forms, distribution, and mitigation of hallucinations in cross-language code translation insufficiently characterized. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of LLM hallucinations in code translation. Across three benchmark datasets, six language pairs, and seven LLMs, we construct a taxonomy with three major categories: Semantic Consistency Conflicts, Factual Knowledge Conflicts, and Project Context Deviations. We manually annotate 13,919 hallucination cases and analyze how hallucination types vary across models, datasets, and language directions. Guided by the taxonomy, we further propose HaluCodeTran, a mitigation framework that combines BM25-based retrieval of relevant translation examples with taxonomy-aware reflective refinement. The study provides an execution-tested characterization of code-translation hallucinations and a practical baseline for building more reliable LLM-based code migration tools.

</details>

### 140. When Do Humans and Machines Truly Complement Each Other? Formal Conditions and Empirical Evidence for Human-AI Synergy

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27965)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`人机协同`、`互补性条件`、`支配陷阱`

👤 **作者**：Andrea Campagner、Federico Cabitza、Vincenzo Crupi

- 🎯 **研究动机**：人机结合能否胜过任一单方（协同/互补性）难以达成，缺乏形式化条件
- 🔬 **研究方法**：以依赖模式刻画人机联合正确性结构，推导协同的充要条件（人与算法互纠错能力），七个临床案例实证
- 📌 **结论**：发现支配陷阱：强算法优势反因侵蚀人类纠正介入而抑制协同；只要错误功能去相关，协同可在自动化偏差等认知偏差下存续

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Can humans and artificial intelligence cooperate such that their combination yields greater accuracy than either agent alone? This result, termed \emph{synergy} or \emph{complementarity}, is notoriously difficult to achieve, even when both agents possess significant independent accuracy. In this paper, we present a formal framework to model human-AI interaction through the lens of reliance patterns, which characterize the joint correctness structure of human and algorithmic decisions. We derive mathematically necessary and sufficient conditions for synergy, demonstrating that its emergence depends on the ability of either agent to identify and correct the errors of the other, what we call Human and Algorithmic Correction. Beyond its theoretical contribution, the proposed framework provides a foundation for the design and evaluation of knowledge-based decision support systems, where interaction policies must dynamically integrate heterogeneous sources of expertise. We demonstrate this through the analysis of seven clinical case studies. Our empirical results reveal a ``dominance trap,'' where a strong algorithmic advantage can paradoxically inhibit synergy by eroding human corrective intervention. We also show that synergy can persist even in the presence of cognitive biases, such as automation bias, provided there is functional de-correlation between human and AI errors. These results provide a formal foundation for key concepts in the human-AI systems literature and offer actionable insights for designing interaction-centric AI systems that foster complementarity.

</details>

### 141. Execution-Time Validation of Context-Carrying Action Intents in Industrial Cyber-Physical Systems

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27971)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`执行间隙`、`动作意图验证`、`工业CPS`

👤 **作者**：Luca D'Agati、Giuseppe Tricomi、Fabio Orazio Mirto、Giovanni Merlino

- 🎯 **研究动机**：工业单元中来自学习策略/操作员/调度器的命令在推理确认与传输后到达控制器时工厂可能已变——命令在不再正确时生效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：动作意图模式 AIS 契约声明决策有效期与假设；运行时 XAIR 发布前复查意图、上下文存储中原子提交授权、版本条件命令仅在控制器状态版本未变时执行；仿真与物理单元（OpenPLC、ARM/RISC-V 板）受控消融
- 📌 **结论**：副本检查总留未保护间隔（提交后数毫秒到百毫秒的暂停可逃逸）；软件控制器扫描内检查或版本条件命令才能闭合该控制器所属状态的间隙，读取范围限定的版本不会过度撤销

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

In industrial cells, commands from learned policies, operators and schedulers reach the controller after inference, confirmation and transport, and the plant may have changed meanwhile, for instance by an operator pause. Such a command is released, or takes effect, when it is no longer correct. We call this the execution gap and ask which component should make the last check, and against which version of which state. The Action Intent Schema (AIS) is a contract in which a producer states how long its decision stays valid and what it assumes. The runtime XAIR rechecks each intent before release, can commit its authorization atomically in the context store, and can release it as a version-conditional command, which the controller executes only if its version of the state is unchanged. In controlled ablations with declared ground truth on emulated and physical cells, OpenPLC and ARM and RISC-V boards, a check on a replica always left an unprotected interval: a pause written from a few to over 100ms after submission escaped it. Only a check in the scan of a software controller, either a controller-local interlock or the version-conditional command, closed the interval for the state that controller owns; only the version-conditional command also refused commands after a pause that had been undone. A global version revoked up to every valid command under unrelated updates. Versions scoped to what the intent reads, or to the truth of its predicates, did not; with continuous signals on the variables the intent reads, only the latter avoided false revocations, though it burdens the store as predicates accumulate. On one embedded-board core, XAIR validated 148-168 intents/s.

</details>

### 142. Media Intelligence Platform: Multimodal Detection of Synthetic and Manipulated Content

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27974)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`深度伪造检测`、`多模态平台`、`取证`

👤 **作者**：David Fischinger、…、Martin Boyer

- 🎯 **研究动机**：生成式 AI 使合成人脸、伪造视频、AI 图像、伪造音频与局部篡改激增，单一篡改类型检测器难以应对异构现实场景
- 🔬 **研究方法**：统一 web 平台集成假视频、人脸 morph、AI 生成图像、音频 deepfake、局部图像篡改与文本分析六类专用模块，模块化架构支持扩展
- 📌 **结论**：代表性基准上跨模态可靠检测并提供取证调查界面，随附公开演示器支持社区评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

The rapid progress of generative artificial intelligence has led to a significant increase in manipulated multimedia content, including synthetic faces, forged videos, AI-generated images, fake audio, and locally edited images. Existing detection approaches are typically designed for a single manipulation type, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios involving heterogeneous media sources. In this work, we present the Media Intelligence Platform (MIP), a unified web-based framework for multimodal media forensics integrating specialized modules for fake video detection, face morphing detection, AI-generated image detection, audio deepfake detection, localized image manipulation detection, and text analysis. Its modular architecture enables flexible integration of heterogeneous models and supports future extensions for emerging manipulation techniques. Experimental results on representative benchmark datasets demonstrate reliable detection performance across multiple media modalities while providing a practical and user-friendly interface for forensic investigations. The platform highlights the potential of unified multimodal systems for comprehensive deepfake detection in real-world environments, and is accompanied by a public demonstrator released with this workshop submission to support reproducibility and community evaluation.

</details>

### 143. Beyond the Evidence Boundary: Behavioral Mirage Under Progressive Degradation in Medical VLMs

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28000)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`行为海市蜃楼`、`渐进退化`、`医学VLM`

👤 **作者**：Edric Castel C. Hao、Rez Samantha Z. Floresca

- 🎯 **研究动机**：医学 VLM 在干净输入上评测，但高风险临床场景存在渐进证据丢失，需确定先前可靠模型-案例何时不再可靠
- 🔬 **研究方法**：基线锚定框架追踪基线正确案例经渐进图像退化后的行为，定义海市蜃楼为证据边界处的持续承诺；三 VLM 三数据集
- 📌 **结论**：MedGemma 海市蜃楼率 16-100%，语言可答性与基线正确及边界承诺相关；残差流探针以 0.895-0.994 AUROC 解码边界处承诺-弃答行为，输出熵不能反映

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

VLMs for medical image interpretation are typically evaluated on clean inputs, despite the progressive evidence loss that can occur in high stakes clinical settings. Existing robustness studies show that performance can degrade while confidence remains high, but do not establish when a previously reliable model-case pair ceases to behave reliably. We introduce a baseline-anchored framework that traces baseline-correct cases through progressive image degradation to identify an empirical evidence boundary, and define mirage as continued commitment at this boundary, regardless of correctness. Across three VLMs on three medical datasets, only MedGemma provides a sufficiently large baseline-correct pool for trajectory analysis. On VQA-RAD, the only dataset in which the question text varies, language-only answerability is associated with baseline correctness (AUROC 0.677) and with commitment at the boundary (AUROC 0.653 to 0.670). MedGemma's post-boundary behavior is heterogeneous, including direct mirage, correct recovery, mirage dissolution, consistent abstention, and oscillation, with mirage rates of 16.0 to 100.0% across dataset-modality pairs. Despite this behavioral heterogeneity, residual-stream probes decode commit-versus-abstain behavior at the boundary with AUROC of 0.895 to 0.994 under nested cross-validation. These results show that progressive evidence loss can produce persistent commitment that output entropy and abstention behavior do not consistently reflect, motivating boundary-aware evaluation, option-order controls, and improved abstention mechanisms for reliable medical foundation models operating under partial observation.

</details>

### 144. Auditing Where Concept Bottleneck Accuracy Comes From on CUB

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28059)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`概念瓶颈模型`、`可解释性审计`、`信息泄露探针`

👤 **作者**：Sascha Saralajew、Eleonora Poeta、Eliana Pastor、Ammar Shaker

- 🎯 **研究动机**：概念瓶颈模型承诺可人工检查纠正的预测，但其准确率来自暴露的概念分数还是共现特征（如整体物种线索）未经审计
- 🔬 **研究方法**：CUB 上审计 CBM/CEM：泄露探针显示 CEM 预测无法从概念分数重构（缺失类信息藏于内部嵌入），识别感受野/共享vs特异表征/范数依赖/任务监督四条信息通道
- 📌 **结论**：限制 ResNet50 为局部 BagNet 补丁使概念分数可恢复精度从 72% 降到 43%（接近真值标注支持），联合训练再抬高但牺牲概念精度——概念监督下的捷径需专门审计

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) promise predictions that humans can inspect and correct through concepts such as "curved beak". Yet on CUB-200-2011, their standard benchmark, progress is measured like that of ordinary classifiers: by task and concept accuracy. Neither reveals whether the label is predicted from the exposed concept scores, or whether a concept is supported by visual evidence of that concept rather than by co-occurring features such as cues to the species as a whole. We audit where the accuracy of CBMs and Concept Embedding Models (CEMs) on CUB comes from. The CUB annotations are too noisy to explain it: class-level training labels agree with per-image annotations on only ~82% of image--concept pairs, and classifiers trained on per-image annotations reach only ~37% species accuracy. Leakage probes show that CEM predictions cannot be reconstructed from their concept scores; the missing class information sits in internal embeddings. We identify four design axes through which class information can enter concept scores---receptive field, shared versus concept-specific representations, norm-dependent scoring, and task supervision. An ablation shows that most of it enters through non-local features: restricting a ResNet50 backbone to local BagNet patches lowers the accuracy recoverable from concept scores from 72% to 43%, close to what ground-truth annotations support, while joint training raises it again at the cost of concept accuracy. Localization and occlusion show that locality makes grounding measurable but does not guarantee it: under class-level labels, local concept scores use the head as a proxy for body, wing, and tail concepts, and CEM concept scores barely respond to occluding their supporting part. Task and concept accuracy should thus support, not drive, the evaluation of CBMs, and given the noisy annotations, CUB accuracy gaps should not be overinterpreted.

</details>

### 145. Same Judgment, Different Yes: Measuring Multilingual Judgment Profiles in Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28066)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多语言判断`、`应答习惯`、`评测方法`

👤 **作者**：Haonan Huang、Yuwei Lu、Chengkun Cai

- 🎯 **研究动机**：多语言评测常对是/非词元计分，而该词元携带模型与语言的应答惯例，何时测的是判断、何时是习惯不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：六语言八模型 110 万条回复，变化问句形式、答案约束与读取方式（词元 vs 句子），FAR-6 道德情景电池
- 📌 **结论**：故事级判断画像跨读取方式收敛（句子间 r=0.94），但否定疑问句后的词元读取中位仅 r=0.67；去除语言共识后模型画像最接近自身其他语言（39/48 格）——模型可以保持判断而改变其“是”

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Multilingual evaluations often score a model's opening yes or no, a token that also carries model and language answering conventions. We ask when different ways of eliciting and reading an answer recover the same judgment, and when they measure an answer habit. We collect 1.1 million replies from eight models in six languages on up to 105 moral vignettes, varying the question's form, the answer constraint, and the reading of the reply, token or sentence, in both wordings (the FAR-6 battery). Story-level judgment profiles converge across the tested readouts and question forms, except for the yes/no token after a negative interrogative read under a preset mapping: on the same replies, token and sentence profiles agree at a median r = 0.67 across cells, while sentence profiles agree with free-sentence profiles at 0.94, a known linguistic variability. With each language's story consensus removed, a model's profile is nearest to its own in another language for 39 of 48 model-language cells under proposition labels and 15 under free sentences, against 5 expected by chance. Particle, position, and named-term preferences follow their own patterns. A model can keep its judgment and change its yes: scoring that token requires establishing which proposition it affirms.

</details>

### 146. BAIT: Can LLMs Find What Biases a Reader? A Benchmark for Cognitive Bias Triggers in News

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28092)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`认知偏差触发`、`新闻偏见`、`评测基准`

👤 **作者**：Yousra Fettach、Tijl De Bie

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 日益中介新闻消费，它们能否识别文本中偏置人类读者的认知偏差触发因素
- 🔬 **研究方法**：BAIT：九类触发因素模式+50 篇英语新闻的训练标注员文章级与 span 级双任务金标，六个前沿 LLM 零样本与少样本评测
- 📌 **结论**：模型接近人类一致性判断文章含哪些触发，但难以恢复完整集合，定位承载段不足人类一致性一半，且对带表层线索的触发过预测——响应表层线索而非机制

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Persuasive writing exploits cognitive biases, recruiting a reader's mental shortcuts to shape belief, often without the reader noticing. We call the linguistic features exploiting such mental shortcuts cognitive bias triggers. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate how people encounter news, summarizing it, ranking it, and answering questions over it, a natural question is whether they can recognize the features of a text that bias a human reader. We introduce BAIT (Bias Annotation In Text), a resource for detecting cognitive bias triggers in news. BAIT pairs a schema of nine triggers, each specified in an annotator codebook, with 50 English news articles annotated by trained annotators at the article and span levels and adjudicated into a gold standard. On this data we define two tasks, detecting which triggers a text contains and locating the spans that carry them. We then benchmark six frontier LLMs zero-shot and with three or ten in-context examples. The models approach human agreement on which triggers an article contains, but rarely recover its full set of triggers, and they reach less than half of human agreement in locating the passages that carry them. Moreover, they over-predict triggers that come with easy surface cues, and most of the passages they mark for these triggers are not triggers, yet carry the trigger's surface cue far more often than random text. These findings suggest that the evaluated LLMs respond to surface cues rather than to the mechanisms that make a passage manipulative. We release the schema, the codebook, standoff annotations, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>

### 147. FIT-MAC: A Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Framework for Robust Vision-Language-Action Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28109)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`VLA 鲁棒性`、`闭环多智能体`、`运行时干预`

👤 **作者**：Jiatong Li、…、Bin Hu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：物体位置或指令扰动可使 VLA 策略完全失败，缺显式运行时机制区分失效模式并选恢复策略
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FIT-MAC 推理时控制层：规划者解析结构化任务规范、接地者消解指令引用、验证者区分目标绑定错误与局部执行失败并决定干预、纠正者路由到目标重绑或有界抓放恢复
- 📌 **结论**：LIBERO-PRO 上跨多个 VLA 骨干提升鲁棒性，无需干预时预训练 VLA 保持默认动作源

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance on standard robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their reliability can degrade substantially when task conditions deviate from the nominal setting. Perturbations in object positions and task instructions may lead to complete task failure. Existing VLA policies generally lack an explicit runtime mechanism for distinguishing these failure modes and selecting an appropriate recovery strategy. To systematically mitigate this issue, we propose FIT-MAC, a failure-aware, closed-loop multi-agent control framework for improving the robustness of VLA policies at inference time. FIT-MAC augments a VLA policy with an inference-time control layer. First, the Planner Agent parses the language instruction into a structured task specification that captures the manipulation goal and spatial constraints, and the Grounder Agent resolves each instruction-level reference to a visual target. Next, the Verifier Agent tracks phase-specific execution progress, distinguishes target-binding errors from local execution failures, and determines whether intervention is warranted. The Correction Agent then routes permitted interventions to target rebinding or bounded grasping or placement recovery, depending on the failure type. When no intervention is required, the pretrained VLA remains the default action generator. Extensive experiments on LIBERO-PRO demonstrate that FIT-MAC improves robustness across multiple VLA backbones, consistently increasing success rates and achieving state-of-the-art performance under diverse perturbations.

</details>

### 148. Digital Nudge Effects in Optimal Stopping Games: Antagonistic Interactions in Layered Choice Architectures

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28154)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`数字助推`、`挤出效应`、`选择架构`

👤 **作者**：Batisse Manhardt、Ja-Nae Duane

- 🎯 **研究动机**：数字助推被广泛部署，但多个助推组合 deployed together 的相互作用知之甚少
- 🔬 **研究方法**：秘书问题 2³ 被试间因子设计（N=181）测锚定、显著性与社会证明单独与组合对停止行为与结果质量的影响
- 📌 **结论**：首次 controlled 证据表明数字助推挤出效应：锚定+社会证明组合结果质量最差且随助推数量单调下降，对界面伦理设计有直接含义

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Digital nudges have become a prominent area of research in human-centered interaction, yet little is known about how they interact when deployed together. This study examines the individual and combined effects of anchoring, salience, and social proof digital nudges on stopping behavior and outcome quality in a sequential decision task. Using a 2^3 between-subjects factorial design grounded in the secretary problem (N = 181), we find that (1) an anchor nudge significantly increased exploration rather than constraining it (b = 1.207, p = .017); (2) the salience nudge produced the smallest effect of any condition; and (3) combining the anchor and social proof nudges produced the worst outcome quality of any condition ($181.83 vs. $192.21 for anchor-only), with outcome quality declining monotonically as nudge count increased. These findings provide the first controlled evidence for a digital nudge crowding-out effect with direct implications for the ethical design of digital interfaces.

</details>

### 149. Africa-Papers: A 10-Billion-Token Multi-Domain Academic Corpus for African Foundation Language Models

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28173)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`非洲语料`、`基础模型`、`多语言资源`

👤 **作者**：Mich-Seth Owusu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：主流预训练语料中非洲学术文献不足 0.5%，导致热带流行病学、本地农业生态等领域系统性失效
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Africa-Papers 汇聚 DOAJ 非洲期刊、CGSpace 农业、arXiv 非洲相关预印本与 OpenAlex 非洲机构发表，84.5 万篇全文 101.5 亿词元，布局感知清洗保结构
- 📌 **结论**：最大泛非学术预训练语料，为非洲基础模型与多语言安全就绪提供数据基础

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Large Language Models (LLMs) depend fundamentally on the diversity, depth, and factual rigor of their pre-training corpora. However, contemporary foundational pre-training datasets (such as The Pile, S2ORC, and Dolma) exhibit a severe structural omission: African scientific, medical, legal, and agricultural scholarship constitutes less than 0.5% of global pre-training tokens. Consequently, state-of-the-art models suffer from systematic failure modes when reasoning about tropical epidemiology, localized smallholder agro-ecologies, regional economic governance, and indigenous linguistic structures. To resolve this representation deficit and advance African intellectual sovereignty in artificial intelligence, we introduce Africa-Papers, the largest curated multi-domain Pan-African academic pre-training corpus to date. Comprising over 844,807 full-text papers spanning 10.15 billion tokens across 879 Parquet shards, Africa-Papers unifies four primary scientific streams: (1) continental peer-reviewed journals indexed in DOAJ across 31 African nations, (2) the CGIAR CGSpace agricultural repository, (3) African-authored and African-focused STEM/AI preprints on arXiv, and (4) open-access international publications by scholars affiliated with African institutions indexed via OpenAlex. The entire corpus is processed into layout-aware, clean Markdown preserving section hierarchies, mathematical equations, and tabular data, accompanied by exact BPE token counts and a standardized 8-domain taxonomy. We release Africa-Papers openly on the Hugging Face Hub under the AfriSpeech organization to accelerate continual pre-training and domain evaluation for foundation models globally.

</details>

### 150. Uncovering Audio-Text Alignment in Large Audio-Language Models through Cross-Modal Concept Neurons

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28189)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`跨模态概念神经元`、`音频语言模型`、`因果干预`

👤 **作者**：Tzu-Quan Lin、Chih-Kai Yang、Yi-Cheng Lin、Hao Tang、Hung-yi Lee

- 🎯 **研究动机**：大型音频语言模型的语言骨干仅经文本预训练，音频表征如何与文本表征整合不明
- 🔬 **研究方法**：假设共享子空间对齐，识别同时表征音频与文本的跨模态神经元，做转向与失活的因果干预
- 📌 **结论**：三个 LALM 四类声学属性上不同程度对齐，跨模态神经元不仅预测必需且可被操纵

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

While the language model backbone is pretrained on text alone, large audio-language models (LALMs) can, to a great extent, recognize acoustic attributes, such as emotion, speaker gender, and spoken language. However, it is unclear how the audio representation is integrated, if at all, with the other text representation before the model produces text. We hypothesize that there is a shared subspace where the audio and text representations are aligned. To test this hypothesis, we identify cross-modal neurons that are responsible for representing both audio and text. We find alignment between audio and text neurons to various degrees across three LALMs and four acoustic attribute types. Causal interventions, steering and deactivating the neurons, confirm that the cross-modal neurons are not just necessary for prediction but can also be manipulated.

</details>

### 151. Can Language Models Get "Aphasia" From Neuronal Ablation?

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28214)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`神经元消融`、`失语症`、`架构失效模式`

👤 **作者**：Pratik Worah、Randall Bateman

- 🎯 **研究动机**：神经元渐进丢失是神经退行性疾病标志，语言模型消融可作认知衰退的计算模型
- 🔬 **研究方法**：系统消融 LSTM 与 Transformer 的神经元并以有限微调模拟代偿可塑性，比较损伤曲线与错误形态
- 📌 **结论**：Transformer 在 MLP 消融下晚期阈值式崩塌并出现语义替换（类 Wernicke 失语），注意力头消融呈平滑 sigmoid 语义特异性丧失（类 svPPA），前馈与注意通路失效模式功能分离

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Progressive neuronal loss is the hallmark of neurodegenerative disease, yet how loss of neurons shapes the pattern of cognitive decline remains poorly understood. Here we systematically ablate neurons (computational units) in language models spanning recurrent long short-term memory (LSTM) and Transformer architectures and apply limited fine-tuning as a simple proxy for compensatory plasticity. With whole-unit ablation, LSTMs degrade gradually with syntactic and semantic faculties declining in parallel across mild-to-moderate damage. By contrast, Transformers exhibit late threshold-like collapse under feedforward multi-layer perceptron (MLP) ablation and smoother sigmoid-like decline under attention-head ablation. Qualitatively, MLP-ablated models continue to make within-category semantic substitutions (semantic paraphasias) alongside rising non-responses, paralleling Wernicke-type aphasia, while attention-head ablation produces a trend toward loss of semantic specificity similar to the semantic variant of primary progressive aphasia (svPPA). These findings indicate that feedforward and attentional pathways have functionally distinct failure modes, suggesting that architecture-dependent failure profiles may offer new computational perspectives on aphasic syndromes and neurodegenerative diseases as an in silico model. Notably, early accuracy retention under Transformer attention-head ablation qualitatively parallels pre-symptomatic reserve before the 40--60% neuronal loss reported by Gomez-Isla et al. (1996) when clinical symptoms emerge in Alzheimer's disease, whereas the steadier LSTM decline parallels the symptomatic progression described by DeKosky and Scheff (1990) and Terry et al. (1991).

</details>

### 152. TPO: Tri-level Distributionally Robust Learning for OOD Direct Preference Optimization

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28229)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`分布鲁棒DPO`、`OOD偏好`、`层级模糊集`

👤 **作者**：Chengtao Jian、Kai Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：DPO 隐含假设训练偏好分布与部署一致，实际存在组级、提示级、偏好级三层自然移位，威胁安全部署
- 🔬 **研究方法**：TPO 三层分布鲁棒框架：层级结构化矩形模糊集联合建模三层不确定性，Wasserstein 实例化得闭式偏好边际惩罚、提示级对抗嵌入扰动与最坏组再加权的可解目标，附有限样本收敛保证
- 📌 **结论**：在 OOD 偏好对齐上系统优于现有鲁棒 DPO 方法

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) minimizes empirical risk over observed preference comparisons, implicitly assuming that the training preference distribution matches the deployment preference distribution. In practice, this i.i.d. assumption often fails, as preference distribution shift exhibits a natural hierarchical structure across three levels: group-level shift, prompt-level shift, and preference-level shift. Such multi-level distributional mismatch poses a critical challenge for safe LLM deployment, yet existing robust DPO methods typically either model uncertainty over the joint preference distribution or focus on individual sources of shift, leaving the hierarchical structure of OOD preference shifts underexplored. We propose TPO, a tri-level distributionally robust framework for OOD preference alignment, which jointly models all three levels of distributional uncertainty through a hierarchically structured rectangular ambiguity set. By instantiating it with Wasserstein distance, we derive a tractable and interpretable objective that combines a closed-form preference-level margin penalty, prompt-level adversarial embedding perturbation, and worst-case group reweighting, each governed by an independent radius. We establish finite-sample convergence guarantees and empirically validate the effectiveness of TPO over existing robust DPO methods.

</details>

### 153. Safe Multi-Robot Collaboration With Uniform Exclusion Using Trust

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28251)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`多机器人安全`、`拜占庭链路排除`、`随时有效推断`

👤 **作者**：Thomas Kaminsky、Áron Vékássy、Hammad Izhar、Angelia Nedić、Stephanie Gil

- 🎯 **研究动机**：机器人集群经时变局部通信协调，部分链路可能被攻陷传输任意恶意数据
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Safe Wrapper 包装通信：用各机器人异常检测器的信任值流做随时有效推断选出可信链路子集，仅要求检测器好于随机
- 📌 **结论**：以用户选定概率一致排除全部被攻陷链路同时快速学会信任合法链路；3 台 LoCoBot 与 8 台 GoPiGo 硬件验证并可扩展到数百机器人

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Robot swarms coordinate through directed, time-varying local communication links, some of which may be compromised to allow the transmission of arbitrary malicious data. We provide an algorithm that acts as a wrapper around robot communication---when a robot seeks to update its state using data from some incoming links, our wrapper provides a subset of trusted links whose data can be safely incorporated. It selects these links by testing a stream of trust values---data collected from an anomaly detector on each robot---using Safe Anytime-Valid Inference. We require only that the detector be better than chance. This wrapper ensures safe and performant autonomy by providing two powerful statistical guarantees: it uniformly excludes all compromised links for all time with high user-chosen probability, but nevertheless quickly learns to trust legitimate links. Both guarantees are vital to secure generic tasks, but no previous work provides them simultaneously without restrictive assumptions on connectivity structure or the number of compromised links that are not guaranteed in practice. We study our Safe Wrapper's performance in hardware for two contrasting tasks---neural implicit mapping and heading consensus---deploying on 3 LoCoBots and 8 GoPiGos, respectively. We provide simulations using trust values sampled from hardware to validate that our approach scales well to swarms of hundreds of robots.

</details>

### 154. Large Multimodal Models for Direct Robot Control: GPT-6 as a Beginning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28259)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`前沿模型评测`、`直接机器人控制`、`具身差距`

👤 **作者**：Youwei Zhou、…、Zhihe Lu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：通用多模态模型直接闭环控制机械臂的能力边界不清
- 🔬 **研究方法**：探测 GPT-6 Astra 深度感知、具身上下文学习与触觉理解三项基本能力，五基准六能力维度评测，并建几何约束 LIBERO-GeoMotion
- 📌 **结论**：多个基准具竞争力（RoboTwin 2.0 76.0%）但语义推理与物理执行差距显著，几何约束任务零样本仅 11.1%，后果感知动作规划与精细协调是短板

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Recent advances in visuospatial understanding and agentic reasoning enable direct robotic manipulation with general-purpose multimodal models, but their embodied capabilities remain poorly understood. This paper systematically evaluate GPT-6 Astra for closed-loop robotic manipulation using a lightweight agent execution framework. We first probe three fundamental embodied capabilities-depth perception, embodied in-context learning, and tactile understanding-and demonstrate that the model can exploit depth information, acquire simple manipulation strategies from contextual demonstrations, and use tactile feedback to correct its actions. We then evaluate GPT-6 Astra across five established manipulation benchmarks along six capability dimensions. Despite competitive performance on several benchmarks-26.92\% success on RoboDojo and 76.0\% on RoboTwin 2.0-our analysis reveals a pronounced gap between semantic reasoning and physical execution, particularly in consequence-aware action planning, precision, and complex motion coordination. To further examine these limitations, we introduce LIBERO-GeoMotion, a new benchmark of geometry-constrained tasks that require precise pose alignment, coordinated translation and rotation, and anticipation of physical action consequences. GPT-6 Astra achieves only 11.1\% zero-shot success, exposing the difficulty of translating spatial understanding into physically feasible motion. Real-world experiments corroborate these findings: zero-shot execution is feasible for simple tasks, yet persistent deficits remain in motion feasibility, precise control, and physical safety, while substantial inference latency further hinders practical deployment. Overall, our results characterize the emerging embodied capabilities of general-purpose multimodal models and delineate the key challenges toward safe, efficient, and reliable robotic manipulation.

</details>

### 155. Local Work, Global Progress: A Convergence Theory of AI Agent Collaboration

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28286)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`协作收敛理论`、`块优化`、`多智能体`

👤 **作者**：Ivan Ilin、Peter Richtárik

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM agent 团队协作协议多为经验设计，缺乏收敛保证
- 🔬 **研究方法**：把协作求解形式化为块优化：各 agent 改进共享解的一部分并补偿对他部分的影响，协调者校验提案对全局目标并合并不相干扰的提案，证明到误差地板的线性收敛
- 📌 **结论**：GPT-OSS-120B 机器人路由上三 agent 团队每次找到无碰撞计划、六 agent 搜索快一倍，实测常数下界每轮成立；干扰提案经组合校验后再合并可再省 30% 时间

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Teams of large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to complex tasks, but their collaboration protocols are mostly designed empirically, without convergence guarantees. We formalize collaborative problem solving as block optimization: each agent improves one part of a shared solution, guided by its local objective plus a correction for how its changes affect the other parts. A coordinator checks every proposal against the global objective and merges proposals that do not interfere with each other. We prove linear convergence up to an error floor that vanishes when the corrections are exact. Interfering proposals can also be merged once their combination has been checked; the rate then depends on how well the proposals actually combine, not on worst-case interactions. With GPT-OSS-120B agents on robot routing, a three-agent team finds collision-free plans in every run, better than those of a single agent that controls all robots. On a larger routing task, a six-agent team completes its search about twice as fast as the single agent (in median). Merging the best checked combination of proposals, rather than only proposals that do not interfere, cuts the team's search time by about 30%. With constants measured on the runs, our convergence bound holds in every round and follows the observed progress. We also test the theory on query planning in SQLite and on a synthetic task.

</details>

### 156. DeclineCraft: A RAG Abstention Benchmark for Jointly Measuring Combined Retrieval and Parametric Knowledge

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28340)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`RAG 弃答`、`参数知识`、`基准`

👤 **作者**：Atharva Thakur、Abulhair Saparov、Bruno Ribeiro

- 🎯 **研究动机**：可靠 RAG 需识别检索文档与参数知识都不支持答案的时刻
- 🔬 **研究方法**：DeclineCraft 以语料截止后 Minecraft 新特性的动态弃答基准（附参数暴露审计），27 个 LLM 测正确弃答率、可答尝试率与准确率
- 📌 **结论**：单次检索在 38/40 配置提升弃答；但迭代 RAG 反而在 30/40 配置降低弃答（均值 46.4%）且准确率几乎不变；开思考使 13 模型中 8 个弃答显著下降并从承认不确定转向前提否认

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Reliable retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires recognizing when neither retrieved documents nor parametric knowledge supports an answer. We introduce DeclineCraft, a dynamic abstention benchmark whose unanswerable questions target Minecraft feature additions after a corpus cut-off, with audits of possible parametric exposure. Later cut-offs support evaluation of future models. Across 27 LLMs, we measure Correct Abstention Rate (CAR; how often models acknowledge insufficient knowledge on unanswerable questions), Answerable Attempt Rate and Answer Accuracy. We find that (1) single-shot retrieval significantly raises CAR in 38 of 40 configurations (model and thinking setting) versus no retrieval. (2) Iterative RAG, in which the model self-assesses its context and can request up to two further retrieval rounds, significantly lowers CAR versus single-shot RAG in 30 of 40 configurations (mean 46.4%), with little change in Answer Accuracy (68.5% to 69.3%). Adding query rewriting and reranking to hybrid (dense & sparse) retrieval lowers CAR for all 12 models tested. (3) Larger models answer more accurately (Spearman $\rho = +0.60$ with total parameters), but neither model size nor release date shows a significant association with CAR. (4) Without retrieval, enabling thinking on the same weights lowers CAR significantly for 8 of 13 models (by 14% points on average), and the responses shift from acknowledged uncertainty to premise denial.

</details>

### 157. Characterizing Cross-Relay Generalization Failure in Machine Learning-Based Power System Protection

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28348)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`安全关键ML`、`跨继电器泛化`、`误报结构`

👤 **作者**：Nithin Pradeep Nadayil Kizhakkethil、Julian Oelhaf、Christian Bergler、Andreas Maier、Siming Bayer

- 🎯 **研究动机**：ML 故障检测器常在训练所用继电器位置评测，而局部电压电流测量随位置变化
- 🔬 **研究方法**：110kV 仿真网络 50ms 窗口，8 继电器×64 源-目标组合×5 种子的跨继电器评估，辅以线性探针与类条件潜分布分析
- 📌 **结论**：F1 几乎不变（0.730→0.734）但平均假阳性率从 0.088 升到 0.341 且强方向性（Bus 2 训练模型到 Bus 1/3 的 FPR 0.670）——聚合指标掩盖位置结构化的误报变化，安全关键 ML 须分错误类型评测

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Machine learning-based fault detectors are often evaluated at the relay locations used for training, although local voltage and current measurements vary across power networks. This work investigates cross-relay generalization for binary detection of faults anywhere in the network using local measurements, rather than line-selective tripping. Using 50 ms windows from a simulated 110 kV transmission network, we evaluate a multilayer perceptron across all 64 source target combinations of eight relays and five random seeds. A shared simulation-level split and source-only threshold selection provide a controlled held-out evaluation without target-specific calibration. Despite nearly unchanged mean F1-scores (0.730 within-relay versus 0.734 cross-relay), the mean false-positive rate (FPR) increases from 0.088 to 0.341, while the mean false-negative rate decreases from 0.399 to 0.318. The change is strongly directional: models trained at Bus 2 relays yield a mean FPR of 0.670 at Bus 1 and Bus 3 relays, compared with 0.096 for cross-relay transfers within the same bus group. Linear probing indicates that learned representations retain coarse bus-group information, while exploratory class-conditional latent distribution analysis shows no consistent monotonic association with transfer errors. These findings demonstrate that aggregate metrics can conceal location-structured changes in false-alarm behavior, highlighting the importance of evaluating error types separately when assessing cross-relay generalization.

</details>

### 158. FALCA: Falsification-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Hierarchical Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Deep Search Agents

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28361)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`证伪感知`、`深度搜索agent`、`分层信用分配`

👤 **作者**：Zijun Dou、Kun Luo、Chaofan Li、Shuqi Lu、Zheng Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长程深度搜索中早期错误被放大：锚定错误候选、收集偏证、忽视矛盾
- 🔬 **研究方法**：FALCA 把深度搜索形式化为提议-证伪过程：搜索者提议候选、独立证伪者主动找反例查约束出冲突报告修订研究状态；证伪感知训练=SFT 热身+并行 rollout 的 agent RL 与研究阶段/状态转移/动作的分层信用分配
- 📌 **结论**：五个搜索基准上超越骨干与 harness 消融，降低确认偏差与错误累积

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-horizon deep search requires LLM agents to iteratively decompose complex information needs, gather evidence from external sources, and maintain a reliable research state under multiple constraints. A central challenge is that early mistakes can be amplified over the search trajectory: agents may anchor on an incorrect candidate, collect partially supporting evidence, overlook contradictions, and accumulate errors that misguide subsequent reasoning. We propose FALCA, a falsification-aware framework that formulates deep search as a propose-falsify process. FALCA adopts a collaborative Searcher-Falsifier architecture, where the Searcher proposes candidate answers or sub-answers, while independent Falsifiers actively seek counterevidence, check unmet constraints, identify conflicts, and produce concise falsification reports for research-state revision. To strengthen this loop, we introduce falsification-aware training, combining SFT warmup on falsification trajectories with agentic RL based on parallel rollouts and hierarchical credit assignment over research stages, state transitions, and actions. Experiments on five search benchmarks show that FALCA improves long-horizon deep search performance over backbone and harness ablations, while reducing confirmation bias and error accumulation through more effective falsification and research-state revision.

</details>

### 159. The Instruction Tax on Factual Recall: Base Models Can Beat Their Instruction-Tuned Counterparts

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28407)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`指令税`、`事实回忆`、`基础vs微调`

👤 **作者**：Christophe Khalil、Aya Mourad

- 🎯 **研究动机**：指令微调对事实回忆的影响可在受控共享任务上分离
- 🔬 **研究方法**：AKBC LM-KBC 2026 方案：≤32B 模型无检索无微调生成主体-关系全部客体力；同一权重比较指令版聊天界面与基础模型文本补全
- 📌 **结论**：指令版 0.5681 而基础模型补全 0.6836（基线 0.2964 的两倍多），指令微调降低事实回忆——指令税源于权重而非提示方式

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

We present our solution to the AKBC LM-KBC 2026 Shared Task, where we achieved a score of 0.6836 on the official leaderboard, more than double the baseline of 0.2964. This task requires generating all possible objects for subject-relation pairs using a language model with up to 32 billion parameters, with no retrieval or fine-tuning involved. We show that the prompting methodology is as important as the model architecture: while the instruction-tuned model version achieves 0.5681 with its chat interface, the base model achieves 0.6836 when prompted using text completion. Thus, instruction tuning decreases factual recall; we refer to this effect as the instruction tax, which is caused by the model's weights rather than the prompting method. Our final solution is fairly straightforward: we prompt the base model with some examples from the training data, generate 64--256 outputs, and retain only the repeated ones. Two specialized methods are then applied for each relation: one improves the recall of large answer sets, and the other verifies a person's vitality before predicting their place of death.

</details>

### 160. EvaOmni: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Audio-Visual Reasoning

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28444)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`证据锚定评测`、`全模态推理`、`捷径过滤`

👤 **作者**：Xiaoxing Hu、…、Xue Yang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：全模态 LLM 的选择题评测只按答案对错计分，难判断模型是否真形成从证据到答案的推理链，文本捷径可假性答对
- 🔬 **研究方法**：EvaOmni 392 视频 1027 条纯人工标注问答，专家标注音视频证据并构造视频干扰项、人工审查与模型过滤单模态捷径；要求同时返回答案与支持证据区间，提出覆盖接地分与瓶颈接地分
- 📌 **结论**：评测完整证据-推理链而非仅终答，揭示捷径对基准可靠性的削弱

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Omni LLMs can naturally locate key audio and visual evidence and integrate information across modalities for audiovisual reasoning. Existing audio-visual reasoning benchmarks that mainly rely on multiple-choice questions usually score models solely based on answer correctness, making it difficult to assess whether a model truly forms a reasoning chain from evidence to the correct answer. In addition, textual or unimodal shortcuts may allow models to answer correctly without analyzing the necessary audio-visual evidence, further weakening the reliability of evaluation benchmarks. To address these issues, we introduce EvaOmni, an evidence-grounded benchmark containing 392 videos and 1,027 purely human-annotated question-answer pairs, covering 15 capabilities and three levels: retrieval and localization, basic understanding, and complex reasoning. We design a series of rigorous annotation procedures in which experts annotate audio and visual evidence and construct video-based distractors; humans review whether questions meet strict inspection criteria, and finally models filter textual and unimodal shortcuts. Beyond high-quality question-answer pairs, we require models to simultaneously return the answer and supporting evidence intervals in order to evaluate the complete reasoning chain. We propose the Coverage Grounded Score (C.G.) to measure how much annotated evidence a model identifies, and the Bottleneck Grounded Score (B.G.) to penalize poor temporal localization in either modality; both are incorporated into answer correctness. Across 7 open-weight and 17 proprietary Omni LLMs, Gemini3.7-Flash achieved the highest overall accuracy (79.4%), C.G. (70.8%), and B.G. (35.1%). At Level 3, it reached 79.1% accuracy, but its B.G. was only 12.3%. These results show that even the most advanced models still have room for development in grounded audio-visual reasoning.

</details>

### 161. Answering in the User's Variety: Calibrated Accommodation Pairs for Post-Training

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28474)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`方言适应`、`校准配对`、`后训练审计`

👤 **作者**：Zhizhuo Kou、…、Yike Guo

- 🎯 **研究动机**：聊天模型对不同方言用户一律标准英语回复，统一回复掩盖训练是否同等对待这些用户，输出审计也无法分辨
- 🔬 **研究方法**：跨 10 个开源谱系追踪 SFT/DPO/RL 各阶段改变匹配用户倾向的方式，从内容匹配的双回复读出更新偏好；提出校准适应对 CAP 偏好数据奖励匹配用户方言密度同时为标准英语用户保留标准英语
- 📌 **结论**：DPO 更新在全部 10 谱系偏向英澳拼写而非 AAE 与新加坡英语；CAP 未训特征上提升收敛且不给标准英语用户加方言、不损指令遵循——审计应读更新而非仅输出

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

People adapt how they talk to the person in front of them. However, chat models tend to respond in Standard English regardless of the dialect used by the user. A uniform answer can hide whether training treats these users alike, and output audits cannot tell as well. We trace how each post-training stage, from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to preference optimization (DPO) and reinforcement learning, changes a model's tendency to match the user, and read what each update prefers from two content-matched replies, one matching the user and one not. Across 10 open model lineages, post-training standardizes replies to every informal cue, yet after adjusting for how likely each reply already was, the DPO update still favours matching British and Australian spelling over AAE and Singlish on 10 lineages, and models still produce AAE features when asked. We then introduce calibrated accommodation pairs (CAP), preference data that reward matching users' dialect density while preserving Standard English for Standard-English users. On a fully open model, CAP raises convergence on features it was not trained on without a detectable increase in dialect for Standard-English users or a cost in instruction following. In contrast, one-sided pairs reach a similar gain only by overshooting the user and imposing dialect. Our findings show that uniform replies can hide selective updates and audits should read the update as well as the output. Code: this https URL.

</details>

### 162. Claims of emergence in AI should be grounded in information decomposition

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28479)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`涌现判据`、`信息分解`、`协同信息`

👤 **作者**：Christoph Riedl、Fernando E. Rosas

- 🎯 **研究动机**：涌现/协同判断多为非正式评估，无法比较甚至无法有意义地争论
- 🔬 **研究方法**：主张信息分解作为统一形式框架：区分独特、冗余与协同信息，涌现的精确可证伪判据是系统行为携带任务相关的协同信息；应用于注意力头、自主 agent 与人机团队
- 📌 **结论**：为跨尺度涌现主张组织现有知识并生成跨层猜想，把 AI 安全中的涌现争论落到可操作判据

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

When a language model develops capabilities not traceable to its individual components, when a multi-agent AI system outperforms each agent on its own, or when a human-AI pair succeeds where either fails in isolation, AI researchers describe it as a case of ``synergy'' or ``emergence''. However, these are usually informal assessments not supported by a theoretical framework, making these claims impossible to compare or even meaningfully dispute. Here we argue that \emph{information decomposition can serve as a unifying formal framework to operationalize assessments about synergy in AI systems}. Information decomposition provides a way to distinguish qualitatively different \emph{types} of information, including unique information available from individual components, redundant information shared across multiple components, and synergistic information accessible only from parts taken together. This gives claims of emergence a precise, falsifiable criterion: a system's behavior is emergent only if it carries synergistic information about task-relevant outcomes. We apply this lens to attention heads, autonomous agents, and human-AI teams, showing how it organizes existing knowledge across scales and substrates, while also generating cross-level conjectures that siloed fields would miss.

</details>

### 163. OptionWorld: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Failures of LLMs as Long-Term Personal Assistants

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28491)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`长期助手`、`失败诊断`、`任务状态维护`

👤 **作者**：Ruoyu Zhang、Zhaocen Liu、Weiyi Tian、Melissa Feng、Shilong Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：长期助理须跨天跟随未完任务，但找到信息或启动动作不等于任务完成
- 🔬 **研究方法**：OptionWorld 追踪模拟日常任务的信息获取、判断、动作与结果，定位 LLM 短板；失败实例中关键事实在期限前最后输入仍存在者占 35.5%
- 📌 **结论**：三类高频问题：所需信息未获取或整合、可用行动机会未被利用、agent 强加任务并不要求的限制——短板在任务状态维护而非能力

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Long-term assistants must follow unfinished tasks across days as information and circumstances change. Finding information or starting an action, however, does not ensure that the task gets completed. We introduce OptionWorld, a benchmark that traces information acquisition, judgments, actions, and outcomes in simulated daily-life tasks to identify where current LLMs fall short as long-term personal assistants. In runs with archived decision inputs, the key facts remain in the model's last input before the deadline in 35.5% of failed instances. Failure analysis identifies the three most frequent problems: needed information is not obtained or combined, available opportunities to act go unused, and agents impose restrictions that the task does not require. Three follow-up experiments show that supplying missing facts restores task success in some cases; action reminders can restart work while subsequent steps still miss the deadline; and correcting a supplied note that wrongly forbids contact restores permitted inquiry. These findings locate shortcomings in maintaining task state: connecting facts to unfinished work, following through before deadlines, and separating actual restrictions from the agent's assumptions.

</details>

### 164. Copyright Leakage Detection in AI Generated Videos

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28524)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`版权泄露检测`、`AI 生成视频`、`对比检索`

👤 **作者**：Shaif Chowdhury、Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy

- 🎯 **研究动机**：视频生成模型可能复现受版权视觉内容，但生成视频的版权泄露检测基本未探
- 🔬 **研究方法**：50 个版权概念×5 图构建基准（图+提示与纯提示两种生成），把泄露检测形式化为视频帧聚合嵌入对版权图像库的检索匹配，单相似度阈值返回最相似参照为证据
- 📌 **结论**：检测准确率 99.6% 仅一个假阴性，提供可扩展可解释的版权泄露检测工具与公开数据代码

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Video generation models can create realistic-looking video footage from images and text prompts. Some of the recent advances have raised concerns about the reproduction of copyrighted visual content. However, copyright leakage detection in generated videos remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new benchmark data set for copyright leakage detection, comprising 50 copyrighted concepts, each with 5 images. For each concept, we have 5 videos created using the image+prompt video generation model and the prompt-only video generation model. Following that, we develop an AI toolset based on a contrastive retrieval framework that formulates copyright leakage detection as a video-to-image retrieval problem by matching aggregated video frame embeddings against a database of copyrighted image embeddings. Our method uses a single similarity threshold and retrieves the most similar copyrighted reference as supporting evidence. We conducted experiments showing strong retrieval and detection performance, achieving 99.6\% detection accuracy with only a single false negative. These results show the effectiveness of the retrieval-based method for scalable and explainable copyright leakage detection in AI-generated videos. Data can be accessed publicly from this https URL and the source code for this project is publicly available at this https URL.

</details>

### 165. Evolving Agent Interfaces

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28601)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`agent 接口演化`、`自改进`、`计算机使用`

👤 **作者**：Saaket Agashe、…、Xin Eric Wang

- 🎯 **研究动机**：计算机使用 agent 被人类给定的接口限制（逐格填表 vs 一行代码），接口与其使用知识都靠手工无法跟上变化——接口设计本身应成为 agent 能力
- 🔬 **研究方法**：Agent S4 元 agent 无成功标签地检视任务轨迹，构建修订接口与使用知识库；种群演化按验证任务表现选择知识库，偏好超越已见任务的泛化
- 📌 **结论**：OSWorld V2 上以 Qwen3.8-27B+演化接口库达开源新 SOTA 21.3%（同模型固定接口 3.8 倍），高于 GPT-5.5 与 Claude Opus 4.8 且成本为其 4.3%/1.5%——自主接口设计亦可自改进

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Computer-use agents are limited by the interfaces humans give them. An agent may type a spreadsheet cell by cell when a single line of code would do, or print all two thousand lines of a file through a shell to find one function. Both the interfaces and the knowledge of when to use each are built by hand, which cannot keep pace with new applications, workflows, and models. We argue that interface design should itself be an agent capability. We introduce Agent S4, an autonomous computer-use scientist that builds Evolving Agent Interfaces from task experience. A meta-agent examines task agents' trajectories on synthetic training tasks, without success labels, and constructs and revises interfaces and usage knowledge in a repository. Population-based evolution then selects which repositories to develop further by their performance on validation tasks the meta-agent never sees, favoring changes that generalize beyond the seen tasks. On OSWorld V2, Agent S4, with Qwen3.8-27B and the evolved repository of interfaces, sets a new state of the art in open-source computer-use agents, reaching 21.3% binary score, 3.8 times the same model with fixed interfaces. This is above both GPT-5.5 (13.0%) and Claude Opus 4.8 (20.6%), at about 4.3% and 1.5% of their respective costs. On ScienceBoard, the evolved interfaces improve the success rate from 41.4% to 58.6% with the same model. These results suggest that useful interfaces can be evolved from experience, making autonomous interface design a source of improvement complementary to advances in models and harnesses. We open-source our code at this https URL.

</details>

### 166. Value-Rich, Differently Organized: Measuring Value Expression and Organization in AI-Generated Debates

📄 [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28604)　📅 2026-10

**关键词**：`价值表达`、`辩论评测`、`Schwartz价值论`

👤 **作者**：Maoyuan Li、Haoyuan Li、Zhongsheng Wang、Jiamou Liu

- 🎯 **研究动机**：LLM 辩论评测聚焦论证质量，生成论证的价值结构未被检视
- 🔬 **研究方法**：基于 Schwartz 十价值论把辩论表示为论证单元-话轮-立场-全文的多层价值分布，定义价值覆盖、对齐、扩散与跨轮动态指标
- 📌 **结论**：AI 辩论价值表达广（常不低于人类参照）但组织方式不同：话轮更局部价值弥散，盲源裁判可分价值清晰度与价值聚焦——辩论评测应纳入价值组织

<details>
<summary>📝 展开完整英文摘要（Abstract）</summary>

Current evaluation of LLM-generated debate largely focuses on argumentative quality, leaving the value structure of generated arguments underexamined. We introduce a diagnostic metric framework for comparing how values are expressed and organized in human-reference and AI-generated debates. The framework separates value expression, the range of recognizable values a debate invokes, from value organization, how those values are distributed and sustained across turns. Grounded in Schwartz's Basic-10 value theory, it represents debates as multi-level value distributions over argumentative units, utterances, stances, and full debates, enabling metrics for value coverage, alignment, diffusion, and cross-turn dynamics. Across multiple generation systems, AI-generated debates are broadly value-expressive, often activating as many or more value dimensions than matched human references, but they organize these values differently. Their utterances are more locally value-diffuse, and source-blind judges distinguish value clarity from value focus. These findings show that debate evaluation should consider not only value presence, but also value organization. Code and supplementary materials are available at this https URL.

</details>
